      res.status(400).json({ success: false, message });
    }
  });

  // ======================== LICENSEES & DEALERS ========================
  app.get('/api/licensees', requireAuth, (_req, res) => {
    const db = ensureDb();
    res.json({ success: true, licensees: db.licensees });
  });

  app.post('/api/licensees', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const licenseeName = String(req.body?.licenseeName || '').trim();
      const address = String(req.body?.address || '').trim();
      const licenseType = String(req.body?.licenseType || '').trim();
      const licenseNo = String(req.body?.licenseNo || '').trim();
      const firstIssueDate = String(req.body?.firstIssueDate || '').trim();
      const nid = String(req.body?.nid || '').trim();

      if (!licenseeName || !licenseNo) {
        throw new Error('লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম ও লাইসেন্স নং অবশ্যই দিতে হবে।');
      }

      const entry = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const item: LicenseeRecord = {
          id: `LIC-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
          serialNo: db.licensees.length + 1,
          licenseeName,
          address,
          licenseType: licenseType || 'ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)',
          licenseNo,
          firstIssueDate,
          nid,
        };
        db.licensees.push(item);
        saveDb(db);
        return item;
      });
      res.json({ success: true, licensee: entry });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'লাইসেন্সধারী যোগ করা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/licensees/import', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = Array.isArray(req.body?.licensees)
        ? (req.body.licensees as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
        : [];
      const result = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const existingKeys = new Set(
          db.licensees.map(
            (l) => `${l.licenseNo.trim()}::${l.licenseeName.trim()}`
          )
        );
        let added = 0;
        let skipped = 0;
        for (const row of incoming) {
          const licenseeName = String(row.licenseeName || '').trim();
          const licenseNo = String(row.licenseNo || '').trim();
          if (!licenseeName) {
            skipped++;
            continue;
          }
          const key = `${licenseNo}::${licenseeName}`;
          if (licenseNo && existingKeys.has(key)) {
            skipped++;
            continue;
          }
          if (licenseNo) existingKeys.add(key);
          db.licensees.push({
            id: `LIC-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 100000)}`,
            serialNo: db.licensees.length + 1,
            licenseeName,
            address: String(row.address || '').trim(),
            licenseType:
              String(row.licenseType || '').trim() ||
              'ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)',
            licenseNo,
            firstIssueDate: String(row.firstIssueDate || '').trim(),
            nid: String(row.nid || '').trim(),
          });
          added++;
        }
        saveDb(db);
        return { added, skipped };
      });
      res.json({ success: true, ...result });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'লাইসেন্স তালিকা ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ।',
      });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/licensees/:id', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        db.licensees = db.licensees
          .filter((l) => l.id !== id)
          .map((l, idx) => ({ ...l, serialNo: idx + 1 }));
        saveDb(db);
      });
      res.json({ success: true });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'লাইসেন্সধারী মুছে ফেলা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/dealers', requireAuth, (_req, res) => {
    const db = ensureDb();
    const unions = Array.from(
      new Set(
        db.dealers.map((d) => String(d.unionName || '').trim()).filter(Boolean)
      )
    ).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'bn'));
    res.json({ success: true, unions, dealers: db.dealers });
  });

  app.post('/api/dealers', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const unionName = String(req.body?.unionName || '').trim();
      const dealerName = String(req.body?.dealerName || '').trim();
      const salesCenter = String(req.body?.salesCenter || '').trim();
      const category = String(req.body?.category || '').trim();
      const customId = String(req.body?.id || '').trim();

      if (!unionName || !dealerName) {
        throw new Error('ইউনিয়নের নাম এবং ডিলারের নাম অবশ্যই দিতে হবে।');
      }

      const dealer = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const exists = db.dealers.some(
          (d) =>
            d.unionName.trim() === unionName &&
            d.dealerName.trim() === dealerName
        );
        if (exists) {
          throw new Error('এই ইউনিয়নে এই ডিলার ইতিমধ্যে যুক্ত আছে।');
        }
        const entry: DealerRecord = {
          id: customId || `DF-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
          unionName,
          dealerName,
          salesCenter,
          category,
        };
        db.dealers.push(entry);
        saveDb(db);
        return entry;
      });

      res.json({ success: true, dealer });
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'ডিলার যোগ করা যায়নি।';
      res.status(400).json({ success: false, message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/dealers/import', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = Array.isArray(req.body?.dealers)
        ? (req.body.dealers as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
        : [];
      const result = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const keySet = new Set(
          db.dealers.map(
            (d) => `${d.unionName.trim()}::${d.dealerName.trim()}`
          )
        );
        let added = 0;
        let skipped = 0;
        for (const item of incoming) {
          const unionName = String(item.unionName || '').trim();
          const dealerName = String(item.dealerName || '').trim();
          const salesCenter = String(item.salesCenter || '').trim();
          const category = String(item.category || '').trim();
          if (!dealerName) {
            skipped++;
            continue;
          }
          const key = `${unionName}::${dealerName}`;
          if (keySet.has(key)) {
            skipped++;
            continue;
          }
          keySet.add(key);
          db.dealers.push({
            id: String(item.id || '').trim() || crypto.randomUUID(),
            unionName,
            dealerName,
            salesCenter,
            category,
          });
          added++;
        }
        saveDb(db);
        return { added, skipped };
      });
      res.json({ success: true, ...result });
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'ডিলার ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।';
      res.status(400).json({ success: false, message });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/dealers/:id', requirePermission('dealers'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        db.dealers = db.dealers.filter((d) => d.id !== id);
        saveDb(db);
      });
      res.json({ success: true });
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'ডিলার মুছে ফেলা যায়নি।';
      res.status(400).json({ success: false, message });
    }
  });

  // ======================== WORD TEMPLATES & REPORTS ========================
  app.get('/api/templates', requirePermission('templates'), (_req, res) => {
    const db = ensureDb();
    res.json({ success: true, templates: db.templates });
  });

  app.post('/api/templates', requirePermission('settings'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const incoming = req.body as Partial<ReportTemplate>;
      const title = String(incoming.title || '').trim();
      const htmlContent = String(incoming.htmlContent || '').trim();
      if (!title) {
        throw new Error('টেম্পলেটের টাইটেল (Title) অবশ্যই দিতে হবে।');
      }
      if (!htmlContent) {
        throw new Error('টেম্পলেটের বিষয়বস্তু খালি রাখা যাবে না।');
      }

      const result = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const id =
          String(incoming.id || '').trim() ||
          `TPL-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`;
        const entry: ReportTemplate = {
          id,
          title,
          category: String(incoming.category || 'রিপোর্ট').trim() || 'রিপোর্ট',
          htmlContent,
          defaultMemoNo: String(incoming.defaultMemoNo || '').trim(),
          defaultDate: String(incoming.defaultDate || '').trim(),
          defaultRecipient: String(incoming.defaultRecipient || '').trim(),
          officerName: String(incoming.officerName || '').trim(),
          officerDesignation: String(incoming.officerDesignation || '').trim(),
          signatureImageDataUrl: String(
            incoming.signatureImageDataUrl || ''
          ).trim(),
          orientation:
            incoming.orientation === 'landscape' ? 'landscape' : 'portrait',
          editableFields: incoming.editableFields
            ? {
                allowDate: Boolean(incoming.editableFields.allowDate),
                allowMemo: Boolean(incoming.editableFields.allowMemo),
                allowRecipient: Boolean(incoming.editableFields.allowRecipient),
                allowTableEdit: Boolean(incoming.editableFields.allowTableEdit),
              }
            : {
                allowDate: true,
                allowMemo: true,
                allowRecipient: true,
                allowTableEdit: true,
              },
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        const idx = db.templates.findIndex((t) => t.id === id);
        if (idx >= 0) {
          db.templates[idx] = entry;
        } else {
          db.templates.unshift(entry);
        }
        saveDb(db);
        return { template: entry, templates: db.templates };
      });

      res.json({ success: true, ...result });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'টেম্পলেট সংরক্ষণ ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/templates/:id', requirePermission('settings'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      const templates = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        db.templates = db.templates.filter((t) => t.id !== id);
        saveDb(db);
        return db.templates;
      });
      res.json({ success: true, templates });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'টেম্পলেট মুছে ফেলা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  });

  // Saved Filled Template Reports History
  app.get('/api/reports', requirePermission('templates'), (_req, res) => {
    const db = ensureDb();
    res.json({ success: true, reports: db.savedReports });
  });

  app.post('/api/templates/:id/reports', requirePermission('templates'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const user = getAuthenticatedUser(req);
      const { id } = req.params;
      const dbSnap = ensureDb();
      const tpl = dbSnap.templates.find((t) => t.id === id);
      if (!tpl) {
        throw new Error('টেম্পলেট পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      }
      const renderedHtml = String(req.body?.renderedHtml || '').trim();
      if (!renderedHtml) {
        throw new Error('রিপোর্টের বিষয়বস্তু খালি।');
      }

      const saved = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        const entry: SavedReport = {
          id: `RPT-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
          templateId: tpl.id,
          templateTitle: tpl.title,
          category: tpl.category || 'রিপোর্ট',
          dateValue: String(req.body?.dateValue || '').trim(),
          memoNo: String(req.body?.memoNo || '').trim(),
          recipient: String(req.body?.recipient || '').trim(),
          renderedHtml,
          orientation: tpl.orientation === 'landscape' ? 'landscape' : 'portrait',
          createdBy: user?.name || user?.userId || 'User',
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        db.savedReports.unshift(entry);
        saveDb(db);
        return { report: entry, reports: db.savedReports };
      });
      res.json({ success: true, ...saved });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'রিপোর্ট সংরক্ষণ ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    }
  });

  app.delete('/api/reports/:id', requirePermission('templates'), async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const { id } = req.params;
      const reports = await withLock(() => {
        const db = ensureDb();
        db.savedReports = db.savedReports.filter((r) => r.id !== id);
        saveDb(db);
        return db.savedReports;
      });
      res.json({ success: true, reports });
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'রিপোর্ট মুছে ফেলা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  });

  // Full App Source Code Download Bundle (.txt) for GitHub & Capacitor APK
  app.get('/api/export/source-bundle', requirePermission('settings'), (_req, res) => {
    try {
      const rootDir = process.cwd();
      const collectFiles = (dir: string, relBase = ''): string[] => {
        if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
        const out: string[] = [];
        const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
        for (const entry of entries) {
          if (
            ['node_modules', 'dist', 'data', '.git', '.aistudio'].includes(
              entry.name
            )
          ) {
            continue;
          }
          const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
          const relPath = relBase ? `${relBase}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
          if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            out.push(...collectFiles(fullPath, relPath));
          } else if (
            /\.(ts|tsx|css|html|json|md|js|mjs|cjs)$/i.test(entry.name) &&
            !entry.name.includes('package-lock') &&
            !entry.name.includes('challan-store')
          ) {
            out.push(relPath);
          }
        }
        return out;
      };

      const rootFiles = [
        'package.json',
        'tsconfig.json',
        'vite.config.ts',
        'index.html',
        'metadata.json',
        'server.ts',
        'firebase-applet-config.json',
      ].filter((f) => fs.existsSync(path.join(rootDir, f)));

      const srcFiles = collectFiles(path.join(rootDir, 'src'), 'src').sort();
      const allFiles = Array.from(new Set([...rootFiles, ...srcFiles]));

      const parts: string[] = [
        '================================================================================',
        'CHALLAN QR SCANNER & REGISTER PORTAL — FULL SOURCE CODE BUNDLE',
        `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`,
        '================================================================================',
        '',
        '--- GITHUB & ANDROID APK QUICK STEPS ---',
        '1. GitHub Repository Setup:',
        '   Create each file below at its exact relative path, then run:',
        '   git init && git add . && git commit -m "Initial commit"',
        '   git branch -M main && git remote add origin <YOUR_GITHUB_REPO_URL>',
        '   git push -u origin main',
        '',
        '2. Run Web Server Locally:',
        '   npm install && npm run dev',
        '',
        '3. Build Android APK (via Capacitor):',
        '   npm run build',
        '   npm install @capacitor/core @capacitor/cli @capacitor/android',
        '   npx cap init "Challan Scan" "com.foodoffice.challanscan" --web-dir=dist',
        '   npx cap add android && npx cap sync android',
        '   (Or build APK on GitHub Actions using gradle assembleDebug in android/)',
        '================================================================================',
        '',
      ];

      for (const relPath of allFiles) {
        const absPath = path.join(rootDir, relPath);
        const content = fs.readFileSync(absPath, 'utf8');
        parts.push(`\n// ==================== FILE: ${relPath} ====================`);
        parts.push(content);
        parts.push(`// ==================== END: ${relPath} ====================\n`);
      }

      const bundle = parts.join('\n');
      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
      res.setHeader(
        'Content-Disposition',
        'attachment; filename="Challan_Portal_Full_Source_Code.txt"'
      );
      res.send(bundle);
    } catch (error) {
      res.status(500).json({
        success: false,
        message:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'সোর্স কোড বান্ডেল তৈরি করা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  });

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    const distPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'dist');
    app.use(express.static(distPath));
    app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(distPath, 'index.html'));
    });
  }

  const initialDb = ensureDb();
  const needsImmediateSync =
    Boolean(initialDb.settings.spreadsheetId) &&
    ((initialDb.dealers.length === 0 &&
      initialDb.suballocations.length === 0 &&
      initialDb.records.length === 0) ||
      initialDb.doIssues.length === 0);

  if (needsImmediateSync) {
    try {
      const counts = await pullAndMergePublicGoogleSheet();
      console.log('Initial Google Sheet auto-sync complete:', counts);
    } catch (err) {
      console.log(
        'Initial public Google Sheet sync skipped:',
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      );
    }
  }

  const PORT = 3000;
  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`Challan portal running on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
    if (initialDb.settings.spreadsheetId && !needsImmediateSync) {
      void pullAndMergePublicGoogleSheet()
        .then((counts) => {
          console.log('Background Google Sheet auto-sync complete:', counts);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.log(
            'Background public Google Sheet sync skipped:',
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
          );
        });
    }
  });
}

startServer();


// ==================== END: server.ts ====================

// ==================== FILE: src/App.tsx ====================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type TouchEvent } from 'react';
import type { User } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  FileText,
  Home,
  LayoutTemplate,
  ListChecks,
  Loader2,
  Lock,
  QrCode,
  RefreshCw,
  Settings,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  Unlock,
  Users,
  Wheat,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  AppSettings,
  ChallanRecord,
  DealerRecord,
  LicenseeRecord,
  PermissionKey,
  ReportTemplate,
  SuballocationRecord,
  UserAccount,
} from './types.ts';
import { apiFetch, clearAuthToken, setAuthToken } from './utils/api.ts';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, toBengaliDigits } from './utils/challan.ts';
import { initGoogleAuth } from './services/googleAuth.ts';
import {
  pullFromAppsScriptBridge,
  pullFromGoogleSheetsOAuth,
  pullFromPublicSheetBackend,
} from './services/googleSheets.ts';
import { LoginView } from './components/LoginView.tsx';
import { AdminPanelView } from './components/AdminPanelView.tsx';
import { ScanEntryView } from './components/ScanEntryView.tsx';
import { RecordsView } from './components/RecordsView.tsx';
import { ReportView } from './components/ReportView.tsx';
import { TemplatesView } from './components/TemplatesView.tsx';
import { DealersView } from './components/DealersView.tsx';
import {
  SettingsView,
  type SettingsSectionKey,
} from './components/SettingsView.tsx';

type ActiveTab =
  | 'scan'
  | 'records'
  | 'report'
  | 'templates'
  | 'dealers'
  | 'settings'
  | 'adminUsers';

export default function App() {
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<UserAccount | null>(null);
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  const [lockEnabled, setLockEnabled] = useState(false);

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActiveTab>('scan');
  const [settingsSection, setSettingsSection] =
    useState<SettingsSectionKey>('sync');
  const [isFocusedScanMode, setIsFocusedScanMode] = useState(false);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AppSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<ChallanRecord[]>([]);
  const [recordsLoading, setRecordsLoading] = useState(false);

  const [dealers, setDealers] = useState<DealerRecord[]>([]);
  const [unions, setUnions] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [licensees, setLicensees] = useState<LicenseeRecord[]>([]);
  const [suballocations, setSuballocations] = useState<SuballocationRecord[]>(
    []
  );
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<ReportTemplate[]>([]);
  const [scanTriggerNonce, setScanTriggerNonce] = useState(0);

  const [googleUser, setGoogleUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [hasGoogleToken, setHasGoogleToken] = useState(false);
  const [quickSyncing, setQuickSyncing] = useState(false);
  const [bannerStatus, setBannerStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const navTouchStartXRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const didAutoSyncRef = useRef(false);

  const hasPerm = useCallback(
    (key: PermissionKey): boolean => {
      if (!currentUser) return false;
      if (currentUser.role === 'admin') return true;
      return Boolean(currentUser.permissions?.[key]);
    },
    [currentUser]
  );

  // Check existing session on mount (always clear loading even on 401/error)
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    async function checkSession() {
      try {
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/auth/me');
        const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
          success?: boolean;
          user?: UserAccount | null;
          lockEnabled?: boolean;
        };
        if (active && typeof data.lockEnabled === 'boolean') {
          setLockEnabled(data.lockEnabled);
        }
        if (res.ok && active && data.user) {
          setCurrentUser(data.user);
        } else if (res.status === 401) {
          clearAuthToken();
          if (active) {
            setCurrentUser(null);
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Not logged in or server unavailable -> render LoginView
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setAuthLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }
    void checkSession();

    const handleAuthExpired = () => {
      clearAuthToken();
      void checkSession();
    };
    window.addEventListener('challan-auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);
    return () => {
      active = false;
      window.removeEventListener('challan-auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);
    };
  }, []);

  const fetchSettings = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/settings');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        settings?: AppSettings;
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success && json.settings) {
        setSettings(json.settings);
        setLockEnabled(Boolean(json.settings.lockEnabled));
      }
    } catch {
      // keep default settings
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchRecords = useCallback(async () => {
    setRecordsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/challans');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        records?: ChallanRecord[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setRecords(json.records || []);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore initial fetch error
    } finally {
      setRecordsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchDealers = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/dealers');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        unions?: string[];
        dealers?: DealerRecord[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setUnions(json.unions || []);
        setDealers(json.dealers || []);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore initial fetch error
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchLicensees = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/licensees');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        licensees?: LicenseeRecord[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setLicensees(json.licensees || []);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore initial fetch error
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchSuballocations = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/suballocations');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        suballocations?: SuballocationRecord[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setSuballocations(json.suballocations || []);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore initial fetch error
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchTemplates = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/templates');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        templates?: ReportTemplate[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setTemplates(json.templates || []);
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore initial fetch error
    }
  }, []);

  const refreshAllData = useCallback(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    if (hasPerm('records') || hasPerm('scan')) {
      void fetchRecords();
    }
    void fetchDealers();
    void fetchLicensees();
    void fetchSuballocations();
    if (hasPerm('templates') || hasPerm('settings')) {
      void fetchTemplates();
    }
  }, [
    currentUser,
    hasPerm,
    fetchRecords,
    fetchDealers,
    fetchLicensees,
    fetchSuballocations,
    fetchTemplates,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser) {
      didAutoSyncRef.current = false;
      return;
    }
    void fetchSettings();
    refreshAllData();
  }, [currentUser, fetchSettings, refreshAllData]);

  const performSheetSync = useCallback(async (): Promise<string> => {
    if (hasGoogleToken) {
      try {
        const payload = await pullFromGoogleSheetsOAuth(settings);
        const res = await apiFetch('/api/sync/snapshot', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        });
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = (await res.json()) as {
            counts?: { dealers: number; suballocations: number; records: number };
          };
          refreshAllData();
          return `সিঙ্কড: ডিলার ${json.counts?.dealers ?? 0}, উপ-বরাদ্দ ${json.counts?.suballocations ?? 0}, চালান ${json.counts?.records ?? 0}`;
        }
      } catch {
        // fall through to backend sync
      }
    }

    if (settings.gasWebAppUrl.trim() && settings.syncMethod === 'apps_script') {
      const payload = await pullFromAppsScriptBridge(
        settings.gasWebAppUrl.trim(),
        settings
      );
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/sync/snapshot', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = (await res.json()) as {
          counts?: { dealers: number; suballocations: number; records: number };
        };
        refreshAllData();
        return `Apps Script সিঙ্কড: চালান ${json.counts?.records ?? 0}`;
      }
    }

    const pub = await pullFromPublicSheetBackend(settings);
    refreshAllData();
    return `সিঙ্কড: ডিলার ${pub.counts?.dealers ?? 0}, উপ-বরাদ্দ ${pub.counts?.suballocations ?? 0}, চালান ${pub.counts?.records ?? 0}`;
  }, [hasGoogleToken, settings, refreshAllData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser || !settings.spreadsheetId.trim()) return;
    if (didAutoSyncRef.current) return;
    didAutoSyncRef.current = true;
    void performSheetSync().catch(() => {
      // background auto-sync should fail silently without interrupting navigation
    });
  }, [currentUser, settings.spreadsheetId, performSheetSync]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = initGoogleAuth(
      (user) => {
        setGoogleUser(user);
        setHasGoogleToken(true);
      },
      (user) => {
        setGoogleUser(user);
        setHasGoogleToken(false);
      }
    );
    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const navItems = useMemo(() => {
    const all: Array<{
      id: ActiveTab;
      perm: PermissionKey;
      label: string;
      shortLabel: string;
      icon: typeof Home;
    }> = [
      {
        id: 'scan',
        perm: 'scan',
        label: 'হোম ও স্ক্যান',
        shortLabel: 'হোম',
        icon: Home,
      },
      {
        id: 'records',
        perm: 'records',
        label: 'চালান তালিকা',
        shortLabel: 'তালিকা',
        icon: ListChecks,
      },
      {
        id: 'report',
        perm: 'report',
        label: 'রেজিস্টার',
        shortLabel: 'রেজিস্টার',
        icon: FileText,
      },
      {
        id: 'templates',
        perm: 'templates',
        label: 'টেম্পলেট',
        shortLabel: 'টেম্পলেট',
        icon: LayoutTemplate,
      },
      {
        id: 'dealers',
        perm: 'dealers',
        label: 'ডিলার ও লাইসেন্স',
        shortLabel: 'ডিলার',
        icon: Users,
      },
      {
        id: 'settings',
        perm: 'settings',
        label: 'সেটিংস',
        shortLabel: 'সেটিংস',
        icon: Settings,
      },
      {
        id: 'adminUsers',
        perm: 'adminUsers',
        label: 'এডমিন',
        shortLabel: 'এডমিন',
        icon: ShieldCheck,
      },
    ];
    return all.filter((item) => hasPerm(item.perm));
  }, [hasPerm]);

  // Keep activeTab always on a permitted tab
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser || navItems.length === 0) return;
    if (!navItems.some((item) => item.id === activeTab)) {
      setActiveTab(navItems[0].id);
    }
  }, [currentUser, navItems, activeTab]);

  function switchTab(next: ActiveTab, nextSettingsSec?: SettingsSectionKey) {
    const allowed = navItems.some((item) => item.id === next);
    if (!allowed) return;
    if (next !== 'scan') {
      setIsFocusedScanMode(false);
    }
    if (next === 'settings' && nextSettingsSec) {
      setSettingsSection(nextSettingsSec);
    }
    setActiveTab(next);
  }

  async function handleLogout() {
    try {
      await apiFetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' });
    } catch {
      // clear local session regardless
    }
    clearAuthToken();
    setCurrentUser(null);
    setBannerStatus(null);
  }

  async function handleSaveSettings(next: AppSettings) {
    const res = await apiFetch('/api/settings', {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(next),
    });
    const json = (await res.json()) as {
      success: boolean;
      settings?: AppSettings;
      token?: string;
      message?: string;
    };
    if (!res.ok || !json.success || !json.settings) {
      throw new Error(json.message || 'সেটিংস সংরক্ষণ করা যায়নি।');
    }
    if (json.token) {
      setAuthToken(json.token);
    }
    setSettings(json.settings);
    setLockEnabled(Boolean(json.settings.lockEnabled));
  }

  async function handleQuickSyncFromHeader() {
    setQuickSyncing(true);
    setBannerStatus(null);
    try {
      const msg = await performSheetSync();
      setBannerStatus(msg);
    } catch {
      if (hasPerm('settings')) {
        switchTab('settings', 'sync');
      }
    } finally {
      setQuickSyncing(false);
    }
  }

  function handleQuickScan() {
    if (!hasPerm('scan')) return;
    switchTab('scan');
    setScanTriggerNonce((n) => n + 1);
  }

  function handleNavTouchStart(e: TouchEvent<HTMLElement>) {
    navTouchStartXRef.current = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? null;
  }

  function handleNavTouchEnd(e: TouchEvent<HTMLElement>) {
    if (navTouchStartXRef.current === null) return;
    const endX = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? navTouchStartXRef.current;
    const dx = endX - navTouchStartXRef.current;
    navTouchStartXRef.current = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 55) return;
    const order = navItems.map((n) => n.id);
    const idx = order.indexOf(activeTab);
    if (dx < 0 && idx < order.length - 1) {
      switchTab(order[idx + 1]);
    } else if (dx > 0 && idx > 0) {
      switchTab(order[idx - 1]);
    }
  }

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700">
        <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-rose-700" />
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">পোর্টাল লোড হচ্ছে...</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!currentUser) {
    return (
      <LoginView
        onLoginSuccess={(user) => setCurrentUser(user)}
        lockEnabled={lockEnabled}
      />
    );
  }

  const isEmailAuthorized =
    !hasGoogleToken ||
    !googleUser?.email ||
    settings.allowedEmails.length === 0 ||
    settings.allowedEmails.some(
      (em) => em.trim().toLowerCase() === googleUser.email!.toLowerCase()
    );

  const syncSummaryText = hasGoogleToken
    ? 'Google Sheet সংযুক্ত'
    : settings.gasWebAppUrl.trim()
      ? 'Apps Script সক্রিয়'
      : bannerStatus || 'Sheet সংযোগ';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
      {/* Vibrant Rose-Magenta Office Banner Header (minimized in focused scan mode) */}
      <header className="no-print bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-700 via-rose-600 to-pink-600 text-white">
        {!(activeTab === 'scan' && isFocusedScanMode) && (
          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 sm:px-6">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navItems[0] && switchTab(navItems[0].id)}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 text-left min-w-0"
            >
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-emerald-800/95 ring-2 ring-white/40">
                <Wheat className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-300" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="truncate text-lg font-extrabold tracking-tight sm:text-xl">
                  {settings.officeTitle}
                </div>
                <div className="truncate text-xs font-medium text-rose-100">
                  {bannerStatus || settings.officeSubtitle}
                </div>
              </div>
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                <span className="truncate max-w-[130px]">{currentUser.name}</span>
                <span className="rounded-full bg-white/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px]">
                  {currentUser.role === 'admin' ? 'এডমিন' : 'অপারেটর'}
                </span>
              </div>

              {hasPerm('settings') && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleQuickSyncFromHeader()}
                  disabled={quickSyncing}
                  className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors"
                  aria-label={syncSummaryText}
                  title={syncSummaryText}
                >
                  {quickSyncing ? (
                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                  ) : (
                    <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />
                  )}
                </button>
              )}

              {hasPerm('scan') && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickScan}
                  className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
                >
                  <QrCode className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>স্ক্যান</span>
                </button>
              )}

              {lockEnabled ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleLogout()}
                  title="পাসওয়ার্ড লক করুন"
                  aria-label="পাসওয়ার্ড লক করুন"
                  className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-900/50 text-white hover:bg-rose-900 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Lock className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              ) : (
                hasPerm('settings') && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => switchTab('settings', 'sync')}
                    title="লক বন্ধ (আনলক অবস্থায় আছে) — সেটিংসে গিয়ে চালু করুন"
                    aria-label="লক সিস্টেম সেটিংস"
                    className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15 text-white hover:bg-white/25 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <Unlock className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                )
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Desktop Sub-Navigation Bar */}
        <nav
          aria-label="প্রধান নেভিগেশন"
          className="hidden md:block border-t border-white/15 bg-rose-800/35"
        >
          <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl items-center gap-5 overflow-x-auto px-6">
            {navItems.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              const isActive = activeTab === item.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => switchTab(item.id)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'border-white text-white'
                      : 'border-transparent text-rose-100 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                  {item.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </nav>
      </header>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 pt-4 pb-24 sm:px-6 md:pb-8">
        {!isEmailAuthorized && (
          <div className="no-print mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-900">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-700" />
              {googleUser?.email} অনুমতি তালিকায় নেই।
            </span>
            {hasPerm('settings') && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => switchTab('settings', 'sync')}
                className="rounded-xl bg-amber-900 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] text-white"
              >
                পারমিশন যোগ করুন
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {!(activeTab === 'scan' && isFocusedScanMode) && (
          <div className="no-print mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-2.5">
            <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 sm:text-lg">
              {activeTab === 'scan' && 'খাত নির্বাচন ও চালান স্ক্যান'}
              {activeTab === 'records' && 'সংরক্ষিত চালান তালিকা'}
              {activeTab === 'report' && 'চালান রেজিস্টার ও প্রিন্ট'}
              {activeTab === 'templates' && 'প্রতিবেদন ও টেম্পলেট'}
              {activeTab === 'dealers' && 'ডিলার, উপ-বরাদ্দ ও লাইসেন্স তালিকা'}
              {activeTab === 'settings' && 'সেটিংস, টেমপ্লেট ও Google Sheet'}
              {activeTab === 'adminUsers' && 'এডমিন ও ইউজার এক্সেস কন্ট্রোল'}
            </h1>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
              <span>চালান: {toBengaliDigits(records.length)}টি</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>ডিলার: {toBengaliDigits(dealers.length)}</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>লাইসেন্স: {toBengaliDigits(licensees.length)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {navItems.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-8 text-center text-sm font-bold text-amber-900">
            আপনার অ্যাকাউন্টে এখনো কোনো বাটনের অনুমতি দেওয়া হয়নি। এডমিনের সাথে যোগাযোগ করুন।
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            {activeTab === 'scan' && hasPerm('scan') && (
              <ScanEntryView
                settings={settings}
                dealers={dealers}
                unions={unions}
                licensees={licensees}
                suballocations={suballocations}
                records={records}
                canOpenSettings={hasPerm('settings')}
                onSaved={refreshAllData}
                onOpenSettings={() => switchTab('settings', 'khats')}
                onOpenSheetSettings={() => switchTab('settings', 'sync')}
                onOpenAllRecords={() => switchTab('records')}
                onFocusModeChange={setIsFocusedScanMode}
                externalScanRequestNonce={scanTriggerNonce}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'records' && hasPerm('records') && (
              <RecordsView
                records={records}
                loading={recordsLoading}
                settings={settings}
                khats={settings.khats}
                customColumns={settings.customColumns}
                hideDealerIds={settings.hideDealerIds}
                canEdit={hasPerm('recordEdit')}
                canDelete={hasPerm('recordDelete')}
                canImport={hasPerm('scan') || hasPerm('recordEdit')}
                onRefresh={refreshAllData}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'report' && hasPerm('report') && (
              <ReportView
                unions={unions}
                khats={settings.khats}
                settings={settings}
                recordsCount={records.length}
                onSyncSheet={handleQuickSyncFromHeader}
                syncing={quickSyncing}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'templates' && hasPerm('templates') && (
              <TemplatesView
                templates={templates}
                settings={settings}
                canEditMaster={hasPerm('settings')}
                onRefreshTemplates={() => void fetchTemplates()}
                onOpenMasterEditor={() => switchTab('settings', 'templates')}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'dealers' && hasPerm('dealers') && (
              <DealersView
                dealers={dealers}
                unions={unions}
                licensees={licensees}
                suballocations={suballocations}
                khats={settings.khats}
                hideDealerIds={settings.hideDealerIds}
                onRefresh={refreshAllData}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'settings' && hasPerm('settings') && (
              <SettingsView
                settings={settings}
                templates={templates}
                initialSection={settingsSection}
                googleUser={googleUser}
                hasGoogleToken={hasGoogleToken}
                onSaveSettings={handleSaveSettings}
                onSyncedFromSheet={refreshAllData}
                onRefreshTemplates={() => void fetchTemplates()}
                onOpenDealersManager={() => switchTab('dealers')}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'adminUsers' && hasPerm('adminUsers') && (
              <AdminPanelView />
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Mobile Fixed Bottom Tab Bar */}
      {navItems.length > 0 && (
        <nav
          aria-label="মোবাইল নিচের নেভিগেশন"
          onTouchStart={handleNavTouchStart}
          onTouchEnd={handleNavTouchEnd}
          className="no-print fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-30 flex h-16 items-center justify-around border-t border-slate-200 bg-white px-0.5 md:hidden"
        >
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => switchTab(item.id)}
                className={`flex min-h-[44px] flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-xl px-0.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'text-rose-700'
                    : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon
                  className={`h-[18px] w-[18px] ${
                    isActive ? 'text-rose-700' : ''
                  }`}
                />
                <span>{item.shortLabel}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/App.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/A4FitPreview.tsx ====================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Maximize2, ZoomIn, ZoomOut } from 'lucide-react';
import { toBengaliDigits } from '../utils/challan.ts';

export type PaperOrientation = 'portrait' | 'landscape';

export function getA4PixelWidth(orientation: PaperOrientation): number {
  return orientation === 'portrait' ? 794 : 1123;
}

export function getA4PixelHeight(orientation: PaperOrientation): number {
  return orientation === 'portrait' ? 1122 : 793;
}

/**
 * Resets preview CSS transform inside html2canvas clone so downloaded PDFs
 * always render crisp 1:1 A4 pages regardless of mobile screen width.
 */
export function prepareA4PdfClone(
  clonedDoc: Document,
  orientation: PaperOrientation
) {
  const mmWidth = orientation === 'portrait' ? '210mm' : '297mm';

  clonedDoc
    .querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.a4-pdf-staging')
    .forEach((el) => {
      el.style.position = 'static';
      el.style.left = 'auto';
      el.style.top = 'auto';
      el.style.zIndex = 'auto';
      el.style.overflow = 'visible';
      el.style.width = mmWidth;
    });

  clonedDoc
    .querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.a4-preview-frame')
    .forEach((el) => {
      el.style.width = mmWidth;
      el.style.height = 'auto';
      el.style.overflow = 'visible';
      el.style.margin = '0';
    });

  clonedDoc
    .querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.a4-preview-scaler')
    .forEach((el) => {
      el.style.transform = 'none';
      el.style.width = mmWidth;
    });

  clonedDoc
    .querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.a4-sheet-page')
    .forEach((el) => {
      el.style.width = mmWidth;
      el.style.margin = '0';
      el.style.border = 'none';
      el.style.boxShadow = 'none';
    });
}

interface A4FitPreviewProps {
  orientation: PaperOrientation;
  children: ReactNode;
  leftSlot?: ReactNode;
  hidePaper?: boolean;
  hideToolbar?: boolean;
}

export function A4FitPreview({
  orientation,
  children,
  leftSlot,
  hidePaper = false,
  hideToolbar = false,
}: A4FitPreviewProps) {
  const viewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  const [availWidth, setAvailWidth] = useState(0);
  const [contentHeight, setContentHeight] = useState(0);
  const [manualScale, setManualScale] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const paperWidth = getA4PixelWidth(orientation);

  useEffect(() => {
    const vp = viewportRef.current;
    if (!vp) return;
    const updateWidth = () => {
      const w = vp.clientWidth;
      if (w > 0) setAvailWidth(w);
    };
    updateWidth();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(updateWidth);
    ro.observe(vp);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const inner = contentRef.current;
    if (!inner || hidePaper) return;
    const updateHeight = () => {
      const h = inner.scrollHeight;
      if (h > 0) setContentHeight(h);
    };
    updateHeight();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(updateHeight);
    ro.observe(inner);
    const mo = new MutationObserver(updateHeight);
    mo.observe(inner, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    return () => {
      ro.disconnect();
      mo.disconnect();
    };
  }, [orientation, hidePaper, children]);

  // Reset to fit mode when orientation changes
  useEffect(() => {
    setManualScale(null);
  }, [orientation]);

  const fitScale =
    availWidth > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0.2, (availWidth - 2) / paperWidth)) : 1;
  const activeScale = manualScale ?? fitScale;
  const isScaledDown = activeScale < 0.999;

  const scaledWidth = Math.floor(paperWidth * activeScale);
  const scaledHeight =
    contentHeight > 0 ? Math.ceil(contentHeight * activeScale) : undefined;

  const pct = Math.round(activeScale * 100);

  function stepZoom(delta: number) {
    const next = Math.min(1.25, Math.max(0.2, Number((activeScale + delta).toFixed(2))));
    setManualScale(next);
  }

  return (
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/90 p-2 sm:p-4">
      {!hideToolbar && (leftSlot || !hidePaper) && (
        <div className="no-print mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{leftSlot}</div>

          {!hidePaper && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-0.5 shadow-2xs">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => stepZoom(-0.1)}
                title="জুম কমান"
                aria-label="জুম কমান"
                className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <ZoomOut className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setManualScale(null)}
                title="স্ক্রিন অনুযায়ী ফিট করুন"
                className={`inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                  manualScale === null
                    ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                    : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'
                }`}
              >
                <Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>ফিট ({toBengaliDigits(pct)}%)</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => stepZoom(0.1)}
                title="জুম বাড়ান"
                aria-label="জুম বাড়ান"
                className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <ZoomIn className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Measured Viewport */}
      <div
        ref={viewportRef}
        className={`w-full ${
          manualScale !== null && scaledWidth > availWidth
            ? 'overflow-x-auto'
            : 'overflow-x-hidden'
        }`}
      >
        {!hidePaper && (
          <div
            className="a4-preview-frame mx-auto"
            style={{
              width: `${scaledWidth}px`,
              height: scaledHeight ? `${scaledHeight}px` : 'auto',
              overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
          >
            <div
              ref={contentRef}
              className="a4-preview-scaler"
              style={{
                width: `${paperWidth}px`,
                transformOrigin: 'top left',
                transform: isScaledDown || activeScale > 1.001 ? `scale(${activeScale})` : 'none',
              }}
            >
              {children}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/components/A4FitPreview.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/AdminPanelView.tsx ====================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  UserPlus,
  Share2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Trash2,
  Shield,
  Mail,
  KeyRound,
  UserCheck,
  UserX,
  Pencil,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { PermissionKey, PermissionsMap, UserAccount } from '../types';
import { PERMISSION_LABELS } from '../utils/bengali';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api';

const DEFAULT_PERMS: PermissionsMap = {
  scan: true,
  records: true,
  recordEdit: false,
  recordDelete: false,
  report: true,
  templates: true,
  dealers: false,
  settings: false,
  adminUsers: false,
};

const ALL_PERM_KEYS = Object.keys(PERMISSION_LABELS) as PermissionKey[];

export const AdminPanelView: React.FC = () => {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<UserAccount[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('');

  // Form state for create / edit user
  const [editingUserId, setEditingUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [userIdInput, setUserIdInput] = useState('');
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState('');
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState('');
  const [passwordInput, setPasswordInput] = useState('');
  const [roleInput, setRoleInput] = useState<'operator' | 'admin'>('operator');
  const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState<PermissionsMap>(DEFAULT_PERMS);

  // Share Modal state
  const [shareUser, setShareUser] = useState<UserAccount | null>(null);
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState(false);

  const fetchUsers = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/users');
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setUsers(data.users || []);
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchUsers();
  }, []);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setEditingUserId(null);
    setUserIdInput('');
    setNameInput('');
    setEmailInput('');
    setPasswordInput('');
    setRoleInput('operator');
    setPermissions({ ...DEFAULT_PERMS });
  };

  const startEdit = (u: UserAccount) => {
    setEditingUserId(u.id);
    setUserIdInput(u.userId);
    setNameInput(u.name);
    setEmailInput(u.email);
    setPasswordInput('');
    setRoleInput(u.role);
    setPermissions({ ...u.permissions });
    setError('');
    setSuccess('');
  };

  const togglePerm = (key: PermissionKey) => {
    setPermissions((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccess('');

    if (editingUserId) {
      const res = await apiFetch(`/api/users/${editingUserId}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: nameInput,
          email: emailInput,
          role: roleInput,
          permissions,
          ...(passwordInput.trim() ? { password: passwordInput.trim() } : {}),
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || 'আপডেট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
        return;
      }
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((u) => (u.id === editingUserId ? data.user : u)));
      setSuccess(`"${data.user.name}" এর পাওয়ার ও তথ্য আপডেট করা হয়েছে।`);
      resetForm();
    } else {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/users', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          userId: userIdInput,
          name: nameInput,
          email: emailInput,
          password: passwordInput,
          role: roleInput,
          permissions,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || 'ইউজার তৈরি ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
        return;
      }
      setUsers((prev) => [...prev, data.user]);
      setSuccess(`নতুন ইউজার "${data.user.userId}" তৈরি হয়েছে। এখন শেয়ার বাটনে ক্লিক করে অ্যাপ শেয়ার করুন।`);
      resetForm();
    }
  };

  const toggleActive = async (u: UserAccount) => {
    const res = await apiFetch(`/api/users/${u.id}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ active: !u.active }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === u.id ? data.user : item)));
    }
  };

  const deleteUser = async (id: string) => {
    const res = await apiFetch(`/api/users/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (res.ok) {
      setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== id));
    }
  };

  const getShareUrl = (u: UserAccount) => {
    const origin = window.location.origin;
    return `${origin}/?inviteEmail=${encodeURIComponent(u.email)}`;
  };

  const copyShareMessage = (u: UserAccount) => {
    const grantedList = ALL_PERM_KEYS.filter((k) => u.role === 'admin' || u.permissions[k])
      .map((k) => `• ${PERMISSION_LABELS[k].label}`)
      .join('\n');
    const msg = `চালান স্ক্যান ও ব্যবস্থাপনা অ্যাপ এক্সেস:\nলিঙ্ক: ${getShareUrl(u)}\nইমেইল: ${u.email}\nইউজার আইডি: ${u.userId}\n\nঅনুমোদিত বাটন ও কাজ:\n${grantedList}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(msg);
    setCopiedId(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
      {/* Left: Create / Edit User & Button Power Assignment */}
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-4"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-700" />
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
              {editingUserId ? 'ইউজার ও পাওয়ার সংশোধন' : 'নতুন ইউজার ও ইমেইল এক্সেস'}
            </h2>
          </div>
          {editingUserId && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={resetForm}
              className="text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
            >
              নতুন ফর্ম
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-xs text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        {success && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-800">
            {success}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">ইউজার আইডি</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              disabled={Boolean(editingUserId)}
              placeholder="operator2"
              value={userIdInput}
              onChange={(e) => setUserIdInput(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl disabled:bg-slate-100"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">নাম / পদবি</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="মোঃ করিম (অপারেটর)"
              value={nameInput}
              onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-700" />
              <span>এক্সেস ইমেইল অ্যাড্রেস</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              required
              placeholder="user@gmail.com"
              value={emailInput}
              onChange={(e) => setEmailInput(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-700" />
              <span>{editingUserId ? 'নতুন পাসওয়ার্ড (ঐচ্ছিক)' : 'পাসওয়ার্ড'}</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required={!editingUserId}
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={passwordInput}
              onChange={(e) => setPasswordInput(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">ইউজার ধরন</label>
          <select
            value={roleInput}
            onChange={(e) => setRoleInput(e.target.value as 'operator' | 'admin')}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl bg-white"
          >
            <option value="operator">সীমিত ইউজার (শুধু অনুমোদিত বাটন দেখবে)</option>
            <option value="admin">সুপার এডমিন (সকল এক্সেস)</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {roleInput === 'operator' && (
          <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 mb-2">
              কোন কোন বাটনে কাজ করার পাওয়ার পাবে:
            </label>
            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {ALL_PERM_KEYS.map((key) => {
                const info = PERMISSION_LABELS[key];
                return (
                  <label
                    key={key}
                    className={`flex items-start gap-2.5 p-2.5 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                      permissions[key]
                        ? 'bg-emerald-50/70 border-emerald-300'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={Boolean(permissions[key])}
                      onChange={() => togglePerm(key)}
                      className="mt-0.5 rounded text-emerald-700 focus:ring-emerald-600"
                    />
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">{info.label}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{info.desc}</div>
                    </div>
                  </label>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          {editingUserId ? 'ইউজার আপডেট করুন' : 'ইউজার তৈরি ও এক্সেস দিন'}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* Right: Existing Users & App Share List */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">অনুমোদিত ইউজার ও শেয়ার তালিকা</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              শেয়ার বাটনে ক্লিক করে নির্দিষ্ট ইমেইল অ্যাড্রেস ও লিংক ইউজারকে পাঠান
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-10 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">লোড হচ্ছে...</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {users.map((u) => {
              const activePerms = ALL_PERM_KEYS.filter(
                (k) => u.role === 'admin' || u.permissions[k]
              );
              return (
                <div
                  key={u.id}
                  className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4"
                >
                  <div className="space-y-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{u.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">({u.userId})</span>
                      {!u.active && (
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-red-600">· নিষ্ক্রিয়</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>{u.email}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-800 pt-0.5">
                      পাওয়ার:{' '}
                      {u.role === 'admin'
                        ? 'সকল বাটন (সুপার এডমিন)'
                        : activePerms.map((k) => PERMISSION_LABELS[k].label).join(' · ') ||
                          'কোনো পাওয়ার নেই'}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShareUser(u)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                      title="অ্যাপ শেয়ার করুন"
                    >
                      <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>শেয়ার</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => startEdit(u)}
                      className="p-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
                      title="এডিট"
                    >
                      <Pencil className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleActive(u)}
                      className="p-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
                      title={u.active ? 'নিষ্ক্রিয় করুন' : 'সক্রিয় করুন'}
                    >
                      {u.active ? (
                        <UserCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-700" />
                      ) : (
                        <UserX className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                    {u.userId !== 'admin' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => deleteUser(u.id)}
                        className="p-2 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600 cursor-pointer"
                        title="মুছুন"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Share App & Email Access Modal */}
      {shareUser && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 space-y-4 shadow-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-800">
              <Shield className="w-5 h-5" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                অ্যাপ শেয়ার ও ইমেইল এক্সেস কার্ড
              </h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
              এই লিংকটি <strong>{shareUser.name}</strong> কে পাঠালে তিনি তার অনুমোদিত ইমেইল (
              <span className="font-mono">{shareUser.email}</span>) ও পাসওয়ার্ড দিয়ে লগইন করে শুধু
              অনুমোদিত বাটনগুলোতে কাজ করতে পারবেন।
            </p>

            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-mono break-all text-slate-700">
              {getShareUrl(shareUser)}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShareUser(null)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
              >
                বন্ধ করুন
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => copyShareMessage(shareUser)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
              >
                {copiedId ? <Check className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span>{copiedId ? 'কপি হয়েছে!' : 'লিংক ও বিবরণ কপি করুন'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


// ==================== END: src/components/AdminPanelView.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/CategoryHub.tsx ====================
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  FileBadge2,
  Flame,
  Receipt,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShoppingBag,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Wheat,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  KhatColorKey,
  KhatConfig,
  KhatGroup,
  KhatIconKey,
  LicenseSubGroup,
} from '../types.ts';

interface CategoryHubProps {
  khats: KhatConfig[];
  unlinkedSuballocationCount: number;
  dealersCount: number;
  canOpenSettings?: boolean;
  onSelectKhat: (
    khat: KhatConfig,
    licenseActionType?: 'নতুন' | 'নবায়ন' | 'জরিমানা' | 'অন্যান্য'
  ) => void;
  onOpenSettings: () => void;
}

const COLOR_MAP: Record<
  KhatColorKey,
  { bg: string; ring: string; text: string }
> = {
  rose: {
    bg: 'bg-rose-50',
    ring: 'border-rose-200',
    text: 'text-rose-600',
  },
  sky: {
    bg: 'bg-sky-50',
    ring: 'border-sky-200',
    text: 'text-sky-700',
  },
  indigo: {
    bg: 'bg-indigo-50',
    ring: 'border-indigo-200',
    text: 'text-indigo-700',
  },
  purple: {
    bg: 'bg-purple-50',
    ring: 'border-purple-200',
    text: 'text-purple-700',
  },
  amber: {
    bg: 'bg-amber-50',
    ring: 'border-amber-200',
    text: 'text-amber-700',
  },
  orange: {
    bg: 'bg-orange-50',
    ring: 'border-orange-200',
    text: 'text-orange-700',
  },
  teal: {
    bg: 'bg-teal-50',
    ring: 'border-teal-200',
    text: 'text-teal-700',
  },
  emerald: {
    bg: 'bg-emerald-50',
    ring: 'border-emerald-200',
    text: 'text-emerald-700',
  },
  red: {
    bg: 'bg-red-50',
    ring: 'border-red-200',
    text: 'text-red-700',
  },
};

export function renderKhatIcon(iconKey: KhatIconKey, className: string) {
  switch (iconKey) {
    case 'wheat':
      return <Wheat className={className} />;
    case 'bag':
      return <ShoppingBag className={className} />;
    case 'flame':
      return <Flame className={className} />;
    case 'license':
      return <FileBadge2 className={className} />;
    case 'refresh':
      return <RefreshCw className={className} />;
    case 'alert':
      return <AlertCircle className={className} />;
    case 'shield':
      return <ShieldCheck className={className} />;
    case 'receipt':
    default:
      return <Receipt className={className} />;
  }
}

const LICENSE_SUBGROUPS: Array<{
  id: LicenseSubGroup;
  label: string;
  actionType: 'নতুন' | 'নবায়ন' | 'জরিমানা' | 'অন্যান্য';
}> = [
  { id: 'new', label: 'নতুন লাইসেন্স', actionType: 'নতুন' },
  { id: 'renewal', label: 'নবায়ন', actionType: 'নবায়ন' },
  { id: 'fine', label: 'জরিমানা', actionType: 'জরিমানা' },
  { id: 'other', label: 'অন্যান্য', actionType: 'অন্যান্য' },
];

export function CategoryHub({
  khats,
  unlinkedSuballocationCount,
  dealersCount,
  canOpenSettings = true,
  onSelectKhat,
  onOpenSettings,
}: CategoryHubProps) {
  const [group, setGroup] = useState<KhatGroup>('grain');
  const [licenseSubGroup, setLicenseSubGroup] =
    useState<LicenseSubGroup>('new');
  const touchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const subTouchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const grainKhats = khats.filter((k) => k.active && k.group === 'grain');

  const licenseSubKhats = khats.filter((k) => {
    if (!k.active || k.group !== 'license') return false;
    if (licenseSubGroup === 'new' || licenseSubGroup === 'renewal') {
      return (
        k.subGroup === 'new' ||
        k.subGroup === 'renewal' ||
        (!k.subGroup && k.name.includes('লাইসেন্স'))
      );
    }
    if (licenseSubGroup === 'fine') {
      return k.subGroup === 'fine' || k.name.includes('জরিমানা');
    }
    return k.subGroup === 'other' || k.name.includes('অন্যান্য');
  });

  const visibleKhats = group === 'grain' ? grainKhats : licenseSubKhats;
  const activeActionType =
    LICENSE_SUBGROUPS.find((s) => s.id === licenseSubGroup)?.actionType ||
    'নতুন';

  function handleMainSwipeEnd(endX: number) {
    if (touchStartX.current === null) return;
    const dx = endX - touchStartX.current;
    touchStartX.current = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 48) return;
    if (dx < 0 && group === 'grain') setGroup('license');
    if (dx > 0 && group === 'license') setGroup('grain');
  }

  function handleSubSwipeEnd(endX: number) {
    if (subTouchStartX.current === null) return;
    const dx = endX - subTouchStartX.current;
    subTouchStartX.current = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 42) return;
    const idx = LICENSE_SUBGROUPS.findIndex((s) => s.id === licenseSubGroup);
    if (dx < 0 && idx < LICENSE_SUBGROUPS.length - 1) {
      setLicenseSubGroup(LICENSE_SUBGROUPS[idx + 1].id);
    } else if (dx > 0 && idx > 0) {
      setLicenseSubGroup(LICENSE_SUBGROUPS[idx - 1].id);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section
      className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 select-none"
      onTouchStart={(e) => {
        touchStartX.current = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? null;
      }}
      onTouchEnd={(e) => {
        const endX = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX;
        if (typeof endX === 'number') handleMainSwipeEnd(endX);
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="grid flex-1 grid-cols-2 gap-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGroup('grain')}
            className={`flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-colors sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap ${
              group === 'grain'
                ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <Wheat className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>খাদ্যশস্য</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGroup('license')}
            className={`flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-colors sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap ${
              group === 'license'
                ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <FileBadge2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>লাইসেন্স ও অন্যান্য</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {canOpenSettings && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenSettings}
            aria-label="সেটিংস"
            title="খাত ও শিট সেটিংস"
            className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
          >
            <SlidersHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {group === 'license' && (
        <div
          className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-100 bg-rose-50/50 p-1 overflow-x-auto"
          onTouchStart={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            subTouchStartX.current = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? null;
          }}
          onTouchEnd={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            const endX = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX;
            if (typeof endX === 'number') handleSubSwipeEnd(endX);
          }}
        >
          {LICENSE_SUBGROUPS.map((sub) => {
            const active = licenseSubGroup === sub.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={sub.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setLicenseSubGroup(sub.id)}
                className={`flex-1 min-h-[38px] rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-white text-rose-800 shadow-xs border border-rose-200'
                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                {sub.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-x-2 gap-y-5 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
        {visibleKhats.map((khat) => {
          const palette = COLOR_MAP[khat.colorKey] || COLOR_MAP.rose;
          return (
            <button
              key={`${group}-${licenseSubGroup}-${khat.id}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                onSelectKhat(
                  khat,
                  group === 'license' ? activeActionType : undefined
                )
              }
              className="group flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-xl p-1.5 text-center transition-transform active:scale-95 hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              <span
                className={`flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105 ${palette.bg} ${palette.ring}`}
              >
                {renderKhatIcon(khat.iconKey, `h-7 w-7 ${palette.text}`)}
              </span>
