              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 leading-tight">
                {khat.name}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 text-[11px] text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span>বরাদ্দ বাকি: {unlinkedSuballocationCount}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>ডিলার: {dealersCount}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGroup('grain')}
            aria-label="খাদ্যশস্য"
            className={`rounded-md p-1 ${
              group === 'grain' ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-slate-400'
            }`}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 w-4 rounded-full ${
              group === 'grain' ? 'bg-rose-600' : 'bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          />
          <span
            className={`h-1.5 w-4 rounded-full ${
              group === 'license' ? 'bg-rose-600' : 'bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGroup('license')}
            aria-label="লাইসেন্স"
            className={`rounded-md p-1 ${
              group === 'license' ? 'text-rose-700' : 'text-slate-400'
            }`}
          >
            <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/components/CategoryHub.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/DealersView.tsx ====================
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Database,
  Download,
  FileBadge2,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Upload,
  Users,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  DealerRecord,
  KhatConfig,
  LicenseeRecord,
  SuballocationRecord,
} from '../types.ts';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import { formatMoney, toNumericValue } from '../utils/challan.ts';

interface DealersViewProps {
  dealers: DealerRecord[];
  unions: string[];
  licensees?: LicenseeRecord[];
  suballocations?: SuballocationRecord[];
  khats?: KhatConfig[];
  hideDealerIds?: boolean;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

type InnerTab = 'dealers' | 'suballocations' | 'licensees';

export function DealersView({
  dealers,
  unions,
  licensees = [],
  suballocations = [],
  khats = [],
  hideDealerIds = true,
  onRefresh,
}: DealersViewProps) {
  const [innerTab, setInnerTab] = useState<InnerTab>('dealers');
  const [unionName, setUnionName] = useState('');
  const [dealerName, setDealerName] = useState('');
  const [salesCenter, setSalesCenter] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('');
  const [filterUnion, setFilterUnion] = useState('');
  const [bulkText, setBulkText] = useState('');
  const [showBulk, setShowBulk] = useState(false);

  const [subBulkText, setSubBulkText] = useState('');
  const [showSubBulk, setShowSubBulk] = useState(false);

  const [licName, setLicName] = useState('');
  const [licAddress, setLicAddress] = useState('');
  const [licType, setLicType] = useState('ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)');
  const [licNo, setLicNo] = useState('');
  const [licFirstDate, setLicFirstDate] = useState('');
  const [licNid, setLicNid] = useState('');
  const [licBulkText, setLicBulkText] = useState('');
  const [showLicBulk, setShowLicBulk] = useState(false);
  const licFileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState<{
    tone: 'success' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const visibleDealers = useMemo(() => {
    if (!filterUnion) return dealers;
    return dealers.filter((d) => d.unionName === filterUnion);
  }, [dealers, filterUnion]);

  function downloadLicenseeTemplateCsv() {
    const headers = [
      'ক্রমিক নং',
      'লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম ও ঠিকানা',
      'লাইসেন্সের ধরণ',
      'লাইসেন্স নং',
      'প্রথম লাইসেন্স গ্রহণের তারিখ',
      'এনআইডি (ঐচ্ছিক)',
    ];
    const sampleRows = [
      [
        '1',
        'মোঃ আবুল কালাম, শিকারপুর বাজার, উজিরপুর',
        'ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)',
        'UZP-RET-1001',
        '15/01/2024',
        '1985123456789',
      ],
      [
        '2',
        'মেসার্স রিয়াদ এন্টারপ্রাইজ, ইচলাদী, উজিরপুর',
        'ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (পাইকারি)',
        'UZP-WHL-2001',
        '20/03/2023',
        '',
      ],
    ];
    const esc = (v: string) => `"${v.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
    const csv =
      '\uFEFF' +
      [headers.map(esc).join(','), ...sampleRows.map((r) => r.map(esc).join(','))].join(
        '\r\n'
      );
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'Licensee_Upload_Template.csv';
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  function parseCsvLine(line: string, sep: string): string[] {
    if (sep === '\t') return line.split('\t').map((c) => c.trim());
    const out: string[] = [];
    let cur = '';
    let inQuotes = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
      const ch = line[i];
      if (ch === '"') {
        if (inQuotes && line[i + 1] === '"') {
          cur += '"';
          i++;
        } else {
          inQuotes = !inQuotes;
        }
      } else if (ch === ',' && !inQuotes) {
        out.push(cur.trim());
        cur = '';
      } else {
        cur += ch;
      }
    }
    out.push(cur.trim());
    return out;
  }

  async function importLicenseesRawText(rawText: string) {
    const lines = rawText
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((l) => l.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const parsed: Array<Record<string, string>> = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const sep = lines[i].includes('\t') ? '\t' : ',';
      const cols = parseCsvLine(lines[i], sep);
      const joined = cols.join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (
        i === 0 &&
        (joined.includes('ক্রমিক') ||
          joined.includes('লাইসেন্সধারীর') ||
          joined.includes('licenseno'))
      ) {
        continue;
      }
      if (cols.length >= 4) {
        const nameAndAddr = (cols[1] || '').trim();
        const commaIdx = nameAndAddr.indexOf(',');
        const licenseeName =
          commaIdx > 0 ? nameAndAddr.slice(0, commaIdx).trim() : nameAndAddr;
        const address =
          commaIdx > 0 ? nameAndAddr.slice(commaIdx + 1).trim() : '';
        parsed.push({
          licenseeName,
          address,
          licenseType: (cols[2] || '').trim() || 'ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)',
          licenseNo: (cols[3] || '').trim(),
          firstIssueDate: (cols[4] || '').trim(),
          nid: (cols[5] || '').trim(),
        });
      }
    }

    if (parsed.length === 0) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'টেমপ্লেট কলাম অনুযায়ী বৈধ কোনো সারি পাওয়া যায়নি।',
      });
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/licensees/import', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ licensees: parsed }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        added?: number;
        skipped?: number;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'লাইসেন্সধারী ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ');
      }
      setLicBulkText('');
      setShowLicBulk(false);
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `নতুন লাইসেন্সধারী যুক্ত: ${json.added ?? 0} জন (বাদ: ${
          json.skipped ?? 0
        })।`,
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleLicenseeFileUpload(
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    e.target.value = '';
    if (!file) return;
    const text = await file.text();
    await importLicenseesRawText(text);
  }

  async function handleAddLicensee(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!licName.trim() || !licNo.trim()) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম ও লাইসেন্স নং দিন।',
      });
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/licensees', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          licenseeName: licName.trim(),
          address: licAddress.trim(),
          licenseType: licType,
          licenseNo: licNo.trim(),
          firstIssueDate: licFirstDate.trim(),
          nid: licNid.trim(),
        }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'যোগ করা যায়নি');
      }
      setLicName('');
      setLicAddress('');
      setLicNo('');
      setLicFirstDate('');
      setLicNid('');
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'success',
        text: 'লাইসেন্সধারী তালিকাভুক্ত হয়েছে।',
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'যোগ করা যায়নি।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleDeleteLicensee(id: string) {
    const res = await apiFetch(`/api/licensees/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (res.ok) onRefresh();
  }

  async function handleAddDealer(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!unionName.trim() || !dealerName.trim()) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'ইউনিয়নের নাম এবং ডিলারের নাম অবশ্যই দিতে হবে।',
      });
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/dealers', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          unionName: unionName.trim(),
          dealerName: dealerName.trim(),
          salesCenter: salesCenter.trim(),
          category: category.trim(),
        }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'ডিলার যোগ করা যায়নি');
      }
      setDealerName('');
      setSalesCenter('');
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'success',
        text: 'ডিলার সফলভাবে তালিকাভুক্ত হয়েছে।',
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ডিলার যোগ করা যায়নি।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleBulkImportDealers() {
    const raw = bulkText.trim();
    if (!raw) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'Food_Dealer শিট থেকে কপি করা সারি অথবা JSON পেস্ট করুন।',
      });
      return;
    }

    let parsedDealers: Array<{
      id?: string;
      unionName: string;
      dealerName: string;
      salesCenter: string;
      category?: string;
    }> = [];

    if (raw.startsWith('{') || raw.startsWith('[')) {
      try {
        const obj = JSON.parse(raw) as
          | { dealers?: Array<Record<string, unknown>> }
          | Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
        const list = Array.isArray(obj)
          ? obj
          : Array.isArray(obj.dealers)
          ? obj.dealers
          : [];
        parsedDealers = list.map((d) => ({
          id: String(d.id || ''),
          unionName: String(d.unionName || '').trim(),
          dealerName: String(d.dealerName || '').trim(),
          salesCenter: String(d.salesCenter || '').trim(),
          category: String(d.category || '').trim(),
        }));
      } catch {
        setStatusMsg({
          tone: 'error',
          text: 'JSON ফরম্যাট সঠিক নয়।',
        });
        return;
      }
    } else {
      const lines = raw
        .split(/\r?\n/)
        .map((l) => l.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const cols = lines[i].includes('\t')
          ? lines[i].split('\t')
          : lines[i].split(',');
        const first = (cols[0] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (
          i === 0 &&
          (first === 'id' || first.includes('ইউনিয়ন') || first === 'unionname')
        ) {
          continue;
        }
        if (
          cols.length >= 6 &&
          (/^(df|fd|dlr)-/i.test(cols[0].trim()) || cols[0].trim().length > 10)
        ) {
          parsedDealers.push({
            id: cols[0].trim(),
            dealerName: (cols[1] || '').trim(),
            unionName: (cols[5] || '').trim() || 'অন্যান্য',
            salesCenter: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
            category: 'খাদ্য বান্ধব',
          });
        } else if (cols.length >= 2) {
          parsedDealers.push({
            unionName: cols[0].trim(),
            dealerName: cols[1].trim(),
            salesCenter: (cols[2] || '').trim(),
            category: (cols[3] || '').trim(),
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (parsedDealers.length === 0) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'কোনো বৈধ ডিলার তথ্য পাওয়া যায়নি।',
      });
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/dealers/import', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ dealers: parsedDealers }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        added?: number;
        skipped?: number;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে');
      }
      setBulkText('');
      setShowBulk(false);
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `নতুন ডিলার ${json.added ?? 0} জন, বাদ ${json.skipped ?? 0}টি।`,
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleBulkImportSuballocations() {
    const raw = subBulkText.trim();
    if (!raw) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'Dealer_Suballocation শিট থেকে কপি করা সারি পেস্ট করুন।',
      });
      return;
    }

    const lines = raw
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((l) => l.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const parsed: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const cols = lines[i].includes('\t')
        ? lines[i].split('\t')
        : lines[i].split(',');
      const joinedHeader = cols.join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (
        i === 0 &&
        (joinedHeader.includes('cardcount') ||
          joinedHeader.includes('totalamount') ||
          joinedHeader.includes('allocationqty'))
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (cols.length >= 14) {
        parsed.push({
          dealerName: (cols[1] || cols[2] || cols[3] || '').trim(),
          unionName: (cols[4] || cols[5] || '').trim(),
          category: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
          cardCount: toNumericValue(cols[7]),
          unusedCards: toNumericValue(cols[8]),
          allocationQty: toNumericValue(cols[9]),
          totalAmount: toNumericValue(cols[10]),
          taxAmount: toNumericValue(cols[11]),
          createdAt: (cols[12] || '').trim(),
          id: (cols[13] || '').trim(),
        });
      } else if (cols.length >= 7) {
        parsed.push({
          dealerName: '',
          cardCount: toNumericValue(cols[0]),
          unusedCards: toNumericValue(cols[1]),
          allocationQty: toNumericValue(cols[2]),
          totalAmount: toNumericValue(cols[3]),
          taxAmount: toNumericValue(cols[4]),
          createdAt: (cols[5] || '').trim(),
          id: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
        });
      }
    }

    if (parsed.length === 0) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'Dealer_Suballocation কলাম ফরম্যাট অনুযায়ী সারি পাওয়া যায়নি।',
      });
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/suballocations/import', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ suballocations: parsed }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        added?: number;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'উপ-বরাদ্দ ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
      }
      setSubBulkText('');
      setShowSubBulk(false);
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `Dealer_Suballocation থেকে নতুন ${json.added ?? 0}টি সারি ইমপোর্ট হয়েছে।`,
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'উপ-বরাদ্দ ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleDeleteDealer(id: string) {
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch(`/api/dealers/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (res.ok) onRefresh();
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setInnerTab('dealers');
              setStatusMsg(null);
            }}
            className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
              innerTab === 'dealers'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <Users className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            ডিলার ({dealers.length})
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setInnerTab('suballocations');
              setStatusMsg(null);
            }}
            className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
              innerTab === 'suballocations'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <Database className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            উপ-বরাদ্দ ({suballocations.length})
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setInnerTab('licensees');
              setStatusMsg(null);
            }}
            className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
              innerTab === 'licensees'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <FileBadge2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            খুচরা ও পাইকারি লাইসেন্স ({licensees.length})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {innerTab === 'dealers' && (
        <>
          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  ডিলার ও ইউনিয়ন মাস্টার
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  ইউনিয়ন: {unions.length}টি · ডিলার: {dealers.length} জন
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowBulk(!showBulk)}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                {showBulk ? 'বন্ধ করুন' : 'বাল্ক ইমপোর্ট'}
              </button>
            </div>

            <form
              onSubmit={handleAddDealer}
              className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-5 sm:items-end"
            >
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ইউনিয়ন
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  list="existing-unions-list"
                  value={unionName}
                  onChange={(e) => setUnionName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ইউনিয়ন"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
                <datalist id="existing-unions-list">
                  {unions.map((u) => (
                    <option key={u} value={u} />
                  ))}
                </datalist>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ডিলারের নাম
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={dealerName}
                  onChange={(e) => setDealerName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="নাম"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  খাত
                </label>
                <select
                  value={category}
                  onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                >
                  <option value="">সাধারণ</option>
                  {khats.map((k) => (
                    <option key={k.id} value={k.name}>
                      {k.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  বিক্রয় কেন্দ্র
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={salesCenter}
                  onChange={(e) => setSalesCenter(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="বাজার / কেন্দ্র"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[42px] items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-60 whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                যোগ করুন
              </button>
            </form>

            {showBulk && (
              <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
                <textarea
                  rows={4}
                  value={bulkText}
                  onChange={(e) => setBulkText(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Food_Dealer শিট থেকে সারি বা JSON পেস্ট করুন..."
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-3 text-xs text-slate-900"
                />
                <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={saving}
                    onClick={handleBulkImportDealers}
                    className="rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-teal-800 disabled:opacity-60"
                  >
                    ইমপোর্ট করুন
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {statusMsg && (
              <div
                role="status"
                className={`mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm ${
                  statusMsg.tone === 'success'
                    ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900'
                    : 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-900'
                }`}
              >
                {statusMsg.tone === 'success' && (
                  <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
                )}
                <span>{statusMsg.text}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">ডিলার তালিকা</h3>
              <select
                value={filterUnion}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterUnion(e.target.value)}
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium"
              >
                <option value="">সব ইউনিয়ন ({dealers.length})</option>
                {unions.map((u) => (
                  <option key={u} value={u}>
                    {u}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
              {visibleDealers.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                  কোনো ডিলার নেই
                </div>
              ) : (
                <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                      <th className="px-3 py-2.5 w-14 text-center">ক্রমিক</th>
                      {!hideDealerIds && <th className="px-3 py-2.5">আইডি</th>}
                      <th className="px-3 py-2.5">ডিলারের নাম</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2.5">ইউনিয়ন</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2.5">খাত / কেন্দ্র</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right w-16">মুছুন</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                    {visibleDealers.map((d, idx) => (
                      <tr key={d.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                          {idx + 1}
                        </td>
                        {!hideDealerIds && (
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            {d.id}
                          </td>
                        )}
                        <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">
                          {d.dealerName}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700">
                          {d.unionName || '—'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
                          {[d.category, d.salesCenter]
                            .filter(Boolean)
                            .join(' · ') || '—'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteDealer(d.id)}
                            className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-700"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        </>
      )}

      {innerTab === 'suballocations' && (
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
              ডিলার উপ-বরাদ্দ (Dealer_Suballocation)
            </h2>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowSubBulk(!showSubBulk)}
              className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              {showSubBulk ? 'বন্ধ করুন' : 'Sheet থেকে পেস্ট'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {showSubBulk && (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 space-y-2">
              <textarea
                rows={4}
                value={subBulkText}
                onChange={(e) => setSubBulkText(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Dealer_Suballocation A:N বা H:N কপি করে পেস্ট করুন..."
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-3 text-xs"
              />
              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={saving}
                  onClick={() => void handleBulkImportSuballocations()}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                >
                  ইমপোর্ট করুন
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            {suballocations.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                কোনো উপ-বরাদ্দ নেই
              </div>
            ) : (
              <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    {!hideDealerIds && <th className="px-3 py-2.5">আইডি</th>}
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">ডিলার ও ইউনিয়ন</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">বরাদ্দ (টন)</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">মূল টাকা</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">আয়কর</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                  {suballocations.map((s) => (
                    <tr key={s.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50">
                      {!hideDealerIds && (
                        <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                          {s.id}
                        </td>
                      )}
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs">
                        <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                          {s.dealerName || '—'}
                        </div>
                        {s.unionName && (
                          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            {s.unionName}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs">
                        {s.allocationQty}
                      </td>
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs font-bold text-teal-800">
                        ৳ {formatMoney(s.totalAmount)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs font-bold text-amber-800">
                        ৳ {formatMoney(s.taxAmount)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {innerTab === 'licensees' && (
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 space-y-4">
          <input
            ref={licFileInputRef}
            type="file"
            accept=".csv,.tsv,.txt"
            className="hidden"
            onChange={(e) => void handleLicenseeFileUpload(e)}
          />

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্সধারী (খুচরা ও পাইকারি)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                এক্সেল/CSV টেমপ্লেট ডাউনলোড করে নাম, ঠিকানা ও লাইসেন্স নং পূরণ
                করে আপলোড করুন
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={downloadLicenseeTemplateCsv}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                টেমপ্লেট ডাউনলোড
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => licFileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-teal-800"
              >
                <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                ফাইল আপলোড (CSV)
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowLicBulk(!showLicBulk)}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700"
              >
                পেস্ট করুন
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form
            onSubmit={handleAddLicensee}
            className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-6 sm:items-end"
          >
            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={licName}
                onChange={(e) => setLicName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="নাম"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                ঠিকানা
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={licAddress}
                onChange={(e) => setLicAddress(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="ঠিকানা"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                ধরণ
              </label>
              <select
                value={licType}
                onChange={(e) => setLicType(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs"
              >
                <option value="ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (খুচরা)">খুচরা</option>
                <option value="ফুড গ্রেইন লাইসেন্স (পাইকারি)">পাইকারি</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                লাইসেন্স নং
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={licNo}
                onChange={(e) => setLicNo(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="লাইসেন্স নং"
                className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white"
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              যোগ
            </button>
          </form>

          {showLicBulk && (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 space-y-2">
              <textarea
                rows={4}
                value={licBulkText}
                onChange={(e) => setLicBulkText(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="ক্রমিক নং [Tab] নাম ও ঠিকানা [Tab] লাইসেন্সের ধরণ [Tab] লাইসেন্স নং [Tab] প্রথম তারিখ [Tab] এনআইডি"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-3 text-xs"
              />
              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={saving}
                  onClick={() => void importLicenseesRawText(licBulkText)}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white"
                >
                  ইমপোর্ট করুন
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {statusMsg && (
            <div
              role="status"
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm ${
                statusMsg.tone === 'success'
                  ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900'
                  : 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-900'
              }`}
            >
              {statusMsg.tone === 'success' && (
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              )}
              <span>{statusMsg.text}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            {licensees.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                কোনো লাইসেন্সধারী যুক্ত হয়নি
              </div>
            ) : (
              <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center w-14">ক্রমিক</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">নাম ও ঠিকানা</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">লাইসেন্সের ধরণ</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">লাইসেন্স নং</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5">প্রথম তারিখ</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right w-16">মুছুন</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                  {licensees.map((l) => (
                    <tr key={l.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50">
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2 text-center text-slate-500">
                        {l.serialNo}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                          {l.licenseeName}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                          {l.address || '—'}
                          {l.nid ? ` · NID: ${l.nid}` : ''}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 text-slate-700">
                        {l.licenseType}
                      </td>
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2 font-bold text-teal-800">
                        {l.licenseNo}
                      </td>
                      <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2 text-slate-600">
                        {l.firstIssueDate || '—'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => void handleDeleteLicensee(l.id)}
                          className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-700"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/components/DealersView.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/LoginView.tsx ====================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Lock, Mail, User, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import type { UserAccount } from '../types';
import { setAuthToken } from '../utils/api';

interface LoginViewProps {
  onLoginSuccess: (user: UserAccount) => void;
  lockEnabled?: boolean;
}

export const LoginView: React.FC<LoginViewProps> = ({
  onLoginSuccess,
  lockEnabled = false,
}) => {
  const [identifier, setIdentifier] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showIdentifierField, setShowIdentifierField] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [quickLoading, setQuickLoading] = useState<'admin' | 'operator' | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [invitedNotice, setInvitedNotice] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const inviteEmail = params.get('inviteEmail');
    if (inviteEmail) {
      setIdentifier(inviteEmail);
      setShowIdentifierField(true);
      setInvitedNotice(
        `আপনাকে ${inviteEmail} ইমেইলের মাধ্যমে এই অ্যাপে এক্সেস দেওয়া হয়েছে। এডমিন প্রদত্ত পাসওয়ার্ড দিয়ে লগইন করুন।`
      );
    }
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        credentials: 'include',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          identifier: showIdentifierField ? identifier.trim() : '',
          password,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || 'লগইন ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
        return;
      }
      if (data.token) {
        setAuthToken(data.token);
      }
      onLoginSuccess(data.user);
    } catch {
      setError('সার্ভারের সাথে সংযোগ স্থাপন করা যায়নি।');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickLogin = async (target: 'admin' | 'operator') => {
    setError('');
    setQuickLoading(target);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/quick-login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        credentials: 'include',
        body: JSON.stringify({ target }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || 'লগইন ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
        return;
      }
      if (data.token) {
        setAuthToken(data.token);
      }
      onLoginSuccess(data.user);
    } catch {
      setError('সার্ভারের সাথে সংযোগ স্থাপন করা যায়নি।');
    } finally {
      setQuickLoading(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-7 sm:p-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="mb-6">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-rose-700 text-white mb-4">
            {lockEnabled ? (
              <Lock className="w-5 h-5" />
            ) : (
              <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
            )}
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            {lockEnabled ? 'পোর্টাল লক করা আছে' : 'চালান স্ক্যান ও ব্যবস্থাপনা'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 mt-1">
            {lockEnabled
              ? 'প্রবেশ করতে নির্ধারিত লক পাসওয়ার্ড দিন'
              : 'ট্রেজারি চালান, আয়কর প্রাপ্য ও অফিসিয়াল রিপোর্ট পোর্টাল'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {invitedNotice && (
          <div className="mb-5 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-900 leading-relaxed">
            {invitedNotice}
          </div>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-5 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-xs sm:text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Instant One-Tap Access (only shown when Lock System is OFF) */}
        {!lockEnabled && (
          <>
            <div className="mb-6 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-700" />
                <span>সরাসরি প্রবেশ করুন</span>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={quickLoading !== null}
                  onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('admin')}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl transition-colors disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{quickLoading === 'admin' ? 'প্রবেশ হচ্ছে...' : 'এডমিন প্রবেশ'}</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={quickLoading !== null}
                  onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('operator')}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition-colors disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-slate-600" />
                  <span>{quickLoading === 'operator' ? 'প্রবেশ হচ্ছে...' : 'সীমিত ইউজার'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center mb-5">
              <div className="border-t border-slate-200 w-full" />
              <span className="bg-white px-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                অথবা পাসওয়ার্ড দিয়ে লগইন
              </span>
              <div className="border-t border-slate-200 w-full" />
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4" autoComplete="off">
          {showIdentifierField && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                ইউজার আইডি বা ইমেইল অ্যাড্রেস (ঐচ্ছিক)
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  name="challan_identifier"
                  autoComplete="off"
                  value={identifier}
                  onChange={(e) => setIdentifier(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ইউজার আইডি বা ইমেইল"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-600 focus:border-rose-600"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              পাসওয়ার্ড
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
              <input
                type="password"
                name="challan_access_code"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                autoFocus
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="পাসওয়ার্ড লিখুন"
                required
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-600 focus:border-rose-600"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>{loading ? 'যাচাই করা হচ্ছে...' : lockEnabled ? 'আনলক করুন' : 'লগইন করুন'}</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          <div className="text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowIdentifierField((v) => !v)}
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:underline cursor-pointer"
            >
              {showIdentifierField
                ? 'শুধু পাসওয়ার্ড দিয়ে প্রবেশ করুন'
                : 'অন্য ইউজার আইডি / ইমেইল দিয়ে লগইন'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


// ==================== END: src/components/LoginView.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/QrScannerModal.tsx ====================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Html5Qrcode } from 'html5-qrcode';
import {
  Camera,
  Flashlight,
  FlashlightOff,
  Focus,
  RefreshCw,
  Upload,
  X,
  ZoomIn,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface ExtendedCapabilities extends MediaTrackCapabilities {
  torch?: boolean;
  zoom?: { min: number; max: number; step?: number };
  focusMode?: string[];
}

interface ExtendedConstraintSet extends MediaTrackConstraintSet {
  torch?: boolean;
  zoom?: number;
  focusMode?: string;
}

interface BarcodeDetectorInstance {
  detect: (
    source: ImageBitmapSource
  ) => Promise<Array<{ rawValue?: string }>>;
}

interface QrScannerModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  targetLabel: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onDetected: (decodedText: string) => void;
  onPickFileFallback: () => void;
}

function playSuccessTone() {
  try {
    const AudioCtx =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext })
        .webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    const ctx = new AudioCtx();
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.5, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.14);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
  } catch {
    // ignore audio block
  }
}

export function QrScannerModal({
  open,
  targetLabel,
  onClose,
  onDetected,
  onPickFileFallback,
}: QrScannerModalProps) {
  const decodeRegionId = `qr_canvas_decode_${useId().replace(/:/g, '_')}`;
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const trackRef = useRef<MediaStreamTrack | null>(null);
  const handledRef = useRef(false);
  const html5DecoderRef = useRef<Html5Qrcode | null>(null);

  const [cameras, setCameras] = useState<MediaDeviceInfo[]>([]);
  const [selectedDeviceId, setSelectedDeviceId] = useState<string>('');
  const [starting, setStarting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');

  const [torchSupported, setTorchSupported] = useState(false);
  const [torchOn, setTorchOn] = useState(false);

  const [zoomRange, setZoomRange] = useState<{
    min: number;
    max: number;
    step: number;
    hardware: boolean;
  }>({ min: 1, max: 3, step: 0.25, hardware: false });
  const [zoomValue, setZoomValue] = useState(1);

  const [focusSupported, setFocusSupported] = useState(false);
  const [decoderMode, setDecoderMode] = useState<'native' | 'canvas'>(
    'canvas'
  );

  const stopStream = useCallback(() => {
    if (trackRef.current) {
      try {
        trackRef.current.stop();
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
      trackRef.current = null;
    }
    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => {
        try {
          t.stop();
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      });
      streamRef.current = null;
    }
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.srcObject = null;
    }
    setTorchOn(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      stopStream();
      return;
    }

    handledRef.current = false;
    setErrorMsg('');
    setStarting(true);
    setZoomValue(1);

    let cancelled = false;
    let rafTimer: number | null = null;
    let fallbackBusy = false;

    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });

    const BarcodeDetectorCtor = (
      window as unknown as {
        BarcodeDetector?: new (opts: {
          formats: string[];
        }) => BarcodeDetectorInstance;
      }
    ).BarcodeDetector;

    const nativeDetector = BarcodeDetectorCtor
      ? new BarcodeDetectorCtor({ formats: ['qr_code'] })
      : null;

    setDecoderMode(nativeDetector ? 'native' : 'canvas');

    if (!html5DecoderRef.current) {
      try {
        html5DecoderRef.current = new Html5Qrcode(decodeRegionId);
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }

    async function startCamera() {
      try {
        const videoConstraints: MediaTrackConstraints = selectedDeviceId
          ? {
              deviceId: { exact: selectedDeviceId },
              width: { ideal: 1920 },
              height: { ideal: 1080 },
            }
          : {
              facingMode: { ideal: 'environment' },
              width: { ideal: 1920 },
              height: { ideal: 1080 },
            };

        const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
          video: videoConstraints,
          audio: false,
        });

        if (cancelled) {
          stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
          return;
        }

        streamRef.current = stream;
        const videoTrack = stream.getVideoTracks()[0] || null;
        trackRef.current = videoTrack;

        try {
          const allDevices = await navigator.mediaDevices.enumerateDevices();
          const videoInputs = allDevices.filter((d) => d.kind === 'videoinput');
          if (!cancelled) setCameras(videoInputs);
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }

        if (videoTrack && typeof videoTrack.getCapabilities === 'function') {
          const caps = videoTrack.getCapabilities() as ExtendedCapabilities;
          setTorchSupported(Boolean(caps.torch));

          const supportsContinuous =
            Array.isArray(caps.focusMode) &&
            caps.focusMode.includes('continuous');
          setFocusSupported(
            Boolean(Array.isArray(caps.focusMode) && caps.focusMode.length > 0)
          );

          if (supportsContinuous) {
            try {
              const c: ExtendedConstraintSet = { focusMode: 'continuous' };
              await videoTrack.applyConstraints({
                advanced: [c as MediaTrackConstraintSet],
              });
            } catch {
              // ignore
            }
          }

          if (caps.zoom && caps.zoom.max > caps.zoom.min) {
            setZoomRange({
              min: caps.zoom.min,
              max: Math.min(caps.zoom.max, 5),
              step: caps.zoom.step || 0.1,
              hardware: true,
            });
            setZoomValue(caps.zoom.min);
          } else {
            setZoomRange({ min: 1, max: 3, step: 0.25, hardware: false });
          }
        }

        const videoEl = videoRef.current;
        if (videoEl) {
          videoEl.srcObject = stream;
          await videoEl.play();
        }

        if (!cancelled) {
          setStarting(false);
          scheduleScanTick();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setStarting(false);
          const msg =
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ক্যামেরা চালু করা যায়নি।';
          setErrorMsg(
            `ক্যামেরা চালু করা যায়নি (${msg})। নিচের "ছবি তুলে স্ক্যান" ব্যবহার করুন।`
          );
        }
      }
    }

    function handleSuccess(rawText: string) {
      if (handledRef.current || !rawText.trim()) return;
      handledRef.current = true;
      playSuccessTone();
      stopStream();
      onDetected(rawText.trim());
    }

    async function scanFrame() {
      if (cancelled || handledRef.current) return;
      const videoEl = videoRef.current;
      if (
        !videoEl ||
        videoEl.readyState < 2 ||
        videoEl.videoWidth === 0 ||
        videoEl.videoHeight === 0
      ) {
        scheduleScanTick();
        return;
      }

      if (nativeDetector) {
        try {
          const barcodes = await nativeDetector.detect(videoEl);
          if (barcodes.length > 0 && barcodes[0].rawValue) {
            handleSuccess(barcodes[0].rawValue);
            return;
          }
        } catch {
          // fallback
        }
      }

      if (!fallbackBusy && ctx && html5DecoderRef.current) {
        fallbackBusy = true;
        try {
          const vw = videoEl.videoWidth;
          const vh = videoEl.videoHeight;
          const side = Math.min(vw, vh);
          const cropSize = Math.floor(side * 0.78);
          const sx = Math.floor((vw - cropSize) / 2);
          const sy = Math.floor((vh - cropSize) / 2);

          const outSize = Math.min(cropSize, 720);
          canvas.width = outSize;
          canvas.height = outSize;
          ctx.drawImage(
            videoEl,
            sx,
            sy,
            cropSize,
            cropSize,
            0,
            0,
            outSize,
            outSize
          );

          const blob = await new Promise<Blob | null>((resolve) =>
            canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/jpeg', 0.9)
          );
          if (blob && !cancelled && !handledRef.current) {
            const file = new File([blob], 'frame.jpg', {
              type: 'image/jpeg',
            });
            const decoded = await html5DecoderRef.current.scanFile(
              file,
              false
            );
            if (decoded) {
              handleSuccess(decoded);
              return;
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // ignore
        } finally {
          fallbackBusy = false;
        }
      }

      scheduleScanTick();
    }

    function scheduleScanTick() {
      if (cancelled || handledRef.current) return;
      rafTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        void scanFrame();
      }, nativeDetector ? 110 : 220);
    }

    void startCamera();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (rafTimer !== null) clearTimeout(rafTimer);
      stopStream();
    };
  }, [open, selectedDeviceId, decodeRegionId, onDetected, stopStream]);

  async function toggleTorch() {
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (!track || !torchSupported) return;
    const nextState = !torchOn;
    try {
      const c: ExtendedConstraintSet = { torch: nextState };
      await track.applyConstraints({
        advanced: [c as MediaTrackConstraintSet],
      });
      setTorchOn(nextState);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  async function handleZoomChange(nextZoom: number) {
    setZoomValue(nextZoom);
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (track && zoomRange.hardware) {
      try {
        const c: ExtendedConstraintSet = { zoom: nextZoom };
        await track.applyConstraints({
          advanced: [c as MediaTrackConstraintSet],
        });
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
  }

  async function triggerRefocus() {
    const track = trackRef.current;
    if (!track || !focusSupported) return;
    try {
      const single: ExtendedConstraintSet = { focusMode: 'single-shot' };
      await track.applyConstraints({
        advanced: [single as MediaTrackConstraintSet],
      });
      setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const cont: ExtendedConstraintSet = { focusMode: 'continuous' };
          await track.applyConstraints({
            advanced: [cont as MediaTrackConstraintSet],
          });
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      }, 450);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  function cycleCamera() {
    if (cameras.length < 2) return;
    const currentIdx = cameras.findIndex(
      (c) => c.deviceId === selectedDeviceId
    );
    const nextIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % cameras.length;
    setSelectedDeviceId(cameras[nextIdx].deviceId);
  }

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/75 p-3 sm:p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="qr-modal-title"
    >
      <div id={decodeRegionId} className="hidden" />

      <div className="w-full max-w-lg overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <Camera className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-rose-700" />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h3
                id="qr-modal-title"
                className="truncate text-sm font-bold text-slate-900"
              >
                {targetLabel} — লাইভ QR স্ক্যানার
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                {decoderMode === 'native'
                  ? 'উচ্চগতির অটোফোকাস স্ক্যান সক্রিয়'
                  : 'স্মার্ট ফ্রেম ডিকোডার সক্রিয়'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              stopStream();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900"
            aria-label="বন্ধ করুন"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div
            onClick={triggerRefocus}
            className="relative aspect-[4/3] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-slate-950 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <video
              ref={videoRef}
              playsInline
              muted
              autoPlay
              style={
                !zoomRange.hardware && zoomValue > 1
                  ? { transform: `scale(${zoomValue})` }
                  : undefined
              }
              className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-150"
            />

            {!starting && !errorMsg && (
              <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                <div className="relative h-52 w-52 sm:h-56 sm:w-56">
                  <span className="absolute left-0 top-0 h-7 w-7 rounded-tl-xl border-l-4 border-t-4 border-emerald-400" />
                  <span className="absolute right-0 top-0 h-7 w-7 rounded-tr-xl border-r-4 border-t-4 border-emerald-400" />
                  <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-7 w-7 rounded-bl-xl border-b-4 border-l-4 border-emerald-400" />
                  <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 h-7 w-7 rounded-br-xl border-b-4 border-r-4 border-emerald-400" />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {starting && !errorMsg && (
              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                ক্যামেরা ও অটোফোকাস চালু হচ্ছে...
              </div>
            )}

            {!starting && !errorMsg && (
              <div
                className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 right-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {torchSupported && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={toggleTorch}
                      className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold backdrop-blur-md transition-colors ${
                        torchOn
                          ? 'bg-amber-400 text-slate-950'
                          : 'bg-slate-900/75 text-white hover:bg-slate-900'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {torchOn ? (
                        <FlashlightOff className="h-4 w-4" />
                      ) : (
                        <Flashlight className="h-4 w-4" />
                      )}
                      ফ্ল্যাশ
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {focusSupported && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={triggerRefocus}
                      className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/75 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-md hover:bg-slate-900"
                    >
                      <Focus className="h-4 w-4" />
                      ফোকাস
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {cameras.length > 1 && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={cycleCamera}
                    className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/75 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-md hover:bg-slate-900"
                  >
                    <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    ক্যামেরা বদলান
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {!starting && !errorMsg && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
              <ZoomIn className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-slate-600" />
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
                জুম
              </span>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={zoomRange.min}
                max={zoomRange.max}
                step={zoomRange.step}
                value={zoomValue}
                onChange={(e) => void handleZoomChange(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="h-2 flex-1 accent-rose-700"
              />
              <span className="font-mono-num w-10 text-right text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                {zoomValue.toFixed(1)}x
              </span>
            </div>
          )}

          {errorMsg ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-800">
              {errorMsg}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              চালানের উপরের ডান কোণের QR কোড ফ্রেমে ধরুন। ফোকাস ঠিক করতে ভিডিওতে
              ট্যাপ করুন অথবা কাছাকাছি জুম ব্যবহার করুন।
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 px-4 py-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              stopStream();
              onClose();
              onPickFileFallback();
            }}
            className="inline-flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            <Upload className="h-4 w-4" />
            ছবি তুলে স্ক্যান
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              stopStream();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="min-h-[44px] rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            বন্ধ করুন
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/components/QrScannerModal.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/RecordsView.tsx ====================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Archive,
  CalendarCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  CloudUpload,
  Download,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Loader2,
  Pencil,
  Search,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  AppSettings,
  ChallanRecord,
  CustomRegisterColumn,
  DateBackupRecord,
  DuplicateBackupRecord,
  KhatConfig,
} from '../types.ts';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import {
  CHALLAN_CATEGORIES,
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  formatMoney,
  getOfficialVerificationUrl,
  maskDateInput,
  normalizeChallanNo,
  toEnglishDigits,
  toNumericValue,
} from '../utils/challan.ts';
import {
  getSheetPushCapability,
  pushRecordsToGoogleSheet,
} from '../services/googleSheets.ts';
import { getGoogleAccessToken, googleSignIn } from '../services/googleAuth.ts';

interface RecordsViewProps {
  records: ChallanRecord[];
  loading: boolean;
  settings?: AppSettings;
  khats?: KhatConfig[];
  customColumns?: CustomRegisterColumn[];
  hideDealerIds?: boolean;
  canEdit?: boolean;
  canDelete?: boolean;
  canImport?: boolean;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

type ToolTab = 'none' | 'dates' | 'import' | 'backups';

export function RecordsView({
  records,
  loading,
  settings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  khats = [],
  customColumns = [],
  hideDealerIds = true,
  canEdit = true,
  canDelete = true,
  canImport = true,
  onRefresh,
}: RecordsViewProps) {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState('সব ক্যাটেগরি');
  const [groupByDealer, setGroupByDealer] = useState(false);
  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<ToolTab>('none');
  const [toolMessage, setToolMessage] = useState<{
    tone: 'success' | 'error' | 'neutral';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [sheetPushBusy, setSheetPushBusy] = useState(false);
  const [pushingRowId, setPushingRowId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pendingPushRecords, setPendingPushRecords] = useState<ChallanRecord[] | null>(
    null
  );

  const [swapAmbiguous, setSwapAmbiguous] = useState(false);
  const [dateFixBusy, setDateFixBusy] = useState(false);

  const [importText, setImportText] = useState('');
  const [importBusy, setImportBusy] = useState(false);

  const [duplicateBackups, setDuplicateBackups] = useState<
    DuplicateBackupRecord[]
  >([]);
  const [dateBackups, setDateBackups] = useState<DateBackupRecord[]>([]);

  const [editingRecord, setEditingRecord] = useState<ChallanRecord | null>(
    null
  );
  const [editSaving, setEditSaving] = useState(false);
  const [deletingRecord, setDeletingRecord] = useState<ChallanRecord | null>(
    null
  );
  const [deleteBusy, setDeleteBusy] = useState(false);

  const categoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const dynamicNames = khats.map((k) => k.name);
    const existingNames = records.map((r) => r.challanCategory).filter(Boolean);
    return Array.from(
      new Set([...dynamicNames, ...existingNames, ...CHALLAN_CATEGORIES])
    );
  }, [khats, records]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const q = toEnglishDigits(searchQuery).trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = records.filter((r) => {
      const catOk =
        categoryFilter === 'সব ক্যাটেগরি' ||
        r.challanCategory === categoryFilter;
      if (!catOk) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      const hay = [
        r.challanNo,
        r.incomeTaxChallanNo,
        r.vatChallanNo,
        r.licenseNo,
        r.new37Code,
        r.dealerAllocationId,
        r.dealerName,
        r.unionName,
        r.department,
        r.toOrganization,
        r.submittedThrough,
        r.submittedOnBehalfOf,
        r.description,
        r.challanDate,
        r.incomeTaxDate,
        r.vatDate,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();
      return hay.includes(q);
    });

    if (groupByDealer) {
      return [...list].sort(
        (a, b) =>
          (a.dealerName || 'zzz').localeCompare(b.dealerName || 'zzz', 'bn') ||
          (a.challanDate || '').localeCompare(b.challanDate || '') ||
          a.serialNo - b.serialNo
      );
    }
    return list;
  }, [records, searchQuery, categoryFilter, groupByDealer]);

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    let amount = 0;
    let taxAmount = 0;
    let vatAmount = 0;
    let quantity = 0;
    for (const r of filteredRecords) {
      amount += toNumericValue(r.amount);
      taxAmount += toNumericValue(r.incomeTaxAmount);
      vatAmount += toNumericValue(r.vatAmount);
      quantity += toNumericValue(r.quantity);
    }
    return { amount, taxAmount, vatAmount, quantity };
  }, [filteredRecords]);

  async function runFixDates() {
    setDateFixBusy(true);
    setToolMessage(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/maintenance/fix-dates', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ swapAmbiguous }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'তারিখ সংশোধন ব্যর্থ হয়েছে');
      }
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: json.message || 'তারিখ ফরম্যাট ঠিক করা হয়েছে।',
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'তারিখ সংশোধন ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setDateFixBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function openBackupsTool() {
    if (activeTool === 'backups') {
      setActiveTool('none');
      return;
    }
    setActiveTool('backups');
    setToolMessage(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/backups');
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        duplicateBackups: DuplicateBackupRecord[];
        dateBackups: DateBackupRecord[];
      };
      if (res.ok && json.success) {
        setDuplicateBackups(json.duplicateBackups || []);
        setDateBackups(json.dateBackups || []);
      }
    } catch {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'ব্যাকআপ তালিকা লোড করা যায়নি।',
      });
    }
  }

  async function handleBulkImport() {
    const raw = importText.trim();
    if (!raw) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'ইমপোর্ট করার জন্য Google Sheet থেকে কপি করা সারি পেস্ট করুন।',
      });
      return;
    }

    const lines = raw
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((l) => l.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const parsedRows: Array<Record<string, string>> = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const cols = lines[i].includes('\t')
        ? lines[i].split('\t')
        : lines[i].split(',');

      if (
        i === 0 &&
        (cols[0]?.includes('ক্রমিক') ||
          cols[0]?.toLowerCase() === 'id' ||
          cols[7]?.toLowerCase() === 'challanno' ||
          cols[7]?.includes('চালান'))
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (/^CHL-/i.test((cols[0] || '').trim()) && cols.length >= 12) {
        parsedRows.push({
          dealerAllocationId: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
          challanCategory: (cols[3] || '').trim() || 'খাদ্য বান্ধব',
          toOrganization: (cols[2] || '').trim(),
          challanNo: (cols[7] || '').trim(),
          challanDate: (cols[8] || '').trim(),
          rate: (cols[9] || '').trim(),
          quantity: (cols[10] || '').trim(),
          amount: (cols[11] || '').trim(),
          incomeTaxChallanNo: (cols[12] || '').trim(),
          incomeTaxDate: (cols[13] || '').trim(),
          incomeTaxAmount: (cols[14] || '').trim(),
          description: (cols[15] || '').trim(),
          entryTime: (cols[16] || '').trim(),
          recordId: (cols[17] || '').trim(),
        });
      } else if (cols.length >= 13) {
        parsedRows.push({
          unionName: (cols[1] || '').trim(),
          dealerName: (cols[2] || '').trim(),
          challanCategory: (cols[3] || '').trim() || 'মূল চালান',
          toOrganization: (cols[4] || '').trim(),
          submittedThrough: (cols[5] || '').trim(),
          submittedOnBehalfOf: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
          challanNo: (cols[7] || '').trim(),
          description: (cols[8] || '').trim(),
          quantity: (cols[9] || '').trim(),
          rate: (cols[10] || '').trim(),
          amount: (cols[11] || '').trim(),
          challanDate: (cols[12] || '').trim(),
          recordId: (cols[13] || '').trim(),
          pdfUrl: (cols[14] || '').trim(),
          entryTime: (cols[15] || '').trim(),
        });
      } else if (cols.length >= 9) {
        parsedRows.push({
          unionName: '',
          dealerName: '',
          challanCategory: 'মূল চালান',
          toOrganization: (cols[0] || '').trim(),
          submittedThrough: (cols[1] || '').trim(),
          submittedOnBehalfOf: (cols[2] || '').trim(),
          challanNo: (cols[3] || '').trim(),
          description: (cols[4] || '').trim(),
          quantity: (cols[5] || '').trim(),
          rate: (cols[6] || '').trim(),
          amount: (cols[7] || '').trim(),
