          challanDate: (cols[8] || '').trim(),
          recordId: (cols[9] || '').trim(),
          pdfUrl: (cols[10] || '').trim(),
          entryTime: (cols[11] || '').trim(),
        });
      }
    }

    if (parsedRows.length === 0) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: 'বৈধ কলাম ফরম্যাটে কোনো সারি পাওয়া যায়নি। Google Sheet থেকে সারি কপি করে পেস্ট করুন।',
      });
      return;
    }

    setImportBusy(true);
    setToolMessage(null);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch('/api/challans/import', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rows: parsedRows }),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        added?: number;
        skippedDuplicates?: number;
        skippedInvalid?: number;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে');
      }
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `ইমপোর্ট সম্পন্ন: নতুন যোগ ${json.added ?? 0}টি, ডুপ্লিকেট বাদ ${
          json.skippedDuplicates ?? 0
        }টি, অকার্যকর ${json.skippedInvalid ?? 0}টি।`,
      });
      setImportText('');
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ইমপোর্ট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setImportBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function exportCsv() {
    const headers = [
      'ক্রমিক নং',
      'id',
      'ইউনিয়নের নাম / ঠিকানা',
      'ডিলার / লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম',
      'dealerAllocationId',
      'খাত (category)',
      'ধরণ (নতুন/নবায়ন)',
      'লাইসেন্স নং',
      'চালান কোড (New37)',
      'প্রতিষ্ঠান (department)',
      'যার মাধ্যমে জমা',
      'যার পক্ষে প্রদত্ত',
      'মূল চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক)',
      'মূল চালান তারিখ',
      'পরিমাণ (মে. টন)',
      'দর',
      'প্রাপ্য / নির্ধারিত টাকা',
      'মূল টাকা',
      'আয়কর চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক)',
      'আয়কর তারিখ',
      'আয়কর টাকা',
      'ভ্যাট চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক)',
      'ভ্যাট তারিখ',
      'ভ্যাট টাকা',
      'ডিও নং',
      'ডিও তারিখ',
      'বিবরণ / মন্তব্য',
      'রেকর্ড আইডি',
      'এন্ট্রি সময়',
      ...customColumns.map((c) => c.label),
    ];

    const escapeCsv = (val: unknown) =>
      `"${String(val ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;

    const lines = [
      headers.map(escapeCsv).join(','),
      ...filteredRecords.map((r) =>
        [
          r.serialNo,
          r.id || '',
          r.unionName,
          r.dealerName,
          r.dealerAllocationId || '',
          r.challanCategory,
          r.licenseActionType || '',
          r.licenseNo || '',
          r.new37Code || '',
          r.department || r.toOrganization,
          r.submittedThrough,
          r.submittedOnBehalfOf,
          r.challanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(r.challanNo) : '',
          r.challanDate,
          r.quantity,
          r.rate,
          r.expectedAmount || '',
          r.amount,
          r.incomeTaxChallanNo
            ? normalizeChallanNo(r.incomeTaxChallanNo)
            : '',
          r.incomeTaxDate || '',
          r.incomeTaxAmount || '',
          r.vatChallanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(r.vatChallanNo) : '',
          r.vatDate || '',
          r.vatAmount || '',
          r.doNo || '',
          r.doIssueDate || '',
          r.description || r.comment || '',
          r.recordId,
          r.entryTime,
          ...customColumns.map((c) => r.customFields?.[c.id] || ''),
        ]
          .map(escapeCsv)
          .join(',')
      ),
    ];

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + lines.join('\r\n')], {
      type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;',
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'Challan_Records.csv';
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  async function handlePushRecordsToSheet(targetRecords: ChallanRecord[], singleRowId?: string) {
    if (singleRowId) {
      setPushingRowId(singleRowId);
    } else {
      setSheetPushBusy(true);
    }
    setToolMessage(null);
    setPendingPushRecords(null);
    try {
      const summary = await pushRecordsToGoogleSheet(settings, targetRecords);
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `Google Sheet পুশ সম্পন্ন (${summary.total}টি): ${summary.notes.join(' · ')}`,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setPendingPushRecords(targetRecords);
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Google Sheet পুশ ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setPushingRowId(null);
      setSheetPushBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSignInAndPushPending() {
    const target = pendingPushRecords || filteredRecords;
    if (target.length === 0) return;
    setSheetPushBusy(true);
    setToolMessage(null);
    try {
      if (!getGoogleAccessToken()) {
        await googleSignIn();
      }
      const effectiveSettings: AppSettings = {
        ...settings,
        syncMethod: settings.syncMethod === 'apps_script' ? 'both' : settings.syncMethod,
      };
      const summary = await pushRecordsToGoogleSheet(effectiveSettings, target);
      setPendingPushRecords(null);
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: `Google Sheet পুশ সম্পন্ন (${summary.total}টি): ${summary.notes.join(' · ')}`,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Google Sheet পুশ ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setSheetPushBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSaveEdit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!editingRecord) return;
    setEditSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch(`/api/challans/${editingRecord.recordId}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(editingRecord),
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        record?: ChallanRecord;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'আপডেট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে');
      }

      const extraNotes: string[] = [];
      const cap = getSheetPushCapability(settings);
      if (cap.anyReady && json.record) {
        try {
          const pushRes = await pushRecordsToGoogleSheet(settings, [json.record]);
          extraNotes.push(...pushRes.notes);
        } catch (err) {
          extraNotes.push(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'শিট পুশ ব্যর্থ');
        }
      }

      setEditingRecord(null);
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: ['চালান রেকর্ড আপডেট করা হয়েছে।', ...extraNotes].join(' · '),
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'আপডেট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।',
      });
    } finally {
      setEditSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deletingRecord) return;
    setDeleteBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiFetch(`/api/challans/${deletingRecord.recordId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
      });
      const json = (await res.json()) as {
        success: boolean;
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'মুছে ফেলা যায়নি');
      }
      setDeletingRecord(null);
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'success',
        text: 'রেকর্ড মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে এবং ক্রমিক নং পুনর্গঠন করা হয়েছে।',
      });
      onRefresh();
    } catch (err) {
      setToolMessage({
        tone: 'error',
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'মুছে ফেলা যায়নি।',
      });
    } finally {
      setDeleteBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
              সংরক্ষিত চালান রেকর্ড (Challan Ledger)
            </h2>
            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
              <span>মোট সারি: {filteredRecords.length}টি</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>পরিমাণ: {formatMoney(totals.quantity)} মে. টন</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>মূল: ৳ {formatMoney(totals.amount)}</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>আয়কর: ৳ {formatMoney(totals.taxAmount)}</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
              <span>ভ্যাট: ৳ {formatMoney(totals.vatAmount)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {canEdit && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setToolMessage(null);
                  setActiveTool(activeTool === 'dates' ? 'none' : 'dates');
                }}
                className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  activeTool === 'dates'
                    ? 'border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white'
                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <CalendarCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                তারিখ ঠিক করুন
              </button>
            )}

            {canImport && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setToolMessage(null);
                  setActiveTool(activeTool === 'import' ? 'none' : 'import');
                }}
                className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  activeTool === 'import'
                    ? 'border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white'
                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <FileSpreadsheet className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                Sheet ইমপোর্ট
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={openBackupsTool}
              className={`inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                activeTool === 'backups'
                  ? 'border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white'
                  : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              <Archive className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              ব্যাকআপ আর্কাইভ
            </button>

            {canEdit && (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={sheetPushBusy || filteredRecords.length === 0}
                onClick={() => void handlePushRecordsToSheet(filteredRecords)}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-teal-800 disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {sheetPushBusy ? (
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <CloudUpload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                )}
                শিটে পুশ ({filteredRecords.length})
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={exportCsv}
              disabled={filteredRecords.length === 0}
              className="inline-flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-50 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              CSV ডাউনলোড
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {activeTool === 'dates' && (
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                  চালান জমার তারিখ ফরম্যাট সংশোধন (dd/MM/yyyy)
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
                  টেক্সট বা yyyy-MM-dd তারিখগুলোকে dd/MM/yyyy ফরম্যাটে রূপান্তর
                  করে এবং পরিবর্তনের আগে ব্যাকআপে সংরক্ষণ করে।
                </p>
                <label className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={swapAmbiguous}
                    onChange={(e) => setSwapAmbiguous(e.target.checked)}
                    className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300"
                  />
                  আগে থেকে মাস/দিন উল্টে যাওয়া (MM/dd → dd/MM) তারিখও ঠিক করুন
                </label>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={dateFixBusy}
                onClick={runFixDates}
                className="rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-60 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                {dateFixBusy ? 'চলছে...' : 'তারিখ ঠিক করুন'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTool === 'import' && (
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
              Google Sheet (Challan A:R বা Challan_Records) থেকে সারি পেস্ট করে
              ইমপোর্ট
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
              Google Sheet থেকে সারিগুলো কপি (Ctrl+C) করে নিচে পেস্ট করুন। নতুন
              Challan (A:R) ও পুরোনো ১৬/১২ কলাম—সব ফরম্যাটই সমর্থিত।
            </p>
            <textarea
              rows={4}
              value={importText}
              onChange={(e) => setImportText(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Google Sheet থেকে কপি করা ট্যাব-সেপারেটেড (TSV) বা CSV ডেটা এখানে পেস্ট করুন..."
              className="mt-2.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-3 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <div className="mt-2.5 flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={importBusy}
                onClick={handleBulkImport}
                className="rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-60 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                {importBusy ? 'ইমপোর্ট হচ্ছে...' : 'ডেটাবেজে ইমপোর্ট করুন'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTool === 'backups' && (
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                ডুপ্লিকেট ব্যাকআপ (Challan_Duplicate_Backup) —{' '}
                {duplicateBackups.length}টি
              </h3>
              {duplicateBackups.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  এখনো কোনো ডুপ্লিকেট চালান মুছে ব্যাকআপ করা হয়নি।
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-2 max-h-48 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
                    <thead className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">আগের সারি</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">চালান নম্বর</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">ডিলার</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2 text-right">টাকা</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">মোছার সময়</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                      {duplicateBackups.map((b, i) => (
                        <tr key={`${b.recordId}-${i}`}>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">
                            {b.originalRowNo}
                          </td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">
                            {b.challanNo}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-3 py-2">{b.dealerName || '—'}</td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2 text-right">
                            {formatMoney(b.amount)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2 text-slate-500">
                            {new Date(b.deletedAt).toLocaleString('bn-BD')}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                তারিখ সংশোধন ব্যাকআপ (Challan_Date_Backup) —{' '}
                {dateBackups.length}টি
              </h3>
              {dateBackups.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  এখনো কোনো তারিখ পরিবর্তনের ব্যাকআপ নেই।
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-2 max-h-48 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white">
                  <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
                    <thead className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">সারি</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">চালান নম্বর</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">আগের মান</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2">নতুন মান</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                      {dateBackups.map((d, i) => (
                        <tr key={`${d.challanNo}-${i}`}>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">{d.rowNo}</td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">
                            {d.challanNo}
                          </td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">
                            {d.previousValue}
                          </td>
                          <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2">
                            {d.newValue}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {toolMessage && (
          <div
            role="status"
            className={`mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm ${
              toolMessage.tone === 'success'
                ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900'
                : toolMessage.tone === 'error'
                  ? 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-900'
                  : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              {toolMessage.tone === 'success' && (
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              )}
              <span>{toolMessage.text}</span>
            </div>
            {toolMessage.tone === 'error' && pendingPushRecords && (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={sheetPushBusy}
                onClick={() => void handleSignInAndPushPending()}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-teal-700 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-teal-800 disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer shrink-0"
              >
                {sheetPushBusy ? (
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <CloudUpload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                )}
                Google সাইন-ইন করে শিটে পুশ করুন
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div className="relative flex-1 md:max-w-md">
            <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="১১-অঙ্কের চালান, আয়কর চালান, ডিলার বা আইডি খুঁজুন..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-2 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <select
              value={categoryFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <option value="সব ক্যাটেগরি">সব খাত / ক্যাটেগরি</option>
              {categoryOptions.map((cat) => (
                <option key={cat} value={cat}>
                  {cat}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setGroupByDealer((prev) => !prev)}
              className={`inline-flex min-h-[38px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                groupByDealer
                  ? 'border-rose-700 bg-rose-50 text-rose-800'
                  : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {groupByDealer
                ? 'ডিলার অনুযায়ী একসাথে সাজানো'
                : 'ডিলার অনুযায়ী সাজান'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              রেকর্ড লোড হচ্ছে...
            </div>
          ) : filteredRecords.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-10 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                কোনো চালান রেকর্ড পাওয়া যায়নি
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                &ldquo;স্ক্যান ও এন্ট্রি&rdquo; থেকে নতুন চালান সংরক্ষণ করুন অথবা
                সেটিংস থেকে Google Sheet সিঙ্ক করুন।
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                    ক্রমিক
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    মূল চালান (Col H-L)
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    আয়কর ও ভ্যাট চালান
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    {hideDealerIds
                      ? 'ডিলার / লাইসেন্সধারী'
                      : 'ডিলার / লাইসেন্সধারী ও আইডি'}
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">খাত (Col D)</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                    পরিমাণ (মে. টন)
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                    টাকা (মূল / আয়কর / ভ্যাট)
                  </th>
                  {(canEdit || canDelete) && (
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                      অ্যাকশন
                    </th>
                  )}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
                {filteredRecords.map((r) => (
                  <tr
                    key={r.recordId}
                    className="transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50/80"
                  >
                    <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-center text-xs text-slate-600">
                      {r.serialNo}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {r.challanNo ? (
                        <div>
                          <a
                            href={getOfficialVerificationUrl(r.challanNo)}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="font-mono-num text-xs font-semibold text-teal-800 hover:underline"
                            title="সরকারি যাচাই পেজে দেখুন"
                          >
                            {normalizeChallanNo(r.challanNo)}
                          </a>
                          <div className="font-mono-num text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            {r.challanDate || r.date || '—'}
                          </div>
                          {r.doNo && (
                            <div className="font-mono-num mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-800">
                              ডিও: {r.doNo}
                              {r.doIssueDate ? ` (${r.doIssueDate})` : ''}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">—</span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-3 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap space-y-1">
                      {r.incomeTaxChallanNo ? (
                        <div>
                          <a
                            href={getOfficialVerificationUrl(
                              r.incomeTaxChallanNo
                            )}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="font-mono-num text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 hover:underline"
                            title="সরকারি যাচাই পেজে দেখুন"
                          >
                            আয়কর: {normalizeChallanNo(r.incomeTaxChallanNo)}
                          </a>
                          <div className="font-mono-num text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            {r.incomeTaxDate || '—'}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ) : null}
                      {r.vatChallanNo ? (
                        <div>
                          <a
                            href={getOfficialVerificationUrl(r.vatChallanNo)}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="font-mono-num text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-800 hover:underline"
                            title="সরকারি যাচাই পেজে দেখুন"
                          >
                            ভ্যাট: {normalizeChallanNo(r.vatChallanNo)}
                          </a>
                          <div className="font-mono-num text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            {r.vatDate || '—'}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ) : null}
                      {!r.incomeTaxChallanNo && !r.vatChallanNo && (
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">—</span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                      <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {r.dealerName || '—'}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                        {r.unionName || (!hideDealerIds ? '' : r.licenseNo ? '' : '—')}
                        {r.licenseNo && (
                          <span className="font-mono-num">
                            {r.unionName ? ' · ' : ''}লাইঃ {r.licenseNo}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {!hideDealerIds && !r.licenseNo && r.dealerAllocationId && (
                          <span className="font-mono-num">
                            {r.unionName ? ' · ' : ''}
                            {r.dealerAllocationId}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="max-w-[200px] px-3 py-2.5">
                      <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {r.challanCategory}
                        {r.licenseActionType ? ` (${r.licenseActionType})` : ''}
                      </div>
                      <div className="truncate text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                        {r.new37Code ? `কোড: ${r.new37Code} · ` : ''}
                        {r.department || r.toOrganization || '—'}
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-right text-xs text-slate-700">
                      {r.quantity ? formatMoney(r.quantity) : '0.00'}
                    </td>

                    <td className="font-mono-num px-3 py-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                      <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                        ৳ {formatMoney(r.amount)}
                      </div>
                      {toNumericValue(r.incomeTaxAmount) > 0 && (
                        <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-800">
                          আয়কর: ৳ {formatMoney(r.incomeTaxAmount)}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {toNumericValue(r.vatAmount) > 0 && (
                        <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-800">
                          ভ্যাট: ৳ {formatMoney(r.vatAmount)}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    {(canEdit || canDelete) && (
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          {canEdit && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={pushingRowId === r.recordId}
                              onClick={() => void handlePushRecordsToSheet([r], r.recordId)}
                              title="এই সারি Google Sheet-এ পুশ করুন"
                              aria-label="শিটে পুশ"
                              className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-teal-700 hover:bg-teal-50 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                              {pushingRowId === r.recordId ? (
                                <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                              ) : (
                                <CloudUpload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                              )}
                            </button>
                          )}
                          {canEdit && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setEditingRecord({ ...r })}
                              className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900"
                              aria-label="সম্পাদনা"
                            >
                              <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </button>
                          )}
                          {canDelete && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setDeletingRecord(r)}
                              className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-700"
                              aria-label="মুছে ফেলুন"
                            >
                              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    )}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {editingRecord && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
        >
          <form
            onSubmit={handleSaveEdit}
            className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                চালান রেকর্ড সম্পাদনা — ক্রমিক {editingRecord.serialNo}
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEditingRecord(null)}
                className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"
              >
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ইউনিয়নের নাম
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.unionName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      unionName: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ডিলারের নাম
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.dealerName}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      dealerName: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ডিলার / বরাদ্দ আইডি (Col G)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.dealerAllocationId || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      dealerAllocationId: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  খাত / ক্যাটেগরি (Col D)
                </label>
                <select
                  value={editingRecord.challanCategory}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      challanCategory: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                >
                  {categoryOptions.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c} value={c}>
                      {c}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  সরকারি প্রতিষ্ঠানের অনুকূলে জমা (Col C)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={
                    editingRecord.department || editingRecord.toOrganization
                  }
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      department: e.target.value,
                      toOrganization: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  মূল চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক — Col H)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.challanNo}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      challanNo: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  মূল চালানের তারিখ (Col I)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={10}
                  value={editingRecord.challanDate}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      challanDate: maskDateInput(e.target.value),
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  পরিমাণ (মে. টন — Col K)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.quantity}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      quantity: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  দর (Col J)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.rate}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({ ...editingRecord, rate: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  মূল টাকা (৳ — Col L)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.amount}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      amount: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  আয়কর চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক — Col M)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.incomeTaxChallanNo || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      incomeTaxChallanNo: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  আয়কর তারিখ (Col N)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={10}
                  value={editingRecord.incomeTaxDate || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      incomeTaxDate: maskDateInput(e.target.value),
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  আয়কর টাকা (৳ — Col O)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.incomeTaxAmount || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      incomeTaxAmount: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ভ্যাট চালান নম্বর (১১ অঙ্ক — Col S)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.vatChallanNo || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      vatChallanNo: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ভ্যাট তারিখ (Col T)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  maxLength={10}
                  value={editingRecord.vatDate || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      vatDate: maskDateInput(e.target.value),
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  ভ্যাট টাকা (৳ — Col U)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.vatAmount || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      vatAmount: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  লাইসেন্স নম্বর (Col V)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.licenseNo || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      licenseNo: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  চালান কোড / New37 (Col W)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.new37Code || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      new37Code: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  প্রাপ্য / নির্ধারিত টাকা (৳)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.expectedAmount || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      expectedAmount: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  প্রাপ্য আয়কর (৳)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={editingRecord.expectedTaxAmount || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      expectedTaxAmount: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                  বিবরণ / মন্তব্য
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={editingRecord.description}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setEditingRecord({
                      ...editingRecord,
                      description: e.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEditingRecord(null)}
                className="min-h-[42px] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                বাতিল
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={editSaving}
                className="min-h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {editSaving ? 'সংরক্ষণ হচ্ছে...' : 'পরিবর্তন সংরক্ষণ'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

      {deletingRecord && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-900/50 p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
        >
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
              রেকর্ড মুছে ফেলার নিশ্চিতকরণ
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
              আপনি কি নিশ্চিতভাবে চালান নম্বর{' '}
              <span className="font-mono-num font-semibold text-slate-900">
                {deletingRecord.challanNo || deletingRecord.incomeTaxChallanNo}
              </span>{' '}
              (ক্রমিক {deletingRecord.serialNo}) মুছে ফেলতে চান?
            </p>
            <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDeletingRecord(null)}
                className="min-h-[42px] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                বাতিল
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={deleteBusy}
                onClick={confirmDelete}
                className="min-h-[42px] rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {deleteBusy ? 'মুছছে...' : 'হ্যাঁ, মুছে ফেলুন'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ==================== END: src/components/RecordsView.tsx ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/components/ReportView.tsx ====================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type TouchEvent } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar,
  FileBadge2,
  FileDown,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Loader2,
  Printer,
  RectangleHorizontal,
  RectangleVertical,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
  Wheat,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  AppSettings,
  ChallanReportResponse,
  ChallanReportRow,
  KhatConfig,
} from '../types.ts';
import {
  CHALLAN_CATEGORIES,
  DEFAULT_LICENSE_FEES,
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  showDate,
  toBengaliDigits,
} from '../utils/challan.ts';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import { downloadNikoshPdf } from '../utils/pdfExport.ts';

interface ReportViewProps {
  unions: string[];
  khats?: KhatConfig[];
  settings?: AppSettings;
  recordsCount?: number;
  onSyncSheet?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  syncing?: boolean;
}

type RegisterType = 'grain' | 'license';
type PageOrientation = 'portrait' | 'landscape';

function getTodayIso(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  return `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(
    2,
    '0'
  )}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function getMonthStartIso(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  return `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(
    2,
    '0'
  )}-01`;
}

function getTodayFormatted(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  return `${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(
    now.getMonth() + 1
  ).padStart(2, '0')}/${now.getFullYear()}`;
}

function fmtBnAmount(val: number | undefined): string {
  const n = Number(val || 0);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n === 0) return '';
  const hasDecimals = Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) > 0.001;
  return toBengaliDigits(
    n.toLocaleString('en-US', {
      minimumFractionDigits: hasDecimals ? 2 : 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    })
  );
}

function fmtBnQty(val: number | undefined): string {
  const n = Number(val || 0);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n === 0) return '';
  return toBengaliDigits(
    n.toLocaleString('en-US', {
      minimumFractionDigits: Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) > 0.001 ? 2 : 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    })
  );
}

function formatGormil(expected: number, deposited: number): {
  text: string;
  shortfall: number;
} {
  if (expected <= 0) {
    return { text: '', shortfall: 0 };
  }
  const diff = Math.round((expected - deposited) * 100) / 100;
  if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.5) {
    return { text: '০', shortfall: 0 };
  }
  if (diff > 0) {
    return { text: fmtBnAmount(diff), shortfall: diff };
  }
  return { text: `বেশি ${fmtBnAmount(Math.abs(diff))}`, shortfall: diff };
}

interface RegisterSubRow {
  key: string;
  orgDesc: string;
  khatLabel: string;
  new37Code: string;
  qtyRateText: string;
  expectedNum: number;
  challanNo: string;
  challanDate: string;
  depositedNum: number;
  gormilText: string;
  shortfall: number;
  comment: string;
  canSign: boolean;
}

interface ChunkedPageRow {
  row: ChallanReportRow;
  globalIndex: number;
  subs: RegisterSubRow[];
}

export function ReportView({
  unions,
  khats = [],
  settings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  recordsCount,
  onSyncSheet,
  syncing = false,
}: ReportViewProps) {
  const [registerType, setRegisterType] = useState<RegisterType>('grain');
  const [orientation, setOrientation] = useState<PageOrientation>('portrait');
  const [datePreset, setDatePreset] = useState<
    'today' | 'thisMonth' | 'lastMonth' | 'all' | 'custom'
  >('thisMonth');
  const [startDateIso, setStartDateIso] = useState(getMonthStartIso);
  const [endDateIso, setEndDateIso] = useState(getTodayIso);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('সকল');
  const [actionType, setActionType] = useState('সকল');
  const [unionName, setUnionName] = useState('সব ইউনিয়ন');

  const [report, setReport] = useState<ChallanReportResponse | null>(null);
  const [appliedMeta, setAppliedMeta] = useState<{
    registerType: RegisterType;
    startISO: string;
    endISO: string;
    allDates?: boolean;
    category: string;
    actionType: string;
    unionName: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [pdfLoading, setPdfLoading] = useState(false);

  const printAreaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const startDateInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const endDateInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const touchStartXRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const userSelectedDateRef = useRef(false);
  const prevRecordsCountRef = useRef(recordsCount);

  const activeKhats = useMemo(() => {
    const list = khats.length > 0 ? khats : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.khats;
    return list.filter((k) => k.group === registerType);
  }, [khats, registerType]);

  const categoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const dynamicNames = activeKhats.map((k) => k.name);
    if (dynamicNames.length > 0) return dynamicNames;
    return [...CHALLAN_CATEGORIES];
  }, [activeKhats]);

  const feeLookupMap = useMemo(() => {
    const list =
      settings.licenseFees && settings.licenseFees.length > 0
        ? settings.licenseFees
        : DEFAULT_LICENSE_FEES;
    const map = new Map<string, (typeof list)[number]>();
    for (const f of list) {
      map.set(f.khatName.trim(), f);
    }
    return map;
  }, [settings.licenseFees]);

  const customCols = useMemo(() => {
    const cols = settings.customColumns || [];
    return cols.filter((c) =>
      registerType === 'grain'
        ? c.includeInGrainRegister
        : c.includeInLicenseRegister
    );
  }, [settings.customColumns, registerType]);

  function applyPreset(
    preset: 'today' | 'thisMonth' | 'lastMonth' | 'all',
    isUserAction = true
  ) {
    if (isUserAction) {
      userSelectedDateRef.current = true;
    }
    setDatePreset(preset);
    const now = new Date();
    const toIso = (d: Date) =>
      `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(
        d.getDate()
      ).padStart(2, '0')}`;

    let nextStart = startDateIso;
    let nextEnd = endDateIso;
    let isAll = false;

    if (preset === 'today') {
      const today = toIso(now);
      nextStart = today;
      nextEnd = today;
    } else if (preset === 'thisMonth') {
      const start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
      nextStart = toIso(start);
      nextEnd = toIso(now);
    } else if (preset === 'lastMonth') {
      const start = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1);
      const end = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0);
      nextStart = toIso(start);
      nextEnd = toIso(end);
    } else {
      nextStart = '2020-01-01';
      nextEnd = toIso(now);
      isAll = true;
    }

    setStartDateIso(nextStart);
    setEndDateIso(nextEnd);
    void runReport({
      startISO: nextStart,
      endISO: nextEnd,
      allDates: isAll,
    });
  }

  async function runReport(overrides?: {
    registerType?: RegisterType;
    startISO?: string;
    endISO?: string;
    category?: string;
    actionType?: string;
    unionName?: string;
    allDates?: boolean;
  }) {
    const effectiveType = overrides?.registerType ?? registerType;
    const effectiveCategory =
      overrides?.category !== undefined
        ? overrides.category
        : overrides?.registerType
          ? 'সকল'
          : category;
    const effectiveAction =
      overrides?.actionType !== undefined
        ? overrides.actionType
        : overrides?.registerType
          ? 'সকল'
          : actionType;
    const effectiveUnion = overrides?.unionName ?? unionName;
    const startISO = overrides?.startISO ?? startDateIso;
    const endISO = overrides?.endISO ?? endDateIso;
    const useAllDates = overrides?.allDates ?? datePreset === 'all';

    if (!useAllDates) {
      if (!startISO || !endISO) {
        setStatusText('তারিখ নির্বাচন করুন');
        return;
      }
      if (startISO > endISO) {
        setStatusText('শুরুর তারিখ বড় হতে পারবে না');
        return;
      }
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setStatusText('');
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({
        startDate: useAllDates ? 'ALL' : startISO,
        endDate: useAllDates ? 'ALL' : endISO,
        allDates: useAllDates ? 'true' : 'false',
        registerType: effectiveType,
        category:
          effectiveCategory === 'সকল' ? 'সব ক্যাটেগরি' : effectiveCategory,
        actionType:
          effectiveType === 'license' && effectiveAction !== 'সকল'
            ? effectiveAction
            : '',
        unionName: effectiveUnion,
      });
      const res = await apiFetch(`/api/report?${params.toString()}`);
      const json = (await res.json()) as ChallanReportResponse & {
        message?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
        throw new Error(json.message || 'রেজিস্টার তৈরি ব্যর্থ।');
      }

      if (
        json.rows.length === 0 &&
        (json.totalInGroup ?? 0) > 0 &&
        !useAllDates &&
        !userSelectedDateRef.current
      ) {
        const allParams = new URLSearchParams({
          startDate: 'ALL',
          endDate: 'ALL',
          allDates: 'true',
          registerType: effectiveType,
          category:
            effectiveCategory === 'সকল' ? 'সব ক্যাটেগরি' : effectiveCategory,
          actionType:
            effectiveType === 'license' && effectiveAction !== 'সকল'
              ? effectiveAction
              : '',
          unionName: effectiveUnion,
        });
        const allRes = await apiFetch(`/api/report?${allParams.toString()}`);
        const allJson = (await allRes.json()) as ChallanReportResponse & {
          message?: string;
        };
        if (allRes.ok && allJson.success && allJson.rows.length > 0) {
          const validDates = allJson.rows
            .map((r) => r.challanDate)
            .filter(Boolean)
            .sort();
          const minIso = validDates[0] || '2020-01-01';
          const maxIso = validDates[validDates.length - 1] || getTodayIso();
          setDatePreset('all');
          setStartDateIso(minIso);
          setEndDateIso(maxIso);
          setReport(allJson);
          setAppliedMeta({
            registerType: effectiveType,
            startISO: minIso,
            endISO: maxIso,
            allDates: true,
            category: effectiveCategory,
            actionType: effectiveAction,
            unionName: effectiveUnion,
          });
          setStatusText(`${toBengaliDigits(allJson.rows.length)}টি`);
          return;
        }
      }

      let displayStart = startISO;
      let displayEnd = endISO;
      if (useAllDates && json.rows.length > 0) {
        const validDates = json.rows
          .map((r) => r.challanDate)
          .filter(Boolean)
          .sort();
        if (validDates.length > 0) {
          displayStart = validDates[0];
          displayEnd = validDates[validDates.length - 1];
        }
      }

      setReport(json);
      setAppliedMeta({
        registerType: effectiveType,
        startISO: displayStart,
        endISO: displayEnd,
        allDates: useAllDates,
        category: effectiveCategory,
        actionType: effectiveAction,
        unionName: effectiveUnion,
      });
      setStatusText(`${toBengaliDigits(json.rows.length)}টি`);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusText(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    void runReport();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      recordsCount !== undefined &&
      recordsCount !== prevRecordsCountRef.current
    ) {
      prevRecordsCountRef.current = recordsCount;
      void runReport();
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [recordsCount]);

  function switchRegisterType(next: RegisterType) {
    if (next === registerType) return;
    setRegisterType(next);
    setCategory('সকল');
    setActionType('সকল');
    void runReport({
      registerType: next,
      category: 'সকল',
      actionType: 'সকল',
    });
  }

  function handleSwipeStart(e: TouchEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    touchStartXRef.current = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? null;
  }

  function handleSwipeEnd(e: TouchEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (touchStartXRef.current === null) return;
    const endX = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? touchStartXRef.current;
    const dx = endX - touchStartXRef.current;
    touchStartXRef.current = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 55) return;
    if (dx < 0 && registerType === 'grain') {
      switchRegisterType('license');
    } else if (dx > 0 && registerType === 'license') {
      switchRegisterType('grain');
    }
  }

  async function downloadPdf() {
    if (!report) return;
    const targetEl = printAreaRef.current;
    if (!targetEl) return;

    setPdfLoading(true);
    try {
      const fileTag =
        (appliedMeta?.registerType ?? registerType) === 'grain'
          ? 'Grain_Register'
          : 'License_Register';
      await downloadNikoshPdf(
        targetEl,
        `${fileTag}_${appliedMeta?.startISO || 'report'}.pdf`,
        orientation
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusText(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'PDF ব্যর্থ।');
    } finally {
      setPdfLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function downloadExcelCsv() {
    if (!report || !appliedMeta) return;
    const headers = [
      'ক্রমিক',
      'ডিলার/লাইসেন্সধারী',
      'ইউনিয়ন',
      'খাত',
      'প্রতিষ্ঠান ও বিবরণ',
      'অর্থনৈতিক কোড',
      'পরিমাণ (টন)',
      'দর',
      'প্রাপ্য টাকা',
      'চালান নং',
      'চালান তারিখ',
      'জমাকৃত টাকা',
      'গরমিল',
      'ডিও নং',
      'ডিও তারিখ',
      'মন্তব্য',
    ];
    const escapeCell = (v: unknown) => {
      const s = String(v ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""');
      return `"${s}"`;
    };
    const csvRows: string[] = [headers.map(escapeCell).join(',')];

    report.rows.forEach((row, idx) => {
      const subs =
        activeMode === 'grain'
          ? buildGrainSubRows(row)
          : buildLicenseSubRows(row);
      subs.forEach((sub, sIdx) => {
        csvRows.push(
          [
            sIdx === 0 ? idx + 1 : '',
            row.dealerName,
            row.unionName,
            sub.khatLabel,
            sub.orgDesc,
            sub.new37Code,
            sIdx === 0 ? row.quantity || '' : '',
            sIdx === 0 ? row.rate || '' : '',
            sub.expectedNum || '',
            sub.challanNo,
            sub.challanDate,
            sub.depositedNum || '',
            sub.gormilText,
            sIdx === 0 ? row.doNo || '' : '',
            sIdx === 0 ? row.doIssueDate || '' : '',
            sub.comment,
          ]
            .map(escapeCell)
            .join(',')
        );
      });
    });

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + csvRows.join('\n')], {
      type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;',
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `Challan_Register_${appliedMeta.startISO}_${appliedMeta.endISO}.csv`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  const signatureText =
    settings.officerSignatureText?.trim() || 'উপজেলা খাদ্য নিয়ন্ত্রক';
  const officerName = settings.officerName?.trim() || '';
  const signatureImage = settings.officerSignatureImageDataUrl?.trim() || '';

  function buildGrainSubRows(row: ChallanReportRow): RegisterSubRow[] {
    const subs: RegisterSubRow[] = [];
    const hasDistinctMain =
      Boolean(
        row.challanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.incomeTaxChallanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.vatChallanNo
      ) || row.amount > 0;
    const hasTax = Boolean(
      row.incomeTaxChallanNo || (row.incomeTaxAmount ?? 0) > 0
    );

    if (hasDistinctMain || !hasTax) {
      const calcExpected = row.expectedAmount > 0 ? row.expectedAmount : 0;
      const gormil = formatGormil(calcExpected, row.amount);
      const qtyLine = row.quantity ? fmtBnQty(row.quantity) : '';
      const rateLine = row.rate ? fmtBnAmount(row.rate) : '';
      const qtyRateText = [qtyLine, rateLine].filter(Boolean).join('\n');

      // গরমিল কলামে প্রাপ্য মূল টাকার চেয়ে জমা কম হলে স্বাক্ষর বসবে না
      const canSign =
        row.amount > 0 && (calcExpected <= 0 || gormil.shortfall <= 0);

      subs.push({
        key: `${row.recordId}-main`,
        orgDesc:
          [row.toOrganization, row.description].filter(Boolean).join(' - ') ||
          'খাদ্য অধিদপ্তর',
        khatLabel: row.category,
        new37Code: row.new37Code || '',
        qtyRateText,
        expectedNum: calcExpected,
        challanNo:
          row.challanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.incomeTaxChallanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.vatChallanNo
            ? row.challanNo
            : '',
        challanDate: showDate(row.challanDate),
        depositedNum: row.amount,
        gormilText: gormil.text,
        shortfall: gormil.shortfall,
        comment: row.comment || '',
        canSign,
      });
    }

    if (hasTax) {
      const taxAmt = Number(row.incomeTaxAmount || 0);
      const expectedTax = Number(row.expectedTaxAmount || 0);
      const gormil = formatGormil(expectedTax, taxAmt);
      const canSign =
        taxAmt > 0 && (expectedTax <= 0 || gormil.shortfall <= 0);

      subs.push({
        key: `${row.recordId}-tax`,
        orgDesc: 'কর অঞ্চল, বরিশাল — আয়কর',
        khatLabel: 'আয়কর',
        new37Code: row.new37Code || '',
        qtyRateText: row.quantity ? fmtBnQty(row.quantity) : '',
        expectedNum: expectedTax,
        challanNo: row.incomeTaxChallanNo || '',
        challanDate: row.incomeTaxDate || showDate(row.challanDate),
        depositedNum: taxAmt,
        gormilText: gormil.text,
        shortfall: gormil.shortfall,
        comment: '',
        canSign,
      });
    }

    return subs;
  }

  function buildLicenseSubRows(row: ChallanReportRow): RegisterSubRow[] {
    const subs: RegisterSubRow[] = [];
    const fee = feeLookupMap.get(row.category.trim());
    const actionLabel = row.licenseActionType || 'নতুন';

    const hasDistinctMain =
      Boolean(
        row.challanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.incomeTaxChallanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.vatChallanNo
      ) || row.amount > 0;
    const hasTax = Boolean(
      row.incomeTaxChallanNo || (row.incomeTaxAmount ?? 0) > 0
    );
    const hasVat = Boolean(row.vatChallanNo || (row.vatAmount ?? 0) > 0);

    if (hasDistinctMain || (!hasTax && !hasVat)) {
      const feeFromSettings =
        actionLabel === 'নবায়ন' ? fee?.renewalFee || 0 : fee?.newFee || 0;
      const expectedFee =
        row.expectedAmount > 0 ? row.expectedAmount : feeFromSettings;
      const gormil = formatGormil(expectedFee, row.amount);
      const canSign =
        row.amount > 0 && (expectedFee <= 0 || gormil.shortfall <= 0);

      subs.push({
        key: `${row.recordId}-lic-main`,
        orgDesc: row.toOrganization || row.category,
        khatLabel: `${row.category}${actionLabel ? ` (${actionLabel})` : ''}`,
        new37Code: row.new37Code || '',
        qtyRateText: '',
        expectedNum: expectedFee,
        challanNo:
          row.challanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.incomeTaxChallanNo &&
          row.challanNo !== row.vatChallanNo
            ? row.challanNo
            : '',
        challanDate: showDate(row.challanDate),
        depositedNum: row.amount,
        gormilText: gormil.text,
        shortfall: gormil.shortfall,
        comment: row.comment || '',
        canSign,
      });
    }

    if (hasTax) {
      const taxAmt = Number(row.incomeTaxAmount || 0);
      const expectedTax =
        row.expectedTaxAmount > 0 ? row.expectedTaxAmount : fee?.taxAmount || 0;
      const gormil = formatGormil(expectedTax, taxAmt);
      subs.push({
        key: `${row.recordId}-lic-tax`,
        orgDesc: 'আয়কর',
        khatLabel: 'আয়কর',
        new37Code: row.new37Code || '',
        qtyRateText: '',
        expectedNum: expectedTax,
        challanNo: row.incomeTaxChallanNo || '',
        challanDate: row.incomeTaxDate || showDate(row.challanDate),
        depositedNum: taxAmt,
        gormilText: gormil.text,
        shortfall: gormil.shortfall,
        comment: '',
        canSign: taxAmt > 0 && (expectedTax <= 0 || gormil.shortfall <= 0),
      });
    }

    if (hasVat) {
      const vatAmt = Number(row.vatAmount || 0);
      const expectedVat = fee?.vatAmount || 0;
      const gormil = formatGormil(expectedVat, vatAmt);
      subs.push({
        key: `${row.recordId}-lic-vat`,
        orgDesc: 'ভ্যাট',
        khatLabel: 'ভ্যাট',
        new37Code: row.new37Code || '',
        qtyRateText: '',
        expectedNum: expectedVat,
        challanNo: row.vatChallanNo || '',
        challanDate: row.vatDate || showDate(row.challanDate),
        depositedNum: vatAmt,
        gormilText: gormil.text,
        shortfall: gormil.shortfall,
        comment: '',
        canSign: vatAmt > 0 && (expectedVat <= 0 || gormil.shortfall <= 0),
      });
    }

    return subs;
  }

  const activeMode = appliedMeta?.registerType ?? registerType;

  // Height-aware pagination: fill each A4 page cleanly down to just above the footer
  // so there is zero wasted blank whitespace on pages.
  const { allPreparedRows, pages, registerTotals } = useMemo(() => {
    if (!report) {
      return {
        allPreparedRows: [] as ChunkedPageRow[],
        pages: [] as ChunkedPageRow[][],
        registerTotals: {
          expectedTotal: 0,
          depositedTotal: 0,
          gormilTotal: 0,
          qtyTotal: 0,
        },
      };
    }

    let expectedTotal = 0;
    let depositedTotal = 0;
    let qtyTotal = 0;

    const prepared: ChunkedPageRow[] = report.rows.map((row, idx) => {
      qtyTotal += row.quantity || 0;
      const subs =
        activeMode === 'grain'
          ? buildGrainSubRows(row)
          : buildLicenseSubRows(row);
      for (const s of subs) {
        expectedTotal += s.expectedNum;
        depositedTotal += s.depositedNum;
      }
      return { row, globalIndex: idx, subs };
    });

    const gormilTotal = Math.max(0, expectedTotal - depositedTotal);

    if (prepared.length === 0) {
      return {
        allPreparedRows: [] as ChunkedPageRow[],
        pages: [[]] as ChunkedPageRow[][],
        registerTotals: { expectedTotal, depositedTotal, gormilTotal, qtyTotal },
      };
    }

    // Available table body height inside A4 after header (~72px), thead (~34px), footer (~68px), padding (~58px)
    const maxHeightBudget = orientation === 'portrait' ? 810 : 495;
    const descChars = orientation === 'portrait' ? 16 : 26;
    const dealerChars = orientation === 'portrait' ? 13 : 21;
    const commentChars = orientation === 'portrait' ? 9 : 15;
    const challanChars = orientation === 'portrait' ? 14 : 20;

    const estimateItemPx = (item: ChunkedPageRow): number => {
      const dealerLines =
        Math.max(1, Math.ceil((item.row.dealerName?.length || 0) / dealerChars)) +
        (item.row.unionName
          ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(item.row.unionName.length / dealerChars))
          : 0) +
        (item.row.licenseNo ? 1 : 0);
      const doLines =
        (item.row.doNo ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(item.row.doNo.length / 10)) : 0) +
        (item.row.doIssueDate ? 1 : 0);

      let total = 0;
      for (const s of item.subs) {
        const descLines =
          Math.max(1, Math.ceil((s.orgDesc?.length || 0) / descChars)) +
          (s.new37Code ? 1 : 0);
        const commentLines = Math.max(
          1,
          Math.ceil((s.comment?.length || 0) / commentChars)
        );
        const challanLines =
          Math.max(1, Math.ceil((s.challanNo?.length || 0) / challanChars)) +
          (s.challanDate ? 1 : 0);
        const lines = Math.max(
          descLines,
          commentLines,
          challanLines,
          s.qtyRateText ? 2 : 1,
          2
        );
        total += Math.max(34, lines * 14.5 + 10);
      }

      const spanMinHeight = Math.max(dealerLines, doLines) * 14 + 10;
      return Math.max(total, spanMinHeight);
    };

    const chunked: ChunkedPageRow[][] = [];
    let currentPage: ChunkedPageRow[] = [];
    let currentPx = 0;

    for (let idx = 0; idx < prepared.length; idx++) {
      const item = prepared[idx];
      const itemPx = estimateItemPx(item);
      const isLastItem = idx === prepared.length - 1;
      const totalsReserve = isLastItem ? 42 : 0;

      if (
        currentPage.length > 0 &&
        currentPx + itemPx + totalsReserve > maxHeightBudget
      ) {
        chunked.push(currentPage);
        currentPage = [item];
        currentPx = itemPx;
      } else {
        currentPage.push(item);
        currentPx += itemPx;
      }
    }
    if (currentPage.length > 0) {
      chunked.push(currentPage);
    }

    return {
      allPreparedRows: prepared,
      pages: chunked,
      registerTotals: { expectedTotal, depositedTotal, gormilTotal, qtyTotal },
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [report, activeMode, orientation, feeLookupMap, signatureText]);

  const showLicenseDoColumn = useMemo(
    () => Boolean(report?.rows.some((r) => Boolean(r.doNo))),
    [report]
  );

  // Column order: ... মন্তব্য | [CustomCols] | ডিও নং ও তারিখ | স্বাক্ষর (LAST)
  // Normalize column widths so sum ALWAYS equals strictly 100% (prevents border overflow)
  const grainWidths = useMemo(() => {
    const base = [4, 12.5, 15, 7, 7, 7.5, 12, 8, 5.5, 7.5]; // 86.0 (cols 0..9)
    const customWeights = customCols.map(() => 5.5);
    const all = [...base, ...customWeights, 8, 6]; // DO = 8, Signature = 6
    const total = all.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    return all.map((w) => `${((w / total) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
  }, [customCols]);

  const licenseWidths = useMemo(() => {
    const base = [4.5, 15.5, 10.5, 10, 13.5, 9, 9.5, 7, 7.5]; // 87.0 (cols 0..8)
    const customWeights = customCols.map(() => 6);
    const doWeight = showLicenseDoColumn ? [8.5] : [];
    const all = [...base, ...customWeights, ...doWeight, 7.5]; // Signature = 7.5 (LAST)
    const total = all.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    return all.map((w) => `${((w / total) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
  }, [customCols, showLicenseDoColumn]);

  const cellFontPx = orientation === 'portrait' ? '10px' : '11px';
  const cellPad = orientation === 'portrait' ? '2.5px 3px' : '3px 4.5px';
  const cellClipStyle = {
    overflow: 'hidden' as const,
    overflowWrap: 'anywhere' as const,
    wordBreak: 'break-word' as const,
  };

  function formatShortChipDate(iso: string): string {
    if (!iso) return 'তারিখ';
    const parts = iso.split('-');
    if (parts.length !== 3) return toBengaliDigits(iso);
    return toBengaliDigits(`${parts[2]}/${parts[1]}/${parts[0].slice(2)}`);
  }

  function renderSignatureCell(sub: RegisterSubRow) {
    if (!sub.canSign) return null;
    if (signatureImage) {
      return (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <img
            src={signatureImage}
            alt="স্বাক্ষর"
            style={{
              height: '20px',
              maxWidth: '58px',
              objectFit: 'contain',
              display: 'block',
            }}
          />
          <span style={{ fontSize: '8px', lineHeight: 1.1 }}>{signatureText}</span>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return <span>{signatureText}</span>;
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="space-y-4 pb-20"
      onTouchStart={handleSwipeStart}
      onTouchEnd={handleSwipeEnd}
    >
      {/* Dynamic Print Page Orientation Rule */}
      <style>{`@media print { @page { size: A4 ${orientation}; margin: 7.5mm; } }`}</style>

      {/* Compact Single-Row Controls */}
      <section className="no-print rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-2.5 sm:p-3 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1 w-full sm:w-auto">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => switchRegisterType('grain')}
              className={`inline-flex min-h-[34px] items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                registerType === 'grain'
                  ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              <Wheat className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              খাদ্যশস্য
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => switchRegisterType('license')}
              className={`inline-flex min-h-[34px] items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                registerType === 'license'
