                  ? 'bg-teal-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              <FileBadge2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              লাইসেন্স
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => applyPreset('today')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                datePreset === 'today'
                  ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              আজ
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => applyPreset('thisMonth')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                datePreset === 'thisMonth'
                  ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              এই মাস
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => applyPreset('lastMonth')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                datePreset === 'lastMonth'
                  ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              গত মাস
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => applyPreset('all')}
              className={`rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                datePreset === 'all'
                  ? 'bg-teal-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              সব তারিখ
            </button>
            {statusText && (
              <span className="px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-700 tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">
                {statusText}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Single-Row Symbolic Filter Bar: Start Date | End Date | Khat | Union/Action | Search */}
        <div className="flex flex-nowrap items-center gap-1 sm:gap-2">
          {/* Symbolic Compact Start Date */}
          <div
            onClick={() => {
              const el = startDateInputRef.current;
              if (el && 'showPicker' in el && typeof el.showPicker === 'function') {
                try {
                  el.showPicker();
                } catch {
                  el.focus();
                }
              }
            }}
            title="শুরুর তারিখ"
            className="relative flex h-9 flex-1 min-w-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 hover:border-slate-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-rose-700" />
            <span className="truncate tabular-nums">
              {datePreset === 'all' ? 'শুরু হতে' : formatShortChipDate(startDateIso)}
            </span>
            <input
              ref={startDateInputRef}
              id="reportStartDate"
              type="date"
              aria-label="শুরুর তারিখ"
              value={startDateIso}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const next = e.target.value;
                userSelectedDateRef.current = true;
                setDatePreset('custom');
                setStartDateIso(next);
                if (next && endDateIso && next <= endDateIso) {
                  void runReport({
                    startISO: next,
                    endISO: endDateIso,
                    allDates: false,
                  });
                }
              }}
              className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full opacity-0 cursor-pointer"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Symbolic Compact End Date */}
          <div
            onClick={() => {
              const el = endDateInputRef.current;
              if (el && 'showPicker' in el && typeof el.showPicker === 'function') {
                try {
                  el.showPicker();
                } catch {
                  el.focus();
                }
              }
            }}
            title="শেষ তারিখ"
            className="relative flex h-9 flex-1 min-w-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-800 hover:border-slate-500 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-teal-700" />
            <span className="truncate tabular-nums">
              {datePreset === 'all' ? 'সকল' : formatShortChipDate(endDateIso)}
            </span>
            <input
              ref={endDateInputRef}
              id="reportEndDate"
              type="date"
              aria-label="শেষ তারিখ"
              value={endDateIso}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const next = e.target.value;
                userSelectedDateRef.current = true;
                setDatePreset('custom');
                setEndDateIso(next);
                if (startDateIso && next && startDateIso <= next) {
                  void runReport({
                    startISO: startDateIso,
                    endISO: next,
                    allDates: false,
                  });
                }
              }}
              className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full opacity-0 cursor-pointer"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Khat Select */}
          <select
            id="reportCategory"
            aria-label="খাত"
            value={category}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const next = e.target.value;
              setCategory(next);
              void runReport({ category: next });
            }}
            className="h-9 flex-1 min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none truncate"
          >
            <option value="সকল">সকল খাত</option>
            {categoryOptions.map((c) => (
              <option key={c} value={c}>
                {c}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {/* Union or License Action Select */}
          {registerType === 'license' ? (
            <select
              id="reportActionType"
              aria-label="ধরণ"
              value={actionType}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const next = e.target.value;
                setActionType(next);
                void runReport({ actionType: next });
              }}
              className="h-9 flex-1 min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none truncate"
            >
              <option value="সকল">সব ধরণ</option>
              <option value="নতুন">নতুন</option>
              <option value="নবায়ন">নবায়ন</option>
              <option value="জরিমানা">জরিমানা</option>
              <option value="অন্যান্য">অন্যান্য</option>
            </select>
          ) : (
            <select
              id="reportUnion"
              aria-label="ইউনিয়ন"
              value={unionName}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const next = e.target.value;
                setUnionName(next);
                void runReport({ unionName: next });
              }}
              className="h-9 flex-1 min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-900 focus:border-slate-900 focus:outline-none truncate"
            >
              <option value="সব ইউনিয়ন">সব ইউনিয়ন</option>
              {unions.map((u) => (
                <option key={u} value={u}>
                  {u}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}

          {/* Symbolic Search Button on Same Line */}
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={loading}
            onClick={() => void runReport()}
            title="সার্চ / রিপোর্ট দেখুন"
            aria-label="সার্চ"
            className="inline-flex h-9 w-9 sm:w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-teal-700 text-white hover:bg-teal-800 disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Helpful Empty-State Actions (Full-size Touch Controls Above A4 Preview) */}
      {report && appliedMeta && report.rows.length === 0 && (
        <section className="no-print rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-center space-y-2.5">
          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-950">
            {(report.totalInGroup ?? 0) > 0
              ? `এই ফিল্টারে কোনো ${
                  activeMode === 'grain' ? 'খাদ্যশস্য' : 'লাইসেন্স'
                } চালান নেই, তবে মোট ${toBengaliDigits(
                  report.totalInGroup ?? 0
                )}টি চালান সংরক্ষিত আছে।`
              : `এই সময়ে কোনো ${
                  activeMode === 'grain' ? 'খাদ্যশস্য' : 'লাইসেন্স বা অন্যান্য'
                } চালান পাওয়া যায়নি।`}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
            {(report.totalInGroup ?? 0) > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setCategory('সকল');
                  setActionType('সকল');
                  setUnionName('সব ইউনিয়ন');
                  userSelectedDateRef.current = true;
                  setDatePreset('all');
                  setStartDateIso('2020-01-01');
                  const today = getTodayIso();
                  setEndDateIso(today);
                  void runReport({
                    category: 'সকল',
                    actionType: 'সকল',
                    unionName: 'সব ইউনিয়ন',
                    startISO: '2020-01-01',
                    endISO: today,
                    allDates: true,
                  });
                }}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[42px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-teal-700 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-teal-800 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                সব তারিখের {toBengaliDigits(report.totalInGroup ?? 0)}টি চালান দেখুন
              </button>
            )}
            {onSyncSheet && (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={syncing}
                onClick={() => void onSyncSheet()}
                className="inline-flex min-h-[42px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-60 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                <RefreshCw
                  className={`h-4 w-4 ${syncing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
                />
                শিট থেকে সিঙ্ক করুন
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Live Normal Page Tabular Report View (Web View) */}
      {report && appliedMeta && (
        <section className="no-print rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-2xs overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2.5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80 px-3.5 py-2.5 sm:px-4">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 tabular-nums">
              {activeMode === 'grain' ? 'খাদ্যশস্য' : 'লাইসেন্স'}
              <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
              {toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি চালান
              {registerTotals.depositedTotal > 0 && (
                <>
                  <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                  মোট জমা {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.depositedTotal)} টাকা
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <div
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white p-0.5"
                role="group"
                aria-label="PDF পেজ ওরিয়েন্টেশন"
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOrientation('portrait')}
                  className={`inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                    orientation === 'portrait'
                      ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                      : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  <RectangleVertical className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  পোর্ট্রেট
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setOrientation('landscape')}
                  className={`inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                    orientation === 'landscape'
                      ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                      : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  <RectangleHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  ল্যান্ডস্কেপ
                </button>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={pdfLoading}
                onClick={() => void downloadPdf()}
                className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-700 px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-rose-800 disabled:opacity-60 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {pdfLoading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <FileDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                )}
                PDF ডাউনলোড
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => window.print()}
                className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-slate-800 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Printer className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                প্রিন্ট
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="px-3 pt-4 pb-2 text-center text-slate-950 sm:px-5">
            <div className="text-base font-bold leading-snug">
              গণপ্রজাতন্ত্রী বাংলাদেশ সরকার
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
              {settings.officeSubtitle || 'উপজেলা খাদ্য নিয়ন্ত্রকের কার্যালয়'},{' '}
              {settings.officeTitle || 'উজিরপুর, বরিশাল'}।
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
              চালান রেজিস্টার —{' '}
              {activeMode === 'grain'
                ? appliedMeta.category === 'সকল'
                  ? 'খাদ্যশস্য'
                  : appliedMeta.category
                : appliedMeta.category === 'সকল'
                ? 'লাইসেন্স ও অন্যান্য'
                : appliedMeta.category}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
              তারিখ: {toBengaliDigits(showDate(appliedMeta.startISO))} হতে{' '}
              {toBengaliDigits(showDate(appliedMeta.endISO))} পর্যন্ত
              {appliedMeta.unionName !== 'সব ইউনিয়ন'
                ? ` · ইউনিয়ন: ${appliedMeta.unionName}`
                : ''}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto px-2 pb-4 sm:px-4">
            {activeMode === 'grain' ? (
              <table className="web-register-table min-w-[820px] text-[12px]">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[0], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      ক্রমিক
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[1] }}>ইউনিয়ন ও ডিলার</th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[2] }}>প্রতিষ্ঠান ও বিবরণ</th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[3], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      খাত
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[4], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      পরিমাণ ও দর
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[5], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      প্রাপ্য টাকা
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[6], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      চালান নং ও তারিখ
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[7], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      জমাকৃত টাকা
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[8], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      গরমিল
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: grainWidths[9] }}>মন্তব্য</th>
                    {customCols.map((col, cIdx) => (
                      <th
                        key={`web-h-${col.id}`}
                        style={{
                          width: grainWidths[10 + cIdx],
                          textAlign: 'center',
                        }}
                      >
                        {col.label}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                    <th
                      style={{
                        width: grainWidths[10 + customCols.length],
                        textAlign: 'center',
                      }}
                    >
                      ডিও নং ও তারিখ
                    </th>
                    <th
                      style={{
                        width: grainWidths[11 + customCols.length],
                        textAlign: 'center',
                      }}
                    >
                      স্বাক্ষর
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {allPreparedRows.length === 0 ? (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={12 + customCols.length}
                        className="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500"
                      >
                        এই সময়ে কোনো খাদ্যশস্য চালান পাওয়া যায়নি।
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : (
                    allPreparedRows.flatMap(({ row, globalIndex, subs }) =>
                      subs.map((sub, subIdx) => (
                        <tr key={`web-${sub.key}`}>
                          {subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td
                              rowSpan={subs.length}
                              className="text-center font-bold tabular-nums"
                            >
                              {toBengaliDigits(globalIndex + 1)}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          {subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td rowSpan={subs.length}>
                              <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                                {row.dealerName || '—'}
                              </div>
                              {row.unionName && (
                                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                  {row.unionName}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          <td>
                            <div>{sub.orgDesc}</div>
                            {sub.new37Code && (
                              <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">
                                কোড: {toBengaliDigits(sub.new37Code)}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-center">{sub.khatLabel}</td>
                          <td className="text-center whitespace-pre-line tabular-nums">
                            {sub.qtyRateText}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right tabular-nums">
                            {fmtBnAmount(sub.expectedNum)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-center tabular-nums">
                            <div className="font-semibold">
                              {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanNo)}
                            </div>
                            {sub.challanDate && (
                              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanDate)}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right font-bold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
                            {fmtBnAmount(sub.depositedNum)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right tabular-nums">
                            {sub.gormilText}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-[11px] text-slate-700">
                            {sub.comment}
                          </td>
                          {customCols.map((col) => (
                            <td
                              key={`web-${sub.key}-${col.id}`}
                              className="text-center"
                            >
                              {subIdx === 0
                                ? toBengaliDigits(row.customFields?.[col.id] || '')
                                : ''}
                            </td>
                          ))}
                          {subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td rowSpan={subs.length} className="text-center tabular-nums">
                              {row.doNo ? (
                                <div>
                                  <div className="font-bold">
                                    {toBengaliDigits(row.doNo)}
                                  </div>
                                  {row.doIssueDate && (
                                    <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                      {toBengaliDigits(row.doIssueDate)}
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                ''
                              )}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          <td className="text-center text-[10.5px]">
                            {renderSignatureCell(sub)}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))
                    )
                  )}
                  {report.rows.length > 0 && (
                    <tr className="bg-slate-100 font-bold">
                      <th colSpan={4} className="text-right">
                        মোট ({toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি)
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-center tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnQty(registerTotals.qtyTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.expectedTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th />
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.depositedTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.gormilTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th />
                      {customCols.map((col) => (
                        <th key={`web-foot-${col.id}`} />
                      ))}
                      <th />
                      <th />
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            ) : (
              <table className="web-register-table min-w-[780px] text-[12px]">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[0], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      ক্রমিক
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[1] }}>
                      লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম ও ঠিকানা
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[2], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      জমা কোড
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[3], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      খাত
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[4], textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      চালান নং ও তারিখ
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[5], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      সরকারি মূল্য
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[6], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      জমাকৃত টাকা
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[7], textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      গরমিল
                    </th>
                    <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[8] }}>মন্তব্য</th>
                    {customCols.map((col, cIdx) => (
                      <th
                        key={`web-lh-${col.id}`}
                        style={{
                          width: licenseWidths[9 + cIdx],
                          textAlign: 'center',
                        }}
                      >
                        {col.label}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                    {showLicenseDoColumn && (
                      <th
                        style={{
                          width: licenseWidths[9 + customCols.length],
                          textAlign: 'center',
                        }}
                      >
                        ডিও নং ও তারিখ
                      </th>
                    )}
                    <th
                      style={{
                        width:
                          licenseWidths[
                            9 + customCols.length + (showLicenseDoColumn ? 1 : 0)
                          ],
                        textAlign: 'center',
                      }}
                    >
                      স্বাক্ষর
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {allPreparedRows.length === 0 ? (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={
                          10 + customCols.length + (showLicenseDoColumn ? 1 : 0)
                        }
                        className="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500"
                      >
                        এই সময়ে কোনো লাইসেন্স বা অন্যান্য চালান পাওয়া যায়নি।
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : (
                    allPreparedRows.flatMap(({ row, globalIndex, subs }) =>
                      subs.map((sub, subIdx) => (
                        <tr key={`web-l-${sub.key}`}>
                          {subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td
                              rowSpan={subs.length}
                              className="text-center font-bold tabular-nums"
                            >
                              {toBengaliDigits(globalIndex + 1)}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          {subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td rowSpan={subs.length}>
                              <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                                {row.dealerName || '—'}
                              </div>

                                  লাইঃ {toBengaliDigits(row.licenseNo)}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          <td className="text-center text-[11px] tabular-nums">
                            {toBengaliDigits(sub.new37Code || '—')}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-center">{sub.khatLabel}</td>
                          <td className="text-center tabular-nums">
                            <div className="font-semibold">
                              {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanNo)}
                            </div>
                            {sub.challanDate && (
                              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanDate)}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right tabular-nums">
                            {fmtBnAmount(sub.expectedNum)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right font-bold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
                            {fmtBnAmount(sub.depositedNum)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-right tabular-nums">
                            {sub.gormilText}
                          </td>
                          <td className="text-[11px] text-slate-700">
                            {sub.comment}
                          </td>
                          {customCols.map((col) => (
                            <td
                              key={`web-l-${sub.key}-${col.id}`}
                              className="text-center"
                            >
                              {subIdx === 0
                                ? toBengaliDigits(row.customFields?.[col.id] || '')
                                : ''}
                            </td>
                          ))}
                          {showLicenseDoColumn && subIdx === 0 && (
                            <td rowSpan={subs.length} className="text-center tabular-nums">
                              {row.doNo ? (
                                <div>
                                  <div className="font-bold">
                                    {toBengaliDigits(row.doNo)}
                                  </div>
                                  {row.doIssueDate && (
                                    <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                      {toBengaliDigits(row.doIssueDate)}
                                    </div>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              ) : (
                                ''
                              )}
                            </td>
                          )}
                          <td className="text-center text-[10.5px]">
                            {renderSignatureCell(sub)}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))
                    )
                  )}
                  {report.rows.length > 0 && (
                    <tr className="bg-slate-100 font-bold">
                      <th colSpan={5} className="text-right">
                        মোট ({toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি)
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.expectedTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.depositedTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th className="text-right tabular-nums">
                        {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.gormilTotal)}
                      </th>
                      <th />
                      {customCols.map((col) => (
                        <th key={`web-lfoot-${col.id}`} />
                      ))}
                      {showLicenseDoColumn && <th />}
                      <th />
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 border-t border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <div>
              মোট {toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি চালান · আপডেট:{' '}
              {toBengaliDigits(getTodayFormatted())}
            </div>
            <div className="text-center min-w-[150px]">
              {signatureImage && (
                <img
                  src={signatureImage}
                  alt="স্বাক্ষর"
                  className="mx-auto mb-1 h-8 max-w-[130px] object-contain"
                />
              )}
              <div
                className={`pt-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 ${
                  signatureImage
                    ? 'border-t border-slate-500'
                    : 'border-t border-dashed border-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {officerName || signatureText}
              </div>
              {officerName && (
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">{signatureText}</div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Off-Screen 1:1 Paginated A4 Sheets Used for Clean PDF Download & Print */}
      {report && appliedMeta && (
        <div className="a4-pdf-staging">
          <div
            id="challanReportPrintArea"
            ref={printAreaRef}
            className="font-nikosh"
          >
            {pages.map((pageRows, pageIdx) => {
              const isLastPage = pageIdx === pages.length - 1;
              return (
                <div
                  key={`a4-page-${pageIdx}`}
                  className={`a4-sheet-page font-nikosh ${
                    orientation === 'portrait'
                      ? 'a4-page-portrait'
                      : 'a4-page-landscape'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="a4-page-content">
                    {/* Repeating Official 4-Line Header on EVERY page */}
                    <div
                      style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        marginBottom: orientation === 'portrait' ? '6px' : '8px',
                        color: '#000000',
                      }}
                    >
                      <div
                        style={{
                          fontSize: orientation === 'portrait' ? '14.5px' : '16px',
                          fontWeight: 'bold',
                          lineHeight: 1.25,
                        }}
                      >
                        গণপ্রজাতন্ত্রী বাংলাদেশ সরকার
                      </div>
                      <div
                        style={{
                          fontSize: orientation === 'portrait' ? '12.5px' : '13.5px',
                          fontWeight: 'bold',
                          lineHeight: 1.25,
                        }}
                      >
                        {settings.officeSubtitle || 'উপজেলা খাদ্য নিয়ন্ত্রকের কার্যালয়'},{' '}
                        {settings.officeTitle || 'উজিরপুর, বরিশাল'}।
                      </div>
                      <div
                        style={{
                          fontSize: orientation === 'portrait' ? '13.5px' : '15px',
                          fontWeight: 'bold',
                          marginTop: '1px',
                          lineHeight: 1.25,
                        }}
                      >
                        চালান রেজিস্টার —{' '}
                        {activeMode === 'grain'
                          ? appliedMeta.category === 'সকল'
                            ? 'খাদ্যশস্য'
                            : appliedMeta.category
                          : appliedMeta.category === 'সকল'
                          ? 'লাইসেন্স ও অন্যান্য'
                          : appliedMeta.category}
                      </div>
                      <div
                        style={{
                          fontSize: orientation === 'portrait' ? '11px' : '12px',
                          marginTop: '1px',
                        }}
                      >
                        তারিখ: {toBengaliDigits(showDate(appliedMeta.startISO))} হতে{' '}
                        {toBengaliDigits(showDate(appliedMeta.endISO))} পর্যন্ত
                        {appliedMeta.unionName !== 'সব ইউনিয়ন'
                          ? ` · ইউনিয়ন: ${appliedMeta.unionName}`
                          : ''}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {activeMode === 'grain' ? (
                      <table style={{ width: '100%', tableLayout: 'fixed', borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
                        <thead>
                          <tr>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[0], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              ক্রমিক
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[1], fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              ইউনিয়ন ও ডিলার
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[2], fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              প্রতিষ্ঠান ও বিবরণ
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[3], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              খাত
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[4], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              পরিমাণ ও দর
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[5], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              প্রাপ্য টাকা
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[6], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              চালান নং ও তারিখ
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[7], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              জমাকৃত টাকা
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[8], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              গরমিল
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[9], fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              মন্তব্য
                            </th>
                            {customCols.map((col, cIdx) => (
                              <th
                                key={col.id}
                                style={{ width: grainWidths[10 + cIdx], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                              >
                                {col.label}
                              </th>
                            ))}
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[10 + customCols.length], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              ডিও নং ও তারিখ
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: grainWidths[11 + customCols.length], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              স্বাক্ষর
                            </th>
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                          {pageRows.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                              <td
                                colSpan={12 + customCols.length}
                                style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '16px', fontSize: cellFontPx }}
                              >
                                এই সময়ে কোনো খাদ্যশস্য চালান পাওয়া যায়নি।
                              </td>
                            </tr>
                          ) : (
                            pageRows.flatMap(({ row, globalIndex, subs }) =>
                              subs.map((sub, subIdx) => (
                                <tr key={sub.key}>
                                  {subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {toBengaliDigits(globalIndex + 1)}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  {subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                        {row.dealerName || '—'}
                                      </div>
                                      {row.unionName && (
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                          {row.unionName}
                                        </div>
                                      )}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  <td style={{ fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    <div>{sub.orgDesc}</div>
                                    {sub.new37Code && (
                                      <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px', fontWeight: 'bold', marginTop: '1px' }}>
                                        কোড: {toBengaliDigits(sub.new37Code)}
                                      </div>
                                    )}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.khatLabel}
                                  </td>
                                  <td
                                    style={{
                                      textAlign: 'center',
                                      whiteSpace: 'pre-line',
                                      fontSize: cellFontPx,
                                      padding: cellPad,
                                      ...cellClipStyle,
                                    }}
                                  >
                                    {sub.qtyRateText}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {fmtBnAmount(sub.expectedNum)}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    <div>{toBengaliDigits(sub.challanNo)}</div>
                                    {sub.challanDate && (
                                      <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                        {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanDate)}
                                      </div>
                                    )}
                                  </td>
                                  <td
                                    style={{
                                      textAlign: 'right',
                                      fontWeight: 'bold',
                                      fontSize: cellFontPx,
                                      padding: cellPad,
                                      ...cellClipStyle,
                                    }}
                                  >
                                    {fmtBnAmount(sub.depositedNum)}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.gormilText}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ fontSize: '9.5px', padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.comment}
                                  </td>
                                  {customCols.map((col) => (
                                    <td
                                      key={`${sub.key}-${col.id}`}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {subIdx === 0
                                        ? toBengaliDigits(row.customFields?.[col.id] || '')
                                        : ''}
                                    </td>
                                  ))}
                                  {subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {row.doNo ? (
                                        <div>
                                          <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                            {toBengaliDigits(row.doNo)}
                                          </div>
                                          {row.doIssueDate && (
                                            <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                              {toBengaliDigits(row.doIssueDate)}
                                            </div>
                                          )}
                                        </div>
                                      ) : (
                                        ''
                                      )}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '9px', padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {renderSignatureCell(sub)}
                                  </td>
                                </tr>
                              ))
                            )
                          )}
                          {isLastPage && report.rows.length > 0 && (
                            <tr style={{ backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' }}>
                              <th
                                colSpan={4}
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                মোট ({toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি)
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnQty(registerTotals.qtyTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.expectedTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.depositedTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.gormilTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              {customCols.map((col) => (
                                <th key={`foot-${col.id}`} style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              ))}
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                            </tr>
                          )}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    ) : (
                      <table style={{ width: '100%', tableLayout: 'fixed', borderCollapse: 'collapse' }}>
                        <thead>
                          <tr>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[0], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              ক্রমিক
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[1], fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              লাইসেন্সধারীর নাম ও ঠিকানা
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[2], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              জমা কোড
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[3], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              খাত
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[4], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              চালান নং ও তারিখ
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[5], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              সরকারি মূল্য
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[6], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              জমাকৃত টাকা
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[7], textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              গরমিল
                            </th>
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[8], fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              মন্তব্য
                            </th>
                            {customCols.map((col, cIdx) => (
                              <th
                                key={col.id}
                                style={{ width: licenseWidths[9 + cIdx], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                              >
                                {col.label}
                              </th>
                            ))}
                            {showLicenseDoColumn && (
                              <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[9 + customCols.length], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                ডিও নং ও তারিখ
                              </th>
                            )}
                            <th style={{ width: licenseWidths[9 + customCols.length + (showLicenseDoColumn ? 1 : 0)], textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                              স্বাক্ষর
                            </th>
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                          {pageRows.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                              <td
                                colSpan={
                                  10 +
                                  customCols.length +
                                  (showLicenseDoColumn ? 1 : 0)
                                }
                                style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '16px', fontSize: cellFontPx }}
                              >
                                এই সময়ে কোনো লাইসেন্স বা অন্যান্য চালান পাওয়া যায়নি।
                              </td>
                            </tr>
                          ) : (
                            pageRows.flatMap(({ row, globalIndex, subs }) =>
                              subs.map((sub, subIdx) => (
                                <tr key={sub.key}>
                                  {subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {toBengaliDigits(globalIndex + 1)}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  {subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                        {row.dealerName || '—'}
                                      </div>
                                      {row.unionName && (
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                          {row.unionName}
                                        </div>
                                      )}
                                      {row.licenseNo && (
                                        <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                          লাইঃ {toBengaliDigits(row.licenseNo)}
                                        </div>
                                      )}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '10px', padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {toBengaliDigits(sub.new37Code || '—')}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.khatLabel}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    <div>{toBengaliDigits(sub.challanNo)}</div>
                                    {sub.challanDate && (
                                      <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                        {toBengaliDigits(sub.challanDate)}
                                      </div>
                                    )}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {fmtBnAmount(sub.expectedNum)}
                                  </td>
                                  <td
                                    style={{
                                      textAlign: 'right',
                                      fontWeight: 'bold',
                                      fontSize: cellFontPx,
                                      padding: cellPad,
                                      ...cellClipStyle,
                                    }}
                                  >
                                    {fmtBnAmount(sub.depositedNum)}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.gormilText}
                                  </td>
                                  <td style={{ fontSize: '9.5px', padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {sub.comment}
                                  </td>
                                  {customCols.map((col) => (
                                    <td
                                      key={`${sub.key}-${col.id}`}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {subIdx === 0
                                        ? toBengaliDigits(row.customFields?.[col.id] || '')
                                        : ''}
                                    </td>
                                  ))}
                                  {showLicenseDoColumn && subIdx === 0 && (
                                    <td
                                      rowSpan={subs.length}
                                      style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}
                                    >
                                      {row.doNo ? (
                                        <div>
                                          <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                            {toBengaliDigits(row.doNo)}
                                          </div>
                                          {row.doIssueDate && (
                                            <div style={{ fontSize: '9.5px' }}>
                                              {toBengaliDigits(row.doIssueDate)}
                                            </div>
                                          )}
                                        </div>
                                      ) : (
                                        ''
                                      )}
                                    </td>
                                  )}
                                  <td style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '9px', padding: cellPad, ...cellClipStyle }}>
                                    {renderSignatureCell(sub)}
                                  </td>
                                </tr>
                              ))
                            )
                          )}
                          {isLastPage && report.rows.length > 0 && (
                            <tr style={{ backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' }}>
                              <th
                                colSpan={5}
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                মোট ({toBengaliDigits(report.rows.length)}টি)
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.expectedTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.depositedTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th
                                style={{ textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: cellFontPx, padding: cellPad }}
                              >
                                {fmtBnAmount(registerTotals.gormilTotal)}
                              </th>
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              {customCols.map((col) => (
                                <th key={`foot-${col.id}`} style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                              ))}
                              {showLicenseDoColumn && <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />}
                              <th style={{ padding: cellPad }} />
                            </tr>
                          )}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Clean Dedicated Page Footer (Never overlaps table!) */}
                  <div className="a4-page-footer">
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '10.5px', color: '#334155' }}>
                      পৃষ্ঠা {toBengaliDigits(pageIdx + 1)} /{' '}
                      {toBengaliDigits(pages.length)} · প্রিন্ট:{' '}
                      {toBengaliDigits(getTodayFormatted())}
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', minWidth: '150px' }}>
                      {signatureImage && (
                        <img
                          src={signatureImage}
                          alt="স্বাক্ষর"
                          style={{
                            height: '32px',
                            maxWidth: '130px',
                            objectFit: 'contain',
                            margin: '0 auto 2px auto',
                            display: 'block',
                          }}
                        />
                      )}
                      <div
                        style={{
                          borderTop: signatureImage ? '1px solid #475569' : '1px dashed #64748b',
                          paddingTop: '2px',
                          fontSize: '11px',
                          fontWeight: 'bold',
                        }}
                      >
                        {officerName || signatureText}
                      </div>
                      {officerName && (
                        <div style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: '#334155' }}>
                          {signatureText}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Floating Action Bar: Portrait | Landscape | Excel CSV | PDF | Print */}
      {report && (
        <div
          className="no-print fixed bottom-20 right-4 z-40 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/95 p-1.5 shadow-xl backdrop-blur md:bottom-6"
          role="toolbar"
          aria-label="রেজিস্টার প্রিন্ট ও পেজ নিয়ন্ত্রণ"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOrientation('portrait')}
            title="পোর্ট্রেট মোড (A4)"
            aria-label="পোর্ট্রেট মোড"
            className={`flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              orientation === 'portrait'
                ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            <RectangleVertical className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOrientation('landscape')}
            title="ল্যান্ডস্কেপ মোড (A4)"
            aria-label="ল্যান্ডস্কেপ মোড"
            className={`flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              orientation === 'landscape'
                ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
            }`}
          >
            <RectangleHorizontal className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <div className="h-6 w-px bg-slate-200" aria-hidden="true" />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={downloadExcelCsv}
            title="Excel / CSV ডাউনলোড"
            aria-label="Excel ডাউনলোড"
            className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-700 text-white hover:bg-emerald-800 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <FileSpreadsheet className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={pdfLoading}
            onClick={() => void downloadPdf()}
            title="PDF ডাউনলোড (Nikosh)"
            aria-label="PDF ডাউনলোড"
            className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-600 text-white hover:bg-amber-700 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {pdfLoading ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <FileDown className="h-5 w-5" />
            )}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => window.print()}
            title="প্রিন্ট (A4)"
            aria-label="প্রিন্ট"
            className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Printer className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function parseFlexibleDateMs(raw?: string): number | null {
  const trimmed = toEnglishDigits(raw || '').trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  const dmy = trimmed.match(/^(\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{2,4})$/);
  if (dmy) {
    const day = Number(dmy[1]);
    const month = Number(dmy[2]);
    const yearRaw = Number(dmy[3]);
    const year = yearRaw < 100 ? 2000 + yearRaw : yearRaw;
    const ms = new Date(year, month - 1, day, 12, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
    return Number.isNaN(ms) ? null : ms;
  }

  const ymd = trimmed.match(/^(\d{4})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})$/);
  if (ymd) {
    const ms = new Date(
      Number(ymd[1]),
      Number(ymd[2]) - 1,
      Number(ymd[3]),
      12,
      0,
      0,
      0
    ).getTime();
    return Number.isNaN(ms) ? null : ms;
  }

  const parsed = Date.parse(trimmed);
  return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
}

function getTimeProximityPenalty(
  createdAt: string | undefined,
  refMs: number
): number {
  const ts = parseFlexibleDateMs(createdAt);
  if (ts === null) return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  if (ts <= refMs) return refMs - ts;
  // Slightly penalize entries timestamped after refMs so immediate past/current entries come first
  return ts - refMs + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}

function formatCompactShortDate(raw?: string): string {
  const ms = parseFlexibleDateMs(raw);
  if (ms === null) return '';
  const d = new Date(ms);
  const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${dd}/${mm}`;
}

interface ScanEntryViewProps {
  settings: AppSettings;
  dealers: DealerRecord[];
  unions: string[];
  licensees: LicenseeRecord[];
  suballocations: SuballocationRecord[];
  records: ChallanRecord[];
  canOpenSettings?: boolean;
  onSaved: () => void;
  onOpenSettings: () => void;
  onOpenSheetSettings?: () => void;
  onOpenAllRecords: () => void;
  onFocusModeChange?: (focused: boolean) => void;
  externalScanRequestNonce: number;
}

export function ScanEntryView({
  settings,
  dealers,
  unions,
  licensees,
  suballocations,
  records,
  canOpenSettings = true,
  onSaved,
  onOpenSettings,
  onOpenSheetSettings,
  onOpenAllRecords,
  onFocusModeChange,
  externalScanRequestNonce,
}: ScanEntryViewProps) {
  const [selectedKhat, setSelectedKhat] = useState<KhatConfig | null>(null);
  const [licenseActionType, setLicenseActionType] = useState<
    'নতুন' | 'নবায়ন' | 'জরিমানা' | 'অন্যান্য' | ''
  >('');
  const [scanRole, setScanRole] = useState<ScanRole>('main');
  const [form, setForm] = useState<ScanFormState>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [economicOptions, setEconomicOptions] = useState<EconomicOption[]>([]);
  const [customFieldValues, setCustomFieldValues] = useState<
    Record<string, string>
  >({});

  // Selected Dealer / Licensee / Manual entry
  const [selectedUnion, setSelectedUnion] = useState('');
  const [selectedDealerId, setSelectedDealerId] = useState('');
  const [selectedDealerName, setSelectedDealerName] = useState('');
  const [selectedAllocId, setSelectedAllocId] = useState('');
  const [manualEntryOpen, setManualEntryOpen] = useState(false);

  // Camera & File scan states
  const [cameraOpen, setCameraOpen] = useState(false);
  const [fetchingOfficial, setFetchingOfficial] = useState(false);
  const [scanStatus, setScanStatus] = useState<{
    tone: 'neutral' | 'success' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Pre-Save Google Sheet confirmation dialog
  const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [pushToSheet, setPushToSheet] = useState<boolean>(
    () => getSheetPushCapability(settings).anyReady
  );
  const pushToggledManuallyRef = useRef(false);
  const [googleAuthNonce, setGoogleAuthNonce] = useState(0);
  const [googleConnecting, setGoogleConnecting] = useState(false);
  const [pendingSheetEntry, setPendingSheetEntry] =
    useState<SheetChallanEntry | null>(null);
  const [saveFeedback, setSaveFeedback] = useState<{
    tone: 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const sheetCapability = useMemo(
    () => getSheetPushCapability(settings),
    [settings, googleAuthNonce]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pushToggledManuallyRef.current) {
      setPushToSheet(sheetCapability.anyReady);
    }
  }, [sheetCapability.anyReady]);

  // Bottom Dashboard Feed State (Sorted by latest deposit date, configurable page size)
  const [dashCategoryFilter, setDashCategoryFilter] = useState('সকল');
  const [dashSearch, setDashSearch] = useState('');
  const [dashPage, setDashPage] = useState(0);
  const dashTouchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const suggestionStripRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const saveFeedbackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [activeSuggestionIdx, setActiveSuggestionIdx] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (saveFeedback) {
      saveFeedbackRef.current?.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: 'smooth',
        block: 'nearest',
      });
    }
  }, [saveFeedback]);

  // Notify parent App when focused full-screen mode changes
  useEffect(() => {
    onFocusModeChange?.(Boolean(selectedKhat));
  }, [selectedKhat, onFocusModeChange]);

  // Respond to header quick-scan CTA
  useEffect(() => {
    if (externalScanRequestNonce > 0) {
      if (!selectedKhat && settings.khats.length > 0) {
        const firstActive =
          settings.khats.find((k) => k.active) || settings.khats[0];
        setSelectedKhat(firstActive);
      }
      setScanRole('main');
      setCameraOpen(true);
    }
  }, [externalScanRequestNonce, selectedKhat, settings.khats]);

  // Helper: get reference expected fee from Settings for active License category + role
  function computeReferenceFee(
    khat: KhatConfig | null,
    action: typeof licenseActionType,
    role: ScanRole
  ): string {
    if (!khat || khat.group !== 'license') return '';
    const feeCfg = settings.licenseFees?.find((f) => f.khatName === khat.name);
    if (!feeCfg) return '';
    if (role === 'tax') return feeCfg.taxAmount ? String(feeCfg.taxAmount) : '';
    if (role === 'vat') return feeCfg.vatAmount ? String(feeCfg.vatAmount) : '';
    if (action === 'নবায়ন') {
      return feeCfg.renewalFee ? String(feeCfg.renewalFee) : '';
    }
    return feeCfg.newFee ? String(feeCfg.newFee) : '';
  }

  // Update reference fee when role/action/khat changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedKhat || selectedKhat.group !== 'license') return;
    const feeCfg = settings.licenseFees?.find((f) => f.khatName === selectedKhat.name);
    const refFee = computeReferenceFee(selectedKhat, licenseActionType, scanRole);
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      ...(refFee ? { expectedAmount: refFee } : {}),
      ...(feeCfg?.taxAmount ? { expectedTaxAmount: String(feeCfg.taxAmount) } : {}),
    }));
  }, [selectedKhat, licenseActionType, scanRole, settings.licenseFees]);

  // Suballocations already saved against a challan for the active role NEVER reappear
  const linkedAllocationIds = useMemo(() => {
    const norm = (v?: string) => (v || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
    const set = new Set<string>();

    for (const sub of suballocations) {
      const subName = norm(sub.dealerName);
      const subUnion = norm(sub.unionName);

      const alreadySaved = records.some((r) => {
        const hasMainSaved =
          Boolean(r.challanNo?.trim()) || toNumericValue(r.amount) > 0;
        const hasTaxSaved =
          Boolean(r.incomeTaxChallanNo?.trim()) ||
          toNumericValue(r.incomeTaxAmount) > 0;
        const hasVatSaved =
          Boolean(r.vatChallanNo?.trim()) || toNumericValue(r.vatAmount) > 0;

        const roleDone =
          scanRole === 'tax'
            ? hasTaxSaved
            : scanRole === 'vat'
            ? hasVatSaved
            : hasMainSaved;

        if (!roleDone) return false;

        if (r.dealerAllocationId && r.dealerAllocationId === sub.id) {
          return true;
        }

        // Fallback match for records synced without dealerAllocationId
        if (!r.dealerAllocationId && subName && norm(r.dealerName) === subName) {
          if (subUnion && norm(r.unionName) && norm(r.unionName) !== subUnion) {
            return false;
          }
          if (scanRole === 'tax' && sub.taxAmount > 0) {
            return Math.abs(toNumericValue(r.incomeTaxAmount) - sub.taxAmount) < 1;
          }
          if (scanRole === 'main' && sub.totalAmount > 0) {
            return Math.abs(toNumericValue(r.amount) - sub.totalAmount) < 1;
          }
        }
        return false;
      });

      if (alreadySaved) {
        set.add(sub.id);
      }
    }
    return set;
  }, [records, suballocations, scanRole]);

  const unlinkedSuballocations = useMemo(
    () => suballocations.filter((s) => !linkedAllocationIds.has(s.id)),
    [suballocations, linkedAllocationIds]
  );

  const scanRefMs = useMemo(() => {
    const challanDayMs = parseFlexibleDateMs(form.challanDate);
    if (challanDayMs !== null) {
      const d = new Date(challanDayMs);
      return new Date(
        d.getFullYear(),
        d.getMonth(),
        d.getDate(),
        23,
        59,
        59,
        999
      ).getTime();
    }
    return Date.now();
  }, [form.challanDate]);

  // 1. Grain Smart Matcher: rank ALL unlinked allocations by match quality + closest date/time to scan time
  const matchedSuballocations = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedKhat || selectedKhat.group !== 'grain') return [];
    const scannedAmt = toNumericValue(form.amount);
    const nameHint =
      form.submittedOnBehalfOf || form.submittedThrough || '';

    const list = unlinkedSuballocations.map((s) => {
      const targetAmt = scanRole === 'tax' ? s.taxAmount : s.totalAmount;
      const diff =
        scannedAmt > 0 ? Math.abs(targetAmt - scannedAmt) : 999999999;
      const exact = scannedAmt > 0 && diff < 0.5;
      const close = scannedAmt > 0 && diff <= 50;
      const nameScore = nameHint
        ? scoreBengaliNameSimilarity(nameHint, s.dealerName)
        : 0;
      const ts = parseFlexibleDateMs(s.createdAt);
      const timePenalty = getTimeProximityPenalty(s.createdAt, scanRefMs);
      return {
        sub: s,
        targetAmt,
        diff,
        exact,
        close,
        nameScore,
        ts,
        timePenalty,
      };
    });

    list.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.exact !== b.exact) return a.exact ? -1 : 1;
      if (a.close !== b.close) return a.close ? -1 : 1;
      if (Math.abs(a.diff - b.diff) > 1) return a.diff - b.diff;
      if (nameHint && Math.abs(a.nameScore - b.nameScore) > 0.15) {
        return b.nameScore - a.nameScore;
      }
      if (a.timePenalty !== b.timePenalty) {
        return a.timePenalty - b.timePenalty;
      }
      return (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0);
    });

    return list;
  }, [
    selectedKhat,
    unlinkedSuballocations,
    form.amount,
    form.submittedOnBehalfOf,
    form.submittedThrough,
    scanRole,
    scanRefMs,
  ]);

  // 2. License Smart Matcher: rank all matching candidates so user can swipe left one-by-one
  const matchedLicenseCandidates = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedKhat || selectedKhat.group !== 'license') return [];
    const queryText = [
      form.submittedThrough,
      form.submittedOnBehalfOf,
      form.description,
      form.licenseNo,
      form.challanNo,
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' ');

    const isRetailOrWholesale =
      selectedKhat.name.includes('খুচরা') ||
      selectedKhat.name.includes('পাইকারি');

    if (isRetailOrWholesale && licensees.length > 0) {
      const scored = licensees.map((lic) => {
        const licMatch =
          lic.licenseNo &&
          queryText.includes(toEnglishDigits(lic.licenseNo).trim())
            ? 1
            : 0;
        const nidMatch =
          lic.nid && queryText.includes(toEnglishDigits(lic.nid).trim())
            ? 0.98
            : 0;
        const nameScore = queryText
          ? scoreBengaliNameSimilarity(queryText, lic.licenseeName)
          : 0;
        return {
          id: lic.id,
          name: lic.licenseeName,
          address: lic.address,
          licenseNo: lic.licenseNo,
          licenseType: lic.licenseType,
          score: Math.max(licMatch, nidMatch, nameScore),
        };
      });
      scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
      return scored.slice(0, 25);
    }

    const catKeyword = selectedKhat.name.includes('ওএমএস')
      ? 'ওএমএস'
      : selectedKhat.name.includes('খাদ্য')
      ? 'খাদ্য'
      : '';
    const pool = catKeyword
      ? dealers.filter(
          (d) => !d.category || d.category.includes(catKeyword)
        )
      : dealers;
