// ==================== FILE: src/services/googleAuth.ts ====================
import { initializeApp, getApps } from 'firebase/app';
import {
  getAuth,
  GoogleAuthProvider,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  signInWithPopup,
  type User,
} from 'firebase/auth';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app =
  getApps().length > 0 ? getApps()[0] : initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
const auth = getAuth(app);

const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
provider.addScope('https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets');
provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });

let isSigningIn = false;
// Store access token strictly in memory (never in localStorage/sessionStorage)
let cachedAccessToken: string | null = null;

export function initGoogleAuth(
  onAuthSuccess?: (user: User, token: string) => void,
  onAuthFailure?: (user: User | null) => void
) {
  return onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
    if (user && cachedAccessToken) {
      onAuthSuccess?.(user, cachedAccessToken);
    } else {
      if (!isSigningIn) {
        cachedAccessToken = null;
      }
      onAuthFailure?.(user);
    }
  });
}

export async function googleSignIn(): Promise<{
  user: User;
  accessToken: string;
} | null> {
  try {
    isSigningIn = true;
    const result = await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
    const credential = GoogleAuthProvider.credentialFromResult(result);
    if (!credential?.accessToken) {
      throw new Error('Google Sheets অ্যাক্সেস টোকেন পাওয়া যায়নি।');
    }
    cachedAccessToken = credential.accessToken;
    return { user: result.user, accessToken: cachedAccessToken };
  } finally {
    isSigningIn = false;
  }
}

export function getGoogleAccessToken(): string | null {
  return cachedAccessToken;
}

export function clearGoogleAccessToken(): void {
  cachedAccessToken = null;
}

export function getCurrentFirebaseUser(): User | null {
  return auth.currentUser;
}

export async function googleLogout(): Promise<void> {
  cachedAccessToken = null;
  await auth.signOut();
}


// ==================== END: src/services/googleAuth.ts ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/services/googleSheets.ts ====================
import type {
  AppSettings,
  ChallanRecord,
  DealerRecord,
  DoIssueRecord,
  SuballocationRecord,
} from '../types.ts';
import { apiFetch } from '../utils/api.ts';
import {
  extractSpreadsheetId,
  normalizeChallanNo,
  toSheetSlashDate,
} from '../utils/challan.ts';
import {
  colLetterToIndex,
  parseChallansFromRows,
  parseDealersFromRows,
  parseDoIssuesFromRows,
  parseSuballocationsFromRows,
} from '../utils/sheetParser.ts';
import { clearGoogleAccessToken, getGoogleAccessToken } from './googleAuth.ts';

const SHEETS_API = 'https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets';

export interface SheetSyncPayload {
  dealers: DealerRecord[];
  suballocations: SuballocationRecord[];
  doIssues: DoIssueRecord[];
  challans: ChallanRecord[];
  availableTabs: string[];
}

async function throwGoogleApiError(
  res: Response,
  fallbackAction: string
): Promise<never> {
  if (res.status === 401) {
    clearGoogleAccessToken();
    throw new Error('Google সাইন-ইন সেশনের মেয়াদ শেষ হয়েছে — পুনরায় সাইন-ইন করুন।');
  }
  let detail = '';
  try {
    const json = (await res.json()) as { error?: { message?: string } };
    detail = json?.error?.message || '';
  } catch {
    try {
      detail = (await res.text()).slice(0, 180);
    } catch {
      detail = '';
    }
  }
  if (res.status === 403) {
    throw new Error(
      `Google Sheet-এ লেখার অনুমতি নেই (403)${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ''}`
    );
  }
  throw new Error(
    `${fallbackAction} (${res.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ''}`
  );
}

async function fetchSheetValues(
  spreadsheetId: string,
  range: string,
  accessToken: string
): Promise<string[][]> {
  const url = `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(
    spreadsheetId
  )}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`;
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    await throwGoogleApiError(res, 'Google Sheet পড়তে সমস্যা');
  }
  const json = (await res.json()) as { values?: string[][] };
  return Array.isArray(json.values) ? json.values : [];
}

export async function getSpreadsheetTabs(
  spreadsheetId: string,
  accessToken: string
): Promise<string[]> {
  const url = `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(
    spreadsheetId
  )}?fields=sheets.properties.title`;
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    await throwGoogleApiError(res, 'স্প্রেডশিট মেটাডেটা পড়া যায়নি');
  }
  const json = (await res.json()) as {
    sheets?: Array<{ properties?: { title?: string } }>;
  };
  return (json.sheets || [])
    .map((s) => s.properties?.title || '')
    .filter(Boolean);
}

export async function pullFromGoogleSheetsOAuth(
  settings: AppSettings
): Promise<SheetSyncPayload> {
  const token = getGoogleAccessToken();
  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('আগে সেটিংস থেকে Google অ্যাকাউন্টে সাইন-ইন করুন।');
  }
  const cleanId = extractSpreadsheetId(settings.spreadsheetId);
  if (!cleanId) {
    throw new Error('Spreadsheet ID প্রদান করুন।');
  }

  const availableTabs = await getSpreadsheetTabs(cleanId, token);

  const dealers: DealerRecord[] = [];
  let suballocations: SuballocationRecord[] = [];
  let doIssues: DoIssueRecord[] = [];
  let challans: ChallanRecord[] = [];

  if (availableTabs.includes(settings.foodDealerTabName)) {
    const rows = await fetchSheetValues(
      cleanId,
      `${settings.foodDealerTabName}!A:Z`,
      token
    );
    dealers.push(...parseDealersFromRows(rows, 'খাদ্য বান্ধব'));
  }

  if (availableTabs.includes(settings.omsDealerTabName)) {
    const rows = await fetchSheetValues(
      cleanId,
      `${settings.omsDealerTabName}!A:Z`,
      token
    );
    dealers.push(...parseDealersFromRows(rows, 'ওএমএস সাধারণ'));
  }

  if (availableTabs.includes(settings.suballocationTabName)) {
    const rows = await fetchSheetValues(
      cleanId,
      `${settings.suballocationTabName}!A:Z`,
      token
    );
    suballocations = parseSuballocationsFromRows(rows);
  }

  const doTab = settings.doIssueTabName?.trim() || 'DO_Issue';
  if (availableTabs.includes(doTab)) {
    const rows = await fetchSheetValues(cleanId, `${doTab}!A:Z`, token);
    doIssues = parseDoIssuesFromRows(rows);
  }

  if (availableTabs.includes(settings.challanTabName)) {
    const rows = await fetchSheetValues(
      cleanId,
      `${settings.challanTabName}!A:AD`,
      token
    );
    challans = parseChallansFromRows(
      rows,
      dealers,
      settings,
      suballocations,
      doIssues
    );
  }

  return { dealers, suballocations, doIssues, challans, availableTabs };
}

export async function pullFromPublicSheetBackend(
  settings?: Partial<AppSettings>
): Promise<{
  success: boolean;
  counts?: { dealers: number; suballocations: number; records: number };
  message?: string;
}> {
  const res = await apiFetch('/api/sync/public-sheet', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(settings || {}),
  });
  const json = (await res.json()) as {
    success: boolean;
    counts?: { dealers: number; suballocations: number; records: number };
    message?: string;
  };
  if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
    throw new Error(
      json.message || 'Google Sheet থেকে তথ্য আনা যায়নি। সেটিংস যাচাই করুন।'
    );
  }
  return json;
}

export interface SheetChallanEntry {
  scanType: 'main' | 'tax' | 'vat' | 'single';
  id: string;
  date: string;
  department: string;
  category: string;
  sourceMemo: string;
  dcMemo: string;
  dealerAllocationId: string;
  dealerName?: string;
  unionName?: string;
  licenseActionType?: string;
  expectedAmount?: string;
  expectedTaxAmount?: string;
  challanNo: string;
  challanDate: string;
  rate: string;
  quantity: string;
  totalAmount: string;
  incomeTaxChallanNo: string;
  incomeTaxDate: string;
  incomeTaxAmount: string;
  vatChallanNo?: string;
  vatDate?: string;
  vatAmount?: string;
  licenseNo?: string;
  new37Code?: string;
  customFields?: Record<string, string>;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
  srcRecordId: string;
}

export function getSheetPushCapability(settings: AppSettings): {
  oauthReady: boolean;
  gasReady: boolean;
  anyReady: boolean;
  methodLabel: string;
} {
  const token = getGoogleAccessToken();
  const hasSheetId = Boolean(extractSpreadsheetId(settings.spreadsheetId));
  const hasGasUrl = Boolean(settings.gasWebAppUrl?.trim());
  const oauthReady =
    (settings.syncMethod === 'oauth' || settings.syncMethod === 'both') &&
    Boolean(token) &&
    hasSheetId;
  const gasReady =
    (settings.syncMethod === 'apps_script' || settings.syncMethod === 'both') &&
    hasGasUrl;

  const methodLabel =
    oauthReady && gasReady
      ? 'Google OAuth ও লগইন-ছাড়া Apps Script উভয় সক্রিয়'
      : oauthReady
      ? 'Google OAuth শিট সংযুক্ত'
      : gasReady
      ? 'লগইন-ছাড়া Apps Script ব্রিজ সক্রিয়'
      : hasSheetId
      ? 'শুধু সরাসরি শিট পড়া (Sync) সক্রিয় — পুশ করতে Apps Script বা OAuth যুক্ত করুন'
      : 'Google Sheet পুশ সংযুক্ত নয়';

  return {
    oauthReady,
    gasReady,
    anyReady: oauthReady || gasReady,
    methodLabel,
  };
}

export function challanRecordToSheetEntry(r: ChallanRecord): SheetChallanEntry {
  const hasMain = Boolean(r.challanNo || r.amount);
  const hasTax = Boolean(r.incomeTaxChallanNo || r.incomeTaxAmount);
  const hasVat = Boolean(r.vatChallanNo || r.vatAmount);
  const scanType: SheetChallanEntry['scanType'] = hasMain
    ? hasTax || hasVat
      ? 'main'
      : 'single'
    : hasTax
    ? 'tax'
    : hasVat
    ? 'vat'
    : 'single';

  return {
    scanType,
    id: r.id || r.recordId || `CHL-${r.serialNo}`,
    date: r.challanDate || r.incomeTaxDate || r.vatDate || r.date || '',
    department: r.department || r.toOrganization || '',
    category: r.challanCategory || '',
    sourceMemo: r.sourceMemo || '',
    dcMemo: r.dcMemo || '',
    dealerAllocationId: r.dealerAllocationId || '',
    dealerName: r.dealerName || '',
    unionName: r.unionName || '',
    licenseActionType: r.licenseActionType || '',
    expectedAmount: r.expectedAmount || '',
    expectedTaxAmount: r.expectedTaxAmount || '',
    challanNo: r.challanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(r.challanNo) : '',
    challanDate: r.challanDate || '',
    rate: r.rate || '',
    quantity: r.quantity || '',
    totalAmount: r.amount || '',
    incomeTaxChallanNo: r.incomeTaxChallanNo
      ? normalizeChallanNo(r.incomeTaxChallanNo)
      : '',
    incomeTaxDate: r.incomeTaxDate || '',
    incomeTaxAmount: r.incomeTaxAmount || '',
    vatChallanNo: r.vatChallanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(r.vatChallanNo) : '',
    vatDate: r.vatDate || '',
    vatAmount: r.vatAmount || '',
    licenseNo: r.licenseNo || '',
    new37Code: r.new37Code || '',
    customFields: r.customFields || {},
    comment:
      r.comment ||
      [r.licenseActionType, r.description].filter(Boolean).join(' | '),
    createdAt: r.entryTime || new Date().toISOString(),
    srcRecordId: r.recordId || '',
  };
}

function findMatchingSheetRowIndex(
  existingRows: string[][],
  entry: SheetChallanEntry
): number {
  const entryNo = entry.challanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.challanNo) : '';
  const entryTaxNo = entry.incomeTaxChallanNo
    ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.incomeTaxChallanNo)
    : '';
  const entryVatNo = entry.vatChallanNo
    ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.vatChallanNo)
    : '';

  const canFillSlot = (
    rowChallanNo: string,
    rowTaxNo: string,
    rowVatNo: string
  ): boolean => {
    if (entryNo && rowChallanNo && rowChallanNo !== entryNo) return false;
    if (!entryNo && entryTaxNo && rowTaxNo && rowTaxNo !== entryTaxNo) {
      return false;
    }
    if (
      !entryNo &&
      !entryTaxNo &&
      entryVatNo &&
      rowVatNo &&
      rowVatNo !== entryVatNo
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  };

  for (let i = 1; i < existingRows.length; i++) {
    const row = existingRows[i] || [];
    const rowId = String(row[0] || '').trim();
    const rowCat = String(row[3] || '').trim();
    const rowAllocId = String(row[6] || '').trim();
    const rowChallanNo = row[7] ? normalizeChallanNo(String(row[7])) : '';
    const rowTaxNo = row[12] ? normalizeChallanNo(String(row[12])) : '';
    const rowSrcId = String(row[17] || '').trim();
    const rowVatNo = row[18] ? normalizeChallanNo(String(row[18])) : '';
    const rowLicenseNo = String(row[21] || '').trim();

    if (entry.srcRecordId && rowSrcId && rowSrcId === entry.srcRecordId) {
      return i + 1;
    }
    if (entry.id && rowId && rowId === entry.id) {
      return i + 1;
    }
    if (entryNo && rowChallanNo && rowChallanNo === entryNo) {
      return i + 1;
    }
    if (entryTaxNo && rowTaxNo && rowTaxNo === entryTaxNo && !entryNo) {
      return i + 1;
    }
    if (
      entry.dealerAllocationId &&
      rowAllocId &&
      rowAllocId === entry.dealerAllocationId &&
      canFillSlot(rowChallanNo, rowTaxNo, rowVatNo)
    ) {
      return i + 1;
    }
    if (
      entry.licenseNo &&
      rowLicenseNo &&
      rowLicenseNo === entry.licenseNo &&
      rowCat === entry.category &&
      canFillSlot(rowChallanNo, rowTaxNo, rowVatNo)
    ) {
      return i + 1;
    }
  }
  return -1;
}

function buildSheetRowValues(
  settings: AppSettings,
  entry: SheetChallanEntry,
  cur: string[] = []
): string[] {
  const row: string[] = new Array(23).fill('');
  for (let k = 0; k < Math.min(cur.length, 30); k++) {
    row[k] = cur[k] ?? '';
  }
  row[0] = cur[0] || entry.id;
  row[1] = cur[1] || toSheetSlashDate(entry.date);
  row[2] = entry.department || cur[2] || '';
  row[3] = entry.category || cur[3] || '';
  row[4] = entry.sourceMemo || cur[4] || '';
  row[5] = entry.dcMemo || cur[5] || '';
  row[6] = entry.dealerAllocationId || cur[6] || '';
  row[7] = entry.challanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.challanNo) : cur[7] || '';
  row[8] = entry.challanDate ? toSheetSlashDate(entry.challanDate) : cur[8] || '';
  row[9] = entry.rate || cur[9] || '';
  row[10] = entry.quantity || cur[10] || '';
  row[11] = entry.totalAmount || cur[11] || '';
  row[12] = entry.incomeTaxChallanNo
    ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.incomeTaxChallanNo)
    : cur[12] || '';
  row[13] = entry.incomeTaxDate
    ? toSheetSlashDate(entry.incomeTaxDate)
    : cur[13] || '';
  row[14] = entry.incomeTaxAmount || cur[14] || '';
  row[15] = entry.comment || cur[15] || '';
  row[16] = cur[16] || entry.createdAt;
  row[17] = cur[17] || entry.srcRecordId;
  row[18] = entry.vatChallanNo
    ? normalizeChallanNo(entry.vatChallanNo)
    : cur[18] || '';
  row[19] = entry.vatDate ? toSheetSlashDate(entry.vatDate) : cur[19] || '';
  row[20] = entry.vatAmount || cur[20] || '';
  row[21] = entry.licenseNo || cur[21] || '';
  row[22] = entry.new37Code || cur[22] || '';

  for (const colDef of settings.customColumns || []) {
    const idx = colLetterToIndex(colDef.sheetColumn);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      while (row.length <= idx) row.push('');
      const val = entry.customFields?.[colDef.id];
      if (val) row[idx] = val;
    }
  }
  return row;
}

export async function writeChallanToGoogleSheetOAuth(
  settings: AppSettings,
  entry: SheetChallanEntry
): Promise<{ mode: 'updated' | 'appended'; rowNumber: number }> {
  const token = getGoogleAccessToken();
  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('Google অ্যাকাউন্টে সাইন-ইন করা নেই।');
  }
  const sheetId = extractSpreadsheetId(settings.spreadsheetId);
  if (!sheetId) {
    throw new Error('Spreadsheet ID সেট করা নেই।');
  }
  const tab = settings.challanTabName.trim() || 'Challan';

  const availableTabs = await getSpreadsheetTabs(sheetId, token);
  if (!availableTabs.includes(tab)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Google Sheet-এ "${tab}" ট্যাবটি পাওয়া যায়নি। (বিদ্যমান ট্যাব: ${availableTabs.join(', ')})`
    );
  }

  const existingRows = await fetchSheetValues(sheetId, `${tab}!A:AD`, token);
  const matchRowIndex = findMatchingSheetRowIndex(existingRows, entry);

  let resultInfo: { mode: 'updated' | 'appended'; rowNumber: number };

  if (matchRowIndex > 0) {
    const cur = existingRows[matchRowIndex - 1] || [];
    const mergedRow = buildSheetRowValues(settings, entry, cur);
    const updateUrl = `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(
      sheetId
    )}/values/${encodeURIComponent(
      `${tab}!A${matchRowIndex}:AD${matchRowIndex}`
    )}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`;

    const res = await fetch(updateUrl, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ values: [mergedRow] }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      await throwGoogleApiError(res, 'Google Sheet আপডেট ব্যর্থ');
    }
    resultInfo = { mode: 'updated', rowNumber: matchRowIndex };
  } else {
    const newRow = buildSheetRowValues(settings, entry);
    const appendUrl = `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(
      sheetId
    )}/values/${encodeURIComponent(
      `${tab}!A:AD`
    )}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`;

    const res = await fetch(appendUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ values: [newRow] }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      await throwGoogleApiError(res, 'Google Sheet সংরক্ষণ ব্যর্থ');
    }
    resultInfo = { mode: 'appended', rowNumber: existingRows.length + 1 };
  }

  const vatTab = settings.separateVatTabName?.trim();
  if (vatTab && entry.vatChallanNo && availableTabs.includes(vatTab)) {
    const vatRow = [
      entry.id,
      toSheetSlashDate(entry.vatDate || entry.date),
      entry.category,
      entry.dealerName || '',
      entry.licenseNo || '',
      normalizeChallanNo(entry.vatChallanNo),
      entry.vatAmount || '',
      entry.new37Code || '',
      entry.createdAt,
    ];
    await fetch(
      `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(
        sheetId
      )}/values/${encodeURIComponent(
        `${vatTab}!A:I`
      )}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ values: [vatRow] }),
      }
    ).catch(() => undefined);
  }

  return resultInfo;
}

async function batchPushChallansToGoogleSheetOAuth(
  settings: AppSettings,
  entries: SheetChallanEntry[]
): Promise<{ updated: number; appended: number }> {
  const token = getGoogleAccessToken();
  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('Google অ্যাকাউন্টে সাইন-ইন করা নেই।');
  }
  const sheetId = extractSpreadsheetId(settings.spreadsheetId);
  const tab = settings.challanTabName.trim() || 'Challan';
  const existingRows = await fetchSheetValues(sheetId, `${tab}!A:AD`, token);

  const updates: Array<{ range: string; values: string[][] }> = [];
  const appends: string[][] = [];

  for (const entry of entries) {
    const matchRowIndex = findMatchingSheetRowIndex(existingRows, entry);
    if (matchRowIndex > 0) {
      const cur = existingRows[matchRowIndex - 1] || [];
      const merged = buildSheetRowValues(settings, entry, cur);
      existingRows[matchRowIndex - 1] = merged;
      updates.push({
        range: `${tab}!A${matchRowIndex}:AD${matchRowIndex}`,
        values: [merged],
      });
    } else {
      const created = buildSheetRowValues(settings, entry);
      existingRows.push(created);
      appends.push(created);
    }
  }

  if (updates.length > 0) {
    const batchRes = await fetch(
      `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(sheetId)}/values:batchUpdate`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED',
          data: updates,
        }),
      }
    );
    if (!batchRes.ok) {
      await throwGoogleApiError(batchRes, 'ব্যাচ আপডেট ব্যর্থ');
    }
  }

  if (appends.length > 0) {
    const appendRes = await fetch(
      `${SHEETS_API}/${encodeURIComponent(sheetId)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(
        `${tab}!A:AD`
      )}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ values: appends }),
      }
    );
    if (!appendRes.ok) {
      await throwGoogleApiError(appendRes, 'ব্যাচ সংরক্ষণ ব্যর্থ');
    }
  }

  return { updated: updates.length, appended: appends.length };
}

export async function pullFromAppsScriptBridge(
  gasWebAppUrl: string,
  settings?: AppSettings
): Promise<Partial<SheetSyncPayload>> {
  const res = await apiFetch('/api/gas-bridge', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ url: gasWebAppUrl, action: 'syncAll' }),
  });
  const json = (await res.json()) as {
    success: boolean;
    message?: string;
    data?: Partial<SheetSyncPayload> & {
      tabs?: {
        challan?: string[][];
        suballocations?: string[][];
        foodDealers?: string[][];
        omsDealers?: string[][];
        doIssues?: string[][];
      };
    };
  };
  if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
    throw new Error(json.message || 'Apps Script ব্রিজ থেকে তথ্য আনা যায়নি।');
  }
  const raw = json.data || {};
  if (raw.tabs) {
    const dealers: DealerRecord[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(raw.tabs.foodDealers)) {
      dealers.push(...parseDealersFromRows(raw.tabs.foodDealers, 'খাদ্য বান্ধব'));
    }
    if (Array.isArray(raw.tabs.omsDealers)) {
      dealers.push(...parseDealersFromRows(raw.tabs.omsDealers, 'ওএমএস সাধারণ'));
    }
    const suballocations = Array.isArray(raw.tabs.suballocations)
      ? parseSuballocationsFromRows(raw.tabs.suballocations)
      : [];
    const doIssues = Array.isArray(raw.tabs.doIssues)
      ? parseDoIssuesFromRows(raw.tabs.doIssues)
      : [];
    const fallbackSettings =
      settings ||
      ({
        customColumns: [],
      } as unknown as AppSettings);
    const challans = Array.isArray(raw.tabs.challan)
      ? parseChallansFromRows(
          raw.tabs.challan,
          dealers,
          fallbackSettings,
          suballocations,
          doIssues
        )
      : [];
    return {
      dealers,
      suballocations,
      doIssues,
      challans,
      availableTabs: raw.availableTabs || [],
    };
  }
  return raw;
}

export async function pushToAppsScriptBridge(
  gasWebAppUrl: string,
  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
  action: 'saveChallan' | 'saveChallanBatch' = 'saveChallan'
): Promise<{ message?: string; updated?: number; appended?: number }> {
  const res = await apiFetch('/api/gas-bridge', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ url: gasWebAppUrl, action, payload }),
  });
  const json = (await res.json()) as {
    success: boolean;
    message?: string;
    data?: { message?: string; updated?: number; appended?: number };
  };
  if (!res.ok || !json.success) {
    throw new Error(
      json.message || 'Apps Script ব্রিজে তথ্য পাঠানো যায়নি।'
    );
  }
  return json.data || { message: json.message };
}

export interface SheetPushSummary {
  total: number;
  oauthUpdated: number;
  oauthAppended: number;
  gasPushed: number;
  notes: string[];
}

export async function pushRecordsToGoogleSheet(
  settings: AppSettings,
  records: ChallanRecord[],
  onProgress?: (done: number, total: number) => void
): Promise<SheetPushSummary> {
  if (records.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('শিটে পাঠানোর জন্য কোনো চালান রেকর্ড নেই।');
  }
  const cap = getSheetPushCapability(settings);
  if (!cap.anyReady) {
    throw new Error(
      'Google Sheet-এ পুশ করার জন্য আগে Google অ্যাকাউন্টে সাইন-ইন অথবা সঠিক Apps Script Web App URL যুক্ত করুন।'
    );
  }

  const entries = records.map(challanRecordToSheetEntry);
  let oauthUpdated = 0;
  let oauthAppended = 0;
  let gasPushed = 0;
  const notes: string[] = [];
  const errors: string[] = [];

  if (cap.oauthReady) {
    try {
      if (entries.length === 1) {
        const r = await writeChallanToGoogleSheetOAuth(settings, entries[0]);
        if (r.mode === 'updated') oauthUpdated = 1;
        else oauthAppended = 1;
        notes.push(
          r.mode === 'updated'
            ? `Google Sheet সারি ${r.rowNumber} আপডেট`
            : `Google Sheet নতুন সারি ${r.rowNumber} যোগ`
        );
      } else {
        const res = await batchPushChallansToGoogleSheetOAuth(settings, entries);
        oauthUpdated = res.updated;
        oauthAppended = res.appended;
        notes.push(
          `Google OAuth: আপডেট ${res.updated}টি, নতুন যোগ ${res.appended}টি`
        );
      }
      onProgress?.(entries.length, entries.length);
    } catch (err) {
      errors.push(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
    }
  }

  if (cap.gasReady) {
    const gasUrl = settings.gasWebAppUrl.trim();
    try {
      if (entries.length === 1) {
        await pushToAppsScriptBridge(
          gasUrl,
          entries[0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
          'saveChallan'
        );
        gasPushed = 1;
        notes.push('Apps Script-এ সংরক্ষিত');
        onProgress?.(1, 1);
      } else {
        try {
          await pushToAppsScriptBridge(
            gasUrl,
            { entries },
            'saveChallanBatch'
          );
          gasPushed = entries.length;
          notes.push(`Apps Script: ${entries.length}টি পুশ সম্পন্ন`);
          onProgress?.(entries.length, entries.length);
        } catch (batchErr) {
          const msg = batchErr instanceof Error ? batchErr.message : '';
          if (msg.includes('Unknown action')) {
            throw batchErr;
          }
          // Fallback sequentially only if batch action wasn't implemented cleanly
          for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
            await pushToAppsScriptBridge(
              gasUrl,
              entries[i] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
              'saveChallan'
            );
            gasPushed++;
            onProgress?.(i + 1, entries.length);
          }
          notes.push(`Apps Script: ${gasPushed}টি পুশ সম্পন্ন`);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      errors.push(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
    }
  }

  const anySucceeded = oauthUpdated + oauthAppended > 0 || gasPushed > 0;
  if (!anySucceeded) {
    throw new Error(
      errors.join(' · ') || 'Google Sheet-এ কোনো তথ্য সংরক্ষণ করা যায়নি।'
    );
  }

  return {
    total: entries.length,
    oauthUpdated,
    oauthAppended,
    gasPushed,
    notes: [...notes, ...errors.map((e) => `সতর্কতা: ${e}`)],
  };
}

export function generateGoogleAppsScriptCode(settings: AppSettings): string {
  const spreadsheetId = extractSpreadsheetId(settings.spreadsheetId || '');
  const challanTab = settings.challanTabName?.trim() || 'Challan';
  const subTab = settings.suballocationTabName?.trim() || 'DealerSuballocations';
  const foodTab = settings.foodDealerTabName?.trim() || 'Dealers';
  const omsTab = settings.omsDealerTabName?.trim() || 'OMS_Dealers';
  const doTab = settings.doIssueTabName?.trim() || 'DO_Issue';
  const vatTab = settings.separateVatTabName?.trim() || '';
  const customCols = (settings.customColumns || []).map((c) => ({
    id: c.id,
    label: c.label,
    colIndex: colLetterToIndex(c.sheetColumn),
  }));

  return `// Google Apps Script Web App Bridge (Code.gs)
// কোথায় বসাবেন: আপনার Google Sheet খুলুন > Extensions > Apps Script > Code.gs-এ পেস্ট করুন
// ডিপ্লয়: Deploy > New deployment > Select type: Web app > Execute as: Me | Who has access: Anyone

// আপনার শিটের ID (খালি রাখলে যে শিট থেকে খুলবেন সেই অ্যাক্টিভ শিটই ব্যবহার হবে):
const SPREADSHEET_ID = ${JSON.stringify(spreadsheetId)};
const TAB_CHALLAN = ${JSON.stringify(challanTab)};
const TAB_SUBALLOC = ${JSON.stringify(subTab)};
const TAB_FOOD_DEALER = ${JSON.stringify(foodTab)};
const TAB_OMS_DEALER = ${JSON.stringify(omsTab)};
const TAB_DO_ISSUE = ${JSON.stringify(doTab)};
const TAB_VAT = ${JSON.stringify(vatTab)};
const CUSTOM_COLS = ${JSON.stringify(customCols)};

function getTargetSpreadsheet() {
  return SPREADSHEET_ID
    ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID)
    : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
}

function jsonOut(obj) {
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

function toAsciiDigits(val) {
  var bn = '০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯';
  return String(val || '').replace(/[০-৯]/g, function(ch) {
    var idx = bn.indexOf(ch);
    return idx >= 0 ? String(idx) : ch;
  });
}

function toSlashDate(val) {
  var s = toAsciiDigits(val).trim();
  var m = s.match(/^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/);
  if (m) return m[3] + '/' + m[2] + '/' + m[1];
  return s;
}

function getOrCreateSheet(ss, name, headers) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(name);
    if (headers && headers.length) {
      sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
      sh.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
  }
  return sh;
}

function upsertChallanRow(sh, entry) {
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  var values = lastRow > 1 ? sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 30).getDisplayValues() : [];
  var matchRow = -1;
  var eChallan = toAsciiDigits(entry.challanNo || '').trim();
  var eTaxNo = toAsciiDigits(entry.incomeTaxChallanNo || '').trim();
  var eVatNo = toAsciiDigits(entry.vatChallanNo || '').trim();

  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var r = values[i];
    var rChallanNo = toAsciiDigits(r[7] || '').trim();
    var rTaxNo = toAsciiDigits(r[12] || '').trim();
    var rVatNo = toAsciiDigits(r[18] || '').trim();
    var slotConflict =
      (eChallan && rChallanNo && rChallanNo !== eChallan) ||
      (!eChallan && eTaxNo && rTaxNo && rTaxNo !== eTaxNo) ||
      (!eChallan && !eTaxNo && eVatNo && rVatNo && rVatNo !== eVatNo);
    if (entry.srcRecordId && String(r[17] || '').trim() === entry.srcRecordId) { matchRow = i + 2; break; }
    if (entry.id && String(r[0] || '').trim() === entry.id) { matchRow = i + 2; break; }
    if (eChallan && rChallanNo === eChallan) { matchRow = i + 2; break; }
    if (!slotConflict && entry.dealerAllocationId && String(r[6] || '').trim() === entry.dealerAllocationId) { matchRow = i + 2; break; }
    if (!slotConflict && entry.licenseNo && String(r[21] || '').trim() === entry.licenseNo && String(r[3] || '').trim() === entry.category) {
      matchRow = i + 2; break;
    }
  }

  var cur = matchRow > 0 ? sh.getRange(matchRow, 1, 1, 30).getDisplayValues()[0] : [];
  var row = new Array(23);
  for (var k = 0; k < 23; k++) row[k] = cur[k] || '';

  row[0] = cur[0] || entry.id || ('CHL-' + Date.now());
  row[1] = cur[1] || toSlashDate(entry.date || '');
  row[2] = entry.department || cur[2] || '';
  row[3] = entry.category || cur[3] || '';
  row[4] = entry.sourceMemo || cur[4] || '';
  row[5] = entry.dcMemo || cur[5] || '';
  row[6] = entry.dealerAllocationId || cur[6] || '';
  row[7] = eChallan || cur[7] || '';
  row[8] = toSlashDate(entry.challanDate || '') || cur[8] || '';
  row[9] = entry.rate || cur[9] || '';
  row[10] = entry.quantity || cur[10] || '';
  row[11] = entry.totalAmount || cur[11] || '';
  row[12] = eTaxNo || cur[12] || '';
  row[13] = toSlashDate(entry.incomeTaxDate || '') || cur[13] || '';
  row[14] = entry.incomeTaxAmount || cur[14] || '';
  row[15] = entry.comment || cur[15] || '';
  row[16] = cur[16] || entry.createdAt || new Date().toISOString();
  row[17] = cur[17] || entry.srcRecordId || '';
  row[18] = eVatNo || cur[18] || '';
  row[19] = toSlashDate(entry.vatDate || '') || cur[19] || '';
  row[20] = entry.vatAmount || cur[20] || '';
  row[21] = entry.licenseNo || cur[21] || '';
  row[22] = entry.new37Code || cur[22] || '';

  for (var c = 0; c < CUSTOM_COLS.length; c++) {
    var colDef = CUSTOM_COLS[c];
    if (colDef.colIndex >= 0) {
      while (row.length <= colDef.colIndex) row.push('');
      var customVal = entry.customFields && entry.customFields[colDef.id];
      if (customVal) row[colDef.colIndex] = customVal;
      else if (cur[colDef.colIndex]) row[colDef.colIndex] = cur[colDef.colIndex];
    }
  }

  if (matchRow > 0) {
    sh.getRange(matchRow, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    return { mode: 'updated', rowNumber: matchRow };
  }
  sh.appendRow(row);
  return { mode: 'appended', rowNumber: sh.getLastRow() };
}

function doGet() {
  try {
    var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet();
    return jsonOut({
      success: true,
      status: 'ready',
      spreadsheetId: ss.getId(),
      availableTabs: ss.getSheets().map(function(s) { return s.getName(); })
    });
  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut({ success: false, message: String(err) });
  }
}

function doPost(e) {
  try {
    var body = JSON.parse((e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || '{}');
    var action = body.action || 'syncAll';
    var payload = body.payload || {};
    var ss = getTargetSpreadsheet();

    if (action === 'saveChallan' || action === 'saveChallanBatch') {
      var headers = [
        'id','date','department','category','sourceMemo','dcMemo',
        'dealerAllocationId','challanNo','challanDate','rate','quantity',
        'totalAmount','incomeTaxChallanNo','incomeTaxDate','incomeTaxAmount',
        'comment','createdAt','srcRecordId','vatChallanNo','vatDate',
        'vatAmount','licenseNo','new37Code'
      ];
      var sh = getOrCreateSheet(ss, TAB_CHALLAN, headers);
      var entries = action === 'saveChallanBatch' ? (payload.entries || []) : [payload];
      var updated = 0, appended = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
        var r = upsertChallanRow(sh, entries[i]);
        if (r.mode === 'updated') updated++; else appended++;
        if (TAB_VAT && entries[i].vatChallanNo) {
          var vSh = getOrCreateSheet(ss, TAB_VAT, ['id','vatDate','category','dealerName','licenseNo','vatChallanNo','vatAmount','new37Code','createdAt']);
          vSh.appendRow([
            entries[i].id || '', toSlashDate(entries[i].vatDate || entries[i].date || ''),
            entries[i].category || '', entries[i].dealerName || '',
            entries[i].licenseNo || '', entries[i].vatChallanNo || '',
            entries[i].vatAmount || '', entries[i].new37Code || '',
            entries[i].createdAt || new Date().toISOString()
          ]);
        }
      }
      return jsonOut({ success: true, updated: updated, appended: appended, message: 'Google Sheet আপডেট সম্পন্ন' });
    }

    var readTab = function(name) {
      var tab = ss.getSheetByName(name);
      return tab ? tab.getDataRange().getDisplayValues() : [];
    };

    return jsonOut({
      success: true,
      availableTabs: ss.getSheets().map(function(s) { return s.getName(); }),
      tabs: {
        challan: readTab(TAB_CHALLAN),
        suballocations: readTab(TAB_SUBALLOC),
        foodDealers: readTab(TAB_FOOD_DEALER),
        omsDealers: readTab(TAB_OMS_DEALER),
        doIssues: readTab(TAB_DO_ISSUE)
      }
    });
  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut({ success: false, message: String(err) });
  }
}`;
}

// ==================== FILE: src/utils/api.ts ====================
const TOKEN_KEY = 'challan_auth_token';

export function getAuthToken(): string {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY) || '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

export function setAuthToken(token: string): void {
  try {
    if (token) {
      localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage restriction errors
  }
}

export function clearAuthToken(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Ignore
  }
}

export async function apiFetch(input: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers || {});
  if (token && !headers.has('Authorization')) {
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
  }
  const res = await fetch(input, {
    ...init,
    headers,
    credentials: 'include',
  });
  if (res.status === 401 && !input.includes('/api/auth/')) {
    clearAuthToken();
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('challan-auth-expired'));
    }
  }
  return res;
}

// ==================== FILE: src/utils/bengali.ts ====================
import type { PermissionKey } from '../types.ts';

const BN_DIGITS = ['০', '১', '২', '৩', '৪', '৫', '৬', '৭', '৮', '৯'];

export function toBnDigits(input: string | number): string {
  return String(input).replace(/\d/g, (d) => BN_DIGITS[Number(d)] ?? d);
}

export function formatBnCurrency(amount: number): string {
  const safe = Number.isFinite(amount) ? amount : 0;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(safe);
  return toBnDigits(formatted);
}

export function formatBnDate(isoDate: string): string {
  if (!isoDate) return '—';
  const parts = isoDate.split('-');
  if (parts.length !== 3) return toBnDigits(isoDate);
  const [y, m, d] = parts;
  return toBnDigits(`${d}/${m}/${y}`);
}

export const PERMISSION_LABELS: Record<PermissionKey, { label: string; desc: string }> = {
  scan: {
    label: 'চালান স্ক্যান ও এন্ট্রি',

  },

export const OFFICIAL_VERIFICATION_BASE =
  'https://challanverification.finance.gov.bd/echalan/index.php';

export function toEnglishDigits(
  value: string | number | null | undefined
): string {
  return String(value ?? '').replace(/[০-৯]/g, (d) =>
    String('০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯'.indexOf(d))
  );
}

export function toBengaliDigits(
  value: string | number | null | undefined
): string {
  const bn = ['০', '১', '২', '৩', '৪', '৫', '৬', '৭', '৮', '৯'];
  return String(value ?? '').replace(/[0-9]/g, (d) => bn[Number(d)] ?? d);
}

export function extractSpreadsheetId(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  const raw = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return '';
  const urlMatch = raw.match(/\/spreadsheets\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  if (urlMatch && urlMatch[1]) {
    return urlMatch[1].trim();
  }
  return raw;
}

export function getOfficialVerificationUrl(challanNo: string): string {
  const cleaned = toEnglishDigits(challanNo).trim();
  const m = cleaned.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{10,11})$/);
  const queryNo = m ? `${m[1]}-${m[2].slice(0, 10)}` : cleaned;
  return `${OFFICIAL_VERIFICATION_BASE}?challanNo=${encodeURIComponent(queryNo)}`;
}

/**
 * চালান নম্বরের শেষে কমন ১ (১১ অঙ্ক):
 * "YYYY-" এর পরে ঠিক ১০ অঙ্ক এলে শেষে ১ বসিয়ে ১১ অঙ্ক করে।
 * ১১ অঙ্ক থাকলে হুবহু ১১ অঙ্কই রাখে। কখনো শেষের ১ বাদ দেবে না।
 */
export function normalizeChallanNo(no: string): string {
  const s = toEnglishDigits(no).trim();
  const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})\s*-\s*(\d+)$/);
  if (!m) return s;
  const body = m[2].length === 10 ? m[2] + '1' : m[2];
  return `${m[1]}-${body}`;
}

export function extractChallanInfo(rawValue: string): {
  number: string;
  normalized11: string;
  url: string;
} {
  const text = toEnglishDigits(rawValue).trim();
  if (!text) {
    throw new Error('QR থেকে কোনো তথ্য পাওয়া যায়নি।');
  }

  const cnoMatch = text.match(/\bCNO\s*:\s*([A-Za-z0-9-]+)/i);
  if (cnoMatch) {
    const number = cnoMatch[1].trim();
    const normalized11 = normalizeChallanNo(number);
    return {
      number: normalized11,
      normalized11,
      url: getOfficialVerificationUrl(normalized11),
    };
  }

  const urlMatch = text.match(
    /https?:\/\/challanverification\.finance\.gov\.bd\/echalan\/index\.php\?[^\s"'<>]+/i
  );
  if (urlMatch) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(urlMatch[0]);
      const number = url.searchParams.get('challanNo');
      if (number) {
        const normalized11 = normalizeChallanNo(number.trim());
        return {
          number: normalized11,
          normalized11,
          url: getOfficialVerificationUrl(normalized11),
        };
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore malformed URL
    }
  }

  const anyUrlMatch = text.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+challanNo=([0-9-]+)/i);
  if (anyUrlMatch) {
    const normalized11 = normalizeChallanNo(anyUrlMatch[1].trim());
    return {
      number: normalized11,
      normalized11,
      url: getOfficialVerificationUrl(normalized11),
    };
  }

  const numberMatch = text.match(/\b\d{4}\s*-\s*\d{10,11}\b/);
  if (numberMatch) {
    const normalized11 = normalizeChallanNo(numberMatch[0]);
    return {
      number: normalized11,
      normalized11,
      url: getOfficialVerificationUrl(normalized11),
    };
  }

  throw new Error('QR-এর লেখায় চালান নম্বর শনাক্ত করা যায়নি।');
}

/** যেকোনো ফরম্যাটের তারিখ -> dd/MM/yyyy */
export function normalizeChallanDate(v: string | null | undefined): string {
  const t = toEnglishDigits(v).trim();
  if (!t) return '';
  let m = t.match(/^(\d{4})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})/);
  if (m) {
    return `${m[3].padStart(2, '0')}/${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}/${m[1]}`;
  }
  m = t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{4}|\d{2})$/);
  if (m) {
    const year = m[3].length === 2 ? `20${m[3]}` : m[3];
    return `${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}/${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}/${year}`;
  }
  return t;
}

/** dd/MM/yyyy -> M/d/yyyy (for Google Sheet date column format like 11/4/2026) */
export function toSheetSlashDate(ddmmyyyy: string): string {
  const m = toEnglishDigits(ddmmyyyy)
    .trim()
    .match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);
  if (!m) return ddmmyyyy;
  return `${Number(m[2])}/${Number(m[1])}/${m[3]}`;
}

/** yyyy-MM-dd -> dd/MM/yyyy */
export function showDate(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  const m = String(iso || '').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  return m ? `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}` : String(iso || '');
}

/** dd/MM/yyyy (or dd/MM/yy) -> yyyy-MM-dd (validates real calendar date) */
export function parseInputDate(v: string): string {
  const t = toEnglishDigits(v).trim();
  const m = t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{4}|\d{2})$/);
  if (!m) return '';
  const d = Number(m[1]);
  const mo = Number(m[2]);
  const y = m[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + Number(m[3]) : Number(m[3]);
  const chk = new Date(y, mo - 1, d);
  if (
    y < 1970 ||
    y > 2100 ||
    mo < 1 ||
    mo > 12 ||
    d < 1 ||
    chk.getFullYear() !== y ||
    chk.getMonth() !== mo - 1 ||
    chk.getDate() !== d
  ) {
    return '';
  }
  return `${y}-${String(mo).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** Parse deposit date on a ChallanRecord into sortable ISO yyyy-MM-dd */
export function getChallanDepositSortKey(r: ChallanRecord): string {
  const candidate =
    r.challanDate || r.incomeTaxDate || r.vatDate || r.date || '';
  const norm = normalizeChallanDate(candidate);
  const iso = parseInputDate(norm);
  if (iso) return iso;
  const m = toEnglishDigits(candidate).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  return m ? `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}` : '0000-00-00';
}

export function maskDateInput(raw: string): string {
  const t = toEnglishDigits(raw).replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8);
  if (t.length > 4) return `${t.slice(0, 2)}/${t.slice(2, 4)}/${t.slice(4)}`;
  if (t.length > 2) return `${t.slice(0, 2)}/${t.slice(2)}`;
  return t;
}

export function formatMoney(
  value: string | number | null | undefined
): string {
  const cleaned = toEnglishDigits(value)
    .replace(/,/g, '')
    .replace(/[^\d.-]/g, '');
  const n = Number(cleaned);
  return Number.isFinite(n)
    ? n.toLocaleString('en-US', {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
      })
    : '0.00';
}

export function toNumericValue(
  value: string | number | null | undefined
): number {
  if (typeof value === 'number') return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
  const cleaned = toEnglishDigits(value)
    .replace(/,/g, '')
    .replace(/[^\d.-]/g, '');
  const n = Number(cleaned);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

/**
 * বাংলা নামের বানান অনুযায়ী সাদৃশ্য স্কোর (0 থেকে 1)
 */
export function scoreBengaliNameSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {
  const clean = (s: string) =>
    s
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/মোঃ|মো:|মোহাম্মদ|md\.?|মেসার্স|m\/s|প্রোঃ|পিতা|ঠিকানা/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/[.,\-_:;()'"/\s]+/g, ' ')
      .trim()
      .replace(/ী/g, 'ি')
      .replace(/ূ/g, 'ু')
      .replace(/শ|ষ/g, 'স')
      .replace(/ণ/g, 'ন')
      .replace(/য়/g, 'য');

  const na = clean(a);
  const nb = clean(b);
  if (!na || !nb) return 0;
  if (na === nb) return 1;
  if (na.includes(nb) || nb.includes(na)) return 0.92;

  const tokensA = new Set(na.split(' ').filter((t) => t.length > 1));
  const tokensB = new Set(nb.split(' ').filter((t) => t.length > 1));
  let tokenMatches = 0;
  for (const ta of tokensA) {
    for (const tb of tokensB) {
      if (ta === tb || ta.includes(tb) || tb.includes(ta)) {
        tokenMatches++;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  const tokenScore =
    tokensA.size + tokensB.size > 0
      ? (2 * tokenMatches) / (tokensA.size + tokensB.size)
      : 0;

  const bigrams = (str: string) => {
    const compact = str.replace(/\s+/g, '');
    const list: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < compact.length - 1; i++) {
      list.push(compact.slice(i, i + 2));
    }
    return list;
  };
  const bgA = bigrams(na);
  const bgB = bigrams(nb);
  if (bgA.length === 0 || bgB.length === 0) return tokenScore;

  const mapB = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const g of bgB) mapB.set(g, (mapB.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  let overlap = 0;
  for (const g of bgA) {
    const count = mapB.get(g) || 0;
    if (count > 0) {
      overlap++;
      mapB.set(g, count - 1);
    }
  }
  const dice = (2 * overlap) / (bgA.length + bgB.length);
  return Math.max(dice, tokenScore * 0.95);
}

export function extractEconomicCodes(data: OfficialChallanData): Array<{
  code: string;
  name: string;
  amount: string;
}> {
  const out: Array<{ code: string; name: string; amount: string }> = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  if (data.New37Code && data.New37Code.trim()) {
    const code = data.New37Code.trim();
    seen.add(code);
    out.push({
      code,
      name: data.ChallanTypeNameBn || 'মূল অর্থনৈতিক কোড',
      amount: toEnglishDigits(String(data.Amount || '')).replace(/,/g, ''),
    });
  }

  if (Array.isArray(data.Economics)) {
    for (const item of data.Economics) {
      const code = String(item.EconomicNew37Code || item.EconomicCode || '').trim();
      if (!code || seen.has(code)) continue;
      seen.add(code);
      out.push({
        code,
        name: String(item.EconomicNameBn || item.EconomicRemarks || 'অর্থনৈতিক কোড').trim(),
        amount: toEnglishDigits(String(item.EconomicAmount || '')).replace(/,/g, ''),
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function parseOfficialRemarks(data: OfficialChallanData): {
  department: string;
  toOrganization: string;
  submittedThrough: string;
  submittedOnBehalfOf: string;
  challanNo: string;
  new37Code: string;
  economicOptions: Array<{ code: string; name: string; amount: string }>;
  description: string;
  quantity: string;
  rate: string;
  amount: string;
  challanDate: string;
} {
  const remarks = String(data.Remarks || '').trim();
  const org = data.ToOrgNameBn || data.OrgNameBn || '';
  const economicOptions = extractEconomicCodes(data);
  const econCode = economicOptions[0]?.code || '';

  const details = [
    data.ChallanTypeNameBn,
    econCode ? `কোড: ${econCode}` : '',
    remarks ? remarks : '',
  ].filter(Boolean);

  const normalizedRemarks = toEnglishDigits(remarks);

  const ratePerMtMatch = normalizedRemarks.match(
    /(?:PER\s*MT\s*TK\.?\s*|প্রতি\s*(?:মে\.?\s*টন|মেট্রিক\s*টন)\s*)([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)/i
  );
  const ratePerKgMatch = normalizedRemarks.match(
    /প্রতি\s*কেজি\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*টাকা/
  );
  const quantityMatch = normalizedRemarks.match(
    /([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*(?:MT\*?|মে\.?\s*টন|মেট্রিক\s*টন)/i
  );

  let rate = '';
  if (ratePerMtMatch) {
    rate = ratePerMtMatch[1];
  } else if (ratePerKgMatch) {
    rate = ratePerKgMatch[1];
  }

  return {
    department: org,
    toOrganization: org,
    submittedThrough: data.NameBn || data.ClientByNameBn || '',
    submittedOnBehalfOf: data.ClientForNameBn || data.NameBn || '',
    challanNo: normalizeChallanNo(data.ChallanNo || ''),
    new37Code: econCode,
    economicOptions,
    description: details.join(' | '),
    quantity: quantityMatch ? quantityMatch[1] : '',
    rate,
    amount: toEnglishDigits(String(data.Amount || '')).replace(/,/g, ''),
    challanDate: normalizeChallanDate(data.ChallanDate),
  };
}


// ==================== END: src/utils/challan.ts ====================


// ==================== FILE: src/utils/pdfExport.ts ====================
import {
  getA4PixelHeight,
  getA4PixelWidth,
  prepareA4PdfClone,
  type PaperOrientation,
} from '../components/A4FitPreview.tsx';

let cachedNikoshDataUrl: string | null = null;
let fontsPreloaded = false;

function arrayBufferToBase64(buffer: ArrayBuffer): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const chunkSize = 0x8000;
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
    const chunk = bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize);
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...chunk);
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

export async function ensureNikoshFontsReady(): Promise<string> {
  if (!cachedNikoshDataUrl) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/fonts/Nikosh.ttf', { cache: 'force-cache' });
      if (res.ok) {
        const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
        if (!contentType.includes('text/html')) {
          const buf = await res.arrayBuffer();
          cachedNikoshDataUrl = `data:font/ttf;base64,${arrayBufferToBase64(buf)}`;
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Optional local font unavailable; fall back to system/web fonts
    }
  }

  if (
    cachedNikoshDataUrl &&
    !fontsPreloaded &&
    typeof FontFace !== 'undefined' &&
    document.fonts
  ) {
    try {
      const f400 = new FontFace('Nikosh', `url("${cachedNikoshDataUrl}")`, {
        weight: '400',
        style: 'normal',
      });
      const f700 = new FontFace('Nikosh', `url("${cachedNikoshDataUrl}")`, {
        weight: '700',
        style: 'normal',
      });
      await Promise.all([f400.load(), f700.load()]);
      document.fonts.add(f400);
      document.fonts.add(f700);
      await Promise.all([
        document.fonts.load('400 13px "Nikosh"'),
        document.fonts.load('700 13px "Nikosh"'),
      ]);
      fontsPreloaded = true;
    } catch {
      // Fall back to stylesheet @font-face
    }
  }

  if (document.fonts?.ready) {
    await document.fonts.ready;
  }

  return cachedNikoshDataUrl || '';
}

function injectNikoshIntoClone(
  clonedDoc: Document,
  orientation: PaperOrientation,
  nikoshDataUrl: string
) {
  prepareA4PdfClone(clonedDoc, orientation);

  // Remove external cross-origin stylesheets (like Google Fonts Hind Siliguri)
  // so SVG foreignObjectRendering never taints or falls back from Nikosh.
  clonedDoc
    .querySelectorAll('link[rel="stylesheet"], link[rel="preconnect"]')
    .forEach((link) => {
      const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (href.startsWith('http://') || href.startsWith('https://')) {
        link.remove();
      }
    });

  const styleEl = clonedDoc.createElement('style');
  const fontFaceCss = nikoshDataUrl
    ? `
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Nikosh";
      src: url("${nikoshDataUrl}") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 400;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Nikosh";
      src: url("${nikoshDataUrl}") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 700;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "NikoshBAN";
      src: url("${nikoshDataUrl}") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 400;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    @font-face {
      font-family: "NikoshBAN";
      src: url("${nikoshDataUrl}") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 700;
      font-style: normal;
    }
    .font-nikosh, .font-nikosh *,
    .a4-sheet-page, .a4-sheet-page *,
    #challanReportPrintArea, #challanReportPrintArea *,
    #templateReportPrintArea, #templateReportPrintArea * {
      font-family: "Nikosh", "NikoshBAN", serif !important;
    }`
    : '';
  styleEl.textContent = `${fontFaceCss}
    .html2pdf__container {
      width: ${orientation === 'portrait' ? '210mm' : '297mm'} !important;
      margin: 0 !important;
      padding: 0 !important;
      background: #ffffff !important;
    }
    .a4-sheet-page {
      width: ${orientation === 'portrait' ? '210mm' : '297mm'} !important;
      min-height: ${orientation === 'portrait' ? '296mm' : '209mm'} !important;
      margin: 0 !important;
      padding: 7.5mm 8.5mm 7mm 8.5mm !important;
      background: #ffffff !important;
      border: none !important;
      box-shadow: none !important;
      display: flex !important;
      flex-direction: column !important;
      justify-content: space-between !important;
      box-sizing: border-box !important;
    }
    .a4-sheet-page table,
    #challanReportPrintArea table,
    .template-doc-body table {
      width: 100% !important;
      border-collapse: collapse !important;
      table-layout: fixed !important;
      border: 1px solid #000000 !important;
    }
    .a4-sheet-page th,
    .a4-sheet-page td,
    #challanReportPrintArea th,
    #challanReportPrintArea td,
    .template-doc-body th,
    .template-doc-body td {
      border: 1px solid #000000 !important;
      color: #000000 !important;
      vertical-align: middle !important;
      overflow: hidden !important;
      overflow-wrap: anywhere !important;
      word-break: break-word !important;
      box-sizing: border-box !important;
    }
    .a4-sheet-page th,
    #challanReportPrintArea th,
    .template-doc-body th {
      font-weight: bold !important;
      background-color: #f1f5f9 !important;
    }
    .a4-sheet-page-footer,
    .a4-page-footer {
      border-top: 1px solid #475569 !important;
    }
  `;
  clonedDoc.head.appendChild(styleEl);
}

interface MinimalJsPdf {
  addPage: (format?: string, orientation?: string) => void;
  addImage: (
    imageData: string,
    format: string,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    w: number,
    h: number
  ) => void;
  save: (filename?: string) => void;
}

export async function downloadNikoshPdf(
  element: HTMLElement,
  filename: string,
  orientation: PaperOrientation
): Promise<void> {
  const nikoshDataUrl = await ensureNikoshFontsReady();
  const html2pdfModule = await import('html2pdf.js');
  const html2pdf = html2pdfModule.default;

  const foundPages = Array.from(
    element.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.a4-sheet-page')
  );
  const pageNodes =
    foundPages.length > 0
      ? foundPages
      : [element];

  const a4W = orientation === 'portrait' ? 210 : 297;
  const a4H = orientation === 'portrait' ? 297 : 210;
  const a4Ratio = a4H / a4W;

  const renderPageToCanvas = async (
    target: HTMLElement,
    useForeignObject: boolean
  ): Promise<HTMLCanvasElement> => {
    const worker = html2pdf().set({
      margin: [0, 0, 0, 0],
      filename,
      image: { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.96 },
      html2canvas: {
        scale: 2,
        useCORS: true,
        allowTaint: false,
        foreignObjectRendering: useForeignObject,
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        scrollX: 0,
        scrollY: 0,
        windowWidth: getA4PixelWidth(orientation),
        onclone: (clonedDoc: Document) =>
          injectNikoshIntoClone(clonedDoc, orientation, nikoshDataUrl),
      },
      jsPDF: {
        unit: 'mm',
        format: 'a4',
        orientation,
      },
      pagebreak: { mode: [] },
    } as Record<string, unknown>);

    const canvas = (await worker
      .from(target)
      .toCanvas()
      .get('canvas')) as HTMLCanvasElement;

    if (!canvas || canvas.width < 10 || canvas.height < 10) {
      throw new Error('Empty canvas rendered');
    }
    return canvas;
  };

  // Initialize 1-page jsPDF document reliably via a clean proportional white seed canvas
  const seedCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  seedCanvas.width = 100;
  seedCanvas.height = Math.floor(100 * a4Ratio);
  const seedCtx = seedCanvas.getContext('2d');
  if (seedCtx) {
    seedCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    seedCtx.fillRect(0, 0, seedCanvas.width, seedCanvas.height);
  }

  const pdf = (await html2pdf()
    .set({
      margin: [0, 0, 0, 0],
      filename,
      image: { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.96 },
      jsPDF: { unit: 'mm', format: 'a4', orientation },
      pagebreak: { mode: [] },
    } as Record<string, unknown>)
    .from(seedCanvas)
    .toPdf()
    .get('pdf')) as MinimalJsPdf;

  let pdfPageCount = 0;

  for (const pageEl of pageNodes) {
    let pageCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
    try {
      pageCanvas = await renderPageToCanvas(pageEl, true);
    } catch {
      pageCanvas = await renderPageToCanvas(pageEl, false);
    }

    const canvasRatio = pageCanvas.height / pageCanvas.width;

    if (canvasRatio <= a4Ratio * 1.18) {
      // Standard pre-paginated A4 sheet: fit onto strictly 1 PDF page (zero blank gaps or splits)
      const scale = Math.min(a4W / pageCanvas.width, a4H / pageCanvas.height);
      const drawW = pageCanvas.width * scale;
      const drawH = pageCanvas.height * scale;
      const offsetX = Math.max(0, (a4W - drawW) / 2);

      if (pdfPageCount > 0) {
        pdf.addPage('a4', orientation);
      }
      const imgData = pageCanvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.96);
      pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', offsetX, 0, drawW, drawH);
      pdfPageCount += 1;
    } else {
      // Oversized single freeform template: slice cleanly across A4 pages skipping empty trailing tails
      const slicePxHeight = Math.floor(pageCanvas.width * a4Ratio);
      const sliceCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      const sliceCtx = sliceCanvas.getContext('2d');
      sliceCanvas.width = pageCanvas.width;

      for (let y = 0; y < pageCanvas.height; y += slicePxHeight) {
        const remaining = pageCanvas.height - y;
        if (remaining < 16 && pdfPageCount > 0) break;
        const h = Math.min(slicePxHeight, remaining);
        sliceCanvas.height = h;
        if (sliceCtx) {
          sliceCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          sliceCtx.fillRect(0, 0, sliceCanvas.width, h);
          sliceCtx.drawImage(
            pageCanvas,
            0,
            y,
            pageCanvas.width,
            h,
            0,
            0,
            pageCanvas.width,
            h
          );
        }
        const drawH = (h * a4W) / pageCanvas.width;
        if (pdfPageCount > 0) {
          pdf.addPage('a4', orientation);
        }
        pdf.addImage(
          sliceCanvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.96),
          'JPEG',
          0,
          0,
          a4W,
          drawH
        );
        pdfPageCount += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  pdf.save(filename);
}

// ==================== FILE: src/utils/sheetParser.ts ====================
import type {
  AppSettings,
  ChallanRecord,
  DealerRecord,
  DoIssueRecord,
  SuballocationRecord,
} from '../types.ts';
import {
  normalizeChallanDate,
  normalizeChallanNo,
  toEnglishDigits,
  toNumericValue,
} from './challan.ts';

export function colLetterToIndex(col: string): number {
  const clean = col
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/[^A-Z]/g, '')
    .trim();
  if (!clean) return -1;
  let idx = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i++) {
    idx = idx * 26 + (clean.charCodeAt(i) - 64);
  }
  return idx - 1;
}

export function normalizeSheetCategory(rawCategory: string | null | undefined): string {
  const s = String(rawCategory || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';
  const low = s.toLowerCase();
  if (low === 'ep চাল' || low === 'ep rice') return 'ইপি চাল';
  if (low === 'ep গম' || low === 'ep wheat') return 'ইপি গম';
  return s;
}

/** Parse RFC-4180 CSV text (handles quoted commas, escaped quotes, newlines inside quotes) */
export function parseCsvMatrix(csvText: string): string[][] {
  const rows: string[][] = [];
  let row: string[] = [];
  let field = '';
  let inQuotes = false;

  const text = csvText.replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const ch = text[i];
    if (inQuotes) {
      if (ch === '"') {
        if (text[i + 1] === '"') {
          field += '"';
          i++;
        } else {
          inQuotes = false;
        }
      } else {
        field += ch;
      }
    } else if (ch === '"') {
      inQuotes = true;
    } else if (ch === ',') {
      row.push(field);
      field = '';
    } else if (ch === '\r') {
      if (text[i + 1] === '\n') i++;
      row.push(field);
      rows.push(row);
      row = [];
      field = '';
    } else if (ch === '\n') {
      row.push(field);
      rows.push(row);
      row = [];
      field = '';
    } else {
      field += ch;
    }
  }
  if (field.length > 0 || row.length > 0) {
    row.push(field);
    rows.push(row);
  }
  return rows;
}

/**
 * Google Sheets returns US locale formatted dates like "11/4/2026" (M/d/yyyy) or "9/14/2026".
 * Convert cleanly into standard dd/MM/yyyy display format.
 */
export function parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(
  raw: string | null | undefined
): string {
  const t = toEnglishDigits(raw).trim();
  if (!t) return '';

  const isoMatch = t.match(/^(\d{4})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})/);
  if (isoMatch) {
    return `${isoMatch[3].padStart(2, '0')}/${isoMatch[2].padStart(
      2,
      '0'
    )}/${isoMatch[1]}`;
  }

  const slashMatch = t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{4})/);
  if (slashMatch) {
    const p1 = Number(slashMatch[1]);
    const p2 = Number(slashMatch[2]);
    const year = slashMatch[3];

    if (p1 > 12 && p2 >= 1 && p2 <= 12) {
      return `${String(p1).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(p2).padStart(
        2,
        '0'
      )}/${year}`;
    }
    if (p1 >= 1 && p1 <= 12 && p2 >= 1 && p2 <= 31) {
      return `${String(p2).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(p1).padStart(
        2,
        '0'
      )}/${year}`;
    }
  }

  return normalizeChallanDate(t);
}

export function parseDealersFromRows(
  rows: string[][],
  categoryLabel: string
): DealerRecord[] {
  if (rows.length < 2) return [];
  const headers = rows[0].map((h) => String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase());
  const idIdx = headers.indexOf('id') !== -1 ? headers.indexOf('id') : 0;
  const nameIdx =
    headers.indexOf('dealername') !== -1 ? headers.indexOf('dealername') : 1;
  const fatherIdx = headers.indexOf('fathername');
  const unionIdx =
    headers.indexOf('unionname') !== -1 ? headers.indexOf('unionname') : 5;
  const municipalityIdx = headers.indexOf('municipality');
  const centerIdx = headers.indexOf('salescenter');
  const categoryIdx = headers.indexOf('category');

  const out: DealerRecord[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const dealerName = String(r[nameIdx] || '').trim();
    if (!dealerName) continue;
    const unionVal = String(r[unionIdx] || '').trim();
    const muniVal =
      municipalityIdx >= 0 ? String(r[municipalityIdx] || '').trim() : '';
    const catVal =
      categoryIdx >= 0 ? String(r[categoryIdx] || '').trim() : '';

    out.push({
      id: String(r[idIdx] || `DLR-${i}`).trim(),
      dealerName,
      fatherName: fatherIdx >= 0 ? String(r[fatherIdx] || '').trim() : '',
      unionName: unionVal || muniVal,
      salesCenter: centerIdx >= 0 ? String(r[centerIdx] || '').trim() : '',
      category: catVal || categoryLabel,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export function parseSuballocationsFromRows(
  rows: string[][]
): SuballocationRecord[] {
  if (rows.length < 2) return [];
  const headers = rows[0].map((h) => String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase());
  const col = (name: string, fallback: number) => {
    const idx = headers.indexOf(name.toLowerCase());
    return idx !== -1 ? idx : fallback;
  };

  const dealerNameIdx = headers.findIndex((h) =>
    ['dealername', 'dealer', 'ডিলারের নাম'].includes(h)
  );
  const dealerIdIdx = headers.findIndex((h) =>
    ['dealerid', 'dealer_id'].includes(h)
  );
  const unionIdx = headers.findIndex((h) =>
    ['unionname', 'union'].includes(h)
  );
  const catIdx = headers.findIndex((h) => ['category', 'khat'].includes(h));
  const dateIdx = headers.findIndex((h) => ['date', 'তারিখ'].includes(h));

  const cardIdx = col('cardcount', 7); // H
  const unusedIdx = col('unusedcards', 8); // I
  const qtyIdx = col('allocationqty', 9); // J
  const totalIdx = col('totalamount', 10); // K (প্রাপ্য মূল টাকা)
  const taxIdx = col('taxamount', 11); // L (প্রাপ্য আয়কর)
  const createdIdx = col('createdat', 12); // M
  const idIdx = col('id', 13); // N

  const out: SuballocationRecord[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const id = String(r[idIdx] || '').trim();
    const totalAmount = toNumericValue(r[totalIdx]);
    const taxAmount = toNumericValue(r[taxIdx]);
    const dealerName =
      dealerNameIdx >= 0
        ? String(r[dealerNameIdx] || '').trim()
        : String(r[4] || r[1] || '').trim();

    if (!id && !dealerName && totalAmount === 0 && taxAmount === 0) continue;

    const createdAtVal = String(r[createdIdx] || '').trim();
    const dateVal = dateIdx >= 0 ? String(r[dateIdx] || '').trim() : '';

    out.push({
      id: id || `ds-row-${i + 1}`,
      dealerId: dealerIdIdx >= 0 ? String(r[dealerIdIdx] || '').trim() : '',
      dealerName,
      unionName:
        unionIdx >= 0
          ? String(r[unionIdx] || '').trim()
          : String(r[5] || '').trim(),
      category:
        catIdx >= 0 ? String(r[catIdx] || '').trim() : String(r[0] || '').trim(),
      cardCount: toNumericValue(r[cardIdx]),
      unusedCards: toNumericValue(r[unusedIdx]),
      allocationQty: toNumericValue(r[qtyIdx]),
      totalAmount,
      taxAmount,
      createdAt: createdAtVal || dateVal,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export function parseDoIssuesFromRows(rows: string[][]): DoIssueRecord[] {
  if (rows.length < 2) return [];
  const out: DoIssueRecord[] = [];

  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const col0 = String(r[0] || '').trim();
    const col1 = String(r[1] || '').trim();
    const col2 = String(r[2] || '').trim();

    const isShiftedLayout =
      !col0 &&
      Boolean(col1) &&
      /^\d{1,2}[\/\-.]\d{1,2}[\/\-.]\d{2,4}|\d{4}[\/\-.]\d{1,2}[\/\-.]\d{1,2}/.test(
        toEnglishDigits(col2)
      );

    let doNo = '';
    let issueDate = '';
    let category = '';
    let unionName = '';
    let recipientName = '';
    let dealerAllocationId = '';
    let challanNo = '';
    let taxChallanNo = '';
    let requiredAmount = 0;
    let requiredTaxAmount = 0;
    let createdAt = '';
    let id = '';

    if (isShiftedLayout) {
      doNo = toEnglishDigits(col1);
      issueDate = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(col2);
      category = String(r[3] || '').trim();
      unionName = String(r[7] || '').trim();
      recipientName = String(r[8] || '').trim();
      challanNo = normalizeChallanNo(String(r[11] || ''));
      taxChallanNo = normalizeChallanNo(String(r[12] || ''));
      createdAt = String(r[14] || '').trim();
      id = `do-shift-${i + 1}-${doNo}`;
    } else {
      doNo = toEnglishDigits(col0);
      issueDate = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(col1);
      category = String(r[2] || '').trim();
      unionName = String(r[3] || '').trim();
      recipientName = String(r[4] || '').trim();
      dealerAllocationId = String(r[11] || '').trim();
      challanNo = normalizeChallanNo(String(r[12] || ''));
      taxChallanNo = normalizeChallanNo(String(r[15] || ''));
      requiredAmount = toNumericValue(r[18]);
      requiredTaxAmount = toNumericValue(r[19]);
      createdAt = String(r[20] || '').trim();
      id = String(r[21] || `do-row-${i + 1}`).trim();
    }

    if (!challanNo) {
      for (const cell of r) {
        const s = toEnglishDigits(String(cell || '')).trim();
        if (/^\d{4}-\d{10,11}$/.test(s)) {
          challanNo = normalizeChallanNo(s);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!doNo && !challanNo && !dealerAllocationId) continue;

    out.push({
      id,
      doNo,
      issueDate,
      category,
      unionName,
      recipientName,
      dealerAllocationId,
      challanNo,
      taxChallanNo,
      requiredAmount,
      requiredTaxAmount,
      createdAt,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export function matchDoIssueForChallan(
  record: Pick<
    ChallanRecord,
    'challanNo' | 'incomeTaxChallanNo' | 'dealerAllocationId' | 'challanCategory'
  >,
  doIssues: DoIssueRecord[]
): DoIssueRecord | undefined {
  if (!doIssues || doIssues.length === 0) return undefined;
  const mainNo = record.challanNo ? normalizeChallanNo(record.challanNo) : '';
  const taxNo = record.incomeTaxChallanNo
    ? normalizeChallanNo(record.incomeTaxChallanNo)
    : '';

  if (mainNo) {
    const byMain = doIssues.find(
      (d) =>
        (d.challanNo && normalizeChallanNo(d.challanNo) === mainNo) ||
        (d.taxChallanNo && normalizeChallanNo(d.taxChallanNo) === mainNo)
    );
    if (byMain) return byMain;
  }

  if (taxNo) {
    const byTax = doIssues.find(
      (d) =>
        (d.challanNo && normalizeChallanNo(d.challanNo) === taxNo) ||
        (d.taxChallanNo && normalizeChallanNo(d.taxChallanNo) === taxNo)
    );
    if (byTax) return byTax;
  }

  if (record.dealerAllocationId) {
    const byAlloc = doIssues.find(
      (d) =>
        d.dealerAllocationId === record.dealerAllocationId &&
        (!d.category ||
          !record.challanCategory ||
          d.category.trim() === record.challanCategory.trim())
    );
    if (byAlloc) return byAlloc;
  }

  return undefined;
}

export function parseChallansFromRows(
  rows: string[][],
  dealers: DealerRecord[],
  settings: AppSettings,
  suballocations: SuballocationRecord[] = [],
  doIssues: DoIssueRecord[] = []
): ChallanRecord[] {
  if (rows.length < 2) return [];
  const dealerById = new Map(dealers.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
  const dealerByName = new Map(
    dealers.map((d) => [d.dealerName.trim(), d])
  );
  const subById = new Map(suballocations.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
  const out: ChallanRecord[] = [];

  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const id = String(r[0] || '').trim();
    const date = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(String(r[1] || ''));
    const department = String(r[2] || '').trim();
    const category = normalizeSheetCategory(String(r[3] || ''));
    const sourceMemo = String(r[4] || '').trim();
    const dcMemo = String(r[5] || '').trim();
    const dealerAllocationId = String(r[6] || '').trim();
    const rawMainNo = String(r[7] || '').trim();
    const challanNo = rawMainNo ? normalizeChallanNo(rawMainNo) : '';
    const challanDate = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(
      String(r[8] || r[1] || '')
    );
    const rate = toEnglishDigits(String(r[9] || '')).replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    const quantity = toEnglishDigits(String(r[10] || '')).trim();
    const totalAmount = toEnglishDigits(String(r[11] || '')).replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    const rawTaxNo = String(r[12] || '').trim();
    const incomeTaxChallanNo = rawTaxNo ? normalizeChallanNo(rawTaxNo) : '';
    const incomeTaxDate = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(String(r[13] || ''));
    const incomeTaxAmount = toEnglishDigits(String(r[14] || '')).replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    const comment = String(r[15] || '').trim();
    const createdAt = String(r[16] || '').trim();
    const srcRecordId = String(r[17] || '').trim();

    const rawVatNo = String(r[18] || '').trim();
    const vatChallanNo = rawVatNo ? normalizeChallanNo(rawVatNo) : '';
    const vatDate = parseGoogleSheetDateToDisplay(String(r[19] || ''));
    const vatAmount = toEnglishDigits(String(r[20] || '')).replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    const licenseNo = String(r[21] || '').trim();
    const new37Code = String(r[22] || '').trim();

    const customFields: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const colDef of settings.customColumns || []) {
      const idx = colLetterToIndex(colDef.sheetColumn);
      if (idx >= 0 && r[idx] !== undefined) {
        customFields[colDef.id] = String(r[idx] || '').trim();
      }
    }

    if (!id && !challanNo && !incomeTaxChallanNo && !vatChallanNo) continue;

    let matchedSub = dealerAllocationId
      ? subById.get(dealerAllocationId)
      : undefined;
    const matchedDealer = dealerAllocationId
      ? dealerById.get(dealerAllocationId)
      : undefined;

    let dealerName =
      matchedSub?.dealerName || matchedDealer?.dealerName || '';
    let unionName = matchedSub?.unionName || matchedDealer?.unionName || '';

    if (!matchedSub && totalAmount) {
      const numAmt = toNumericValue(totalAmount);
      if (numAmt > 0) {
        const amtMatches = suballocations.filter(
          (s) =>
            Math.abs(s.totalAmount - numAmt) < 1 &&
            (!category || !s.category || s.category === category)
        );
        if (amtMatches.length === 1) {
          matchedSub = amtMatches[0];
          if (!dealerName) dealerName = matchedSub.dealerName;
          if (!unionName) unionName = matchedSub.unionName || '';
        }
      }
    }

    if (dealerName && !unionName) {
      unionName = dealerByName.get(dealerName.trim())?.unionName || '';
    }

    const matchedDo = matchDoIssueForChallan(
      {
        challanNo: challanNo || incomeTaxChallanNo || vatChallanNo,
        incomeTaxChallanNo,
        dealerAllocationId,
        challanCategory: category || 'খাদ্য বান্ধব',
      },
      doIssues
    );

    if (!dealerName && matchedDo?.recipientName) {
      dealerName = matchedDo.recipientName.trim();
    }
    if (!unionName && matchedDo?.unionName) {
      unionName = matchedDo.unionName.trim();
    }
    if (dealerName && !unionName) {
      unionName = dealerByName.get(dealerName.trim())?.unionName || '';
    }

    const expectedAmount =
      matchedSub && matchedSub.totalAmount > 0
        ? String(matchedSub.totalAmount)
        : matchedDo && matchedDo.requiredAmount && matchedDo.requiredAmount > 0
          ? String(matchedDo.requiredAmount)
          : '';

    const expectedTaxAmount =
      matchedSub && matchedSub.taxAmount > 0
        ? String(matchedSub.taxAmount)
        : matchedDo && matchedDo.requiredTaxAmount && matchedDo.requiredTaxAmount > 0
          ? String(matchedDo.requiredTaxAmount)
          : '';

    out.push({
      serialNo: out.length + 1,
      id,
      date,
      department,
      unionName,
      dealerName,
      dealerAllocationId,
      challanCategory: category || 'খাদ্য বান্ধব',
      sourceMemo,
      dcMemo,
      toOrganization: department,
      submittedThrough: '',
      submittedOnBehalfOf: '',
      challanNo: challanNo || incomeTaxChallanNo || vatChallanNo,
      description: comment,
      quantity,
      rate,
      expectedAmount,
      expectedTaxAmount,
      amount: totalAmount,
      challanDate,
      incomeTaxChallanNo,
      incomeTaxDate,
      incomeTaxAmount,
      vatChallanNo,
      vatDate,
      vatAmount,
      licenseNo,
      new37Code,
      doNo: matchedDo?.doNo || '',
      doIssueDate: matchedDo?.issueDate || '',
      customFields,
      comment,
      recordId: srcRecordId || id || `sheet-row-${i + 1}`,
      pdfUrl: '',
      entryTime: createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }
  return out;
}


// ==================== END: src/utils/sheetParser.ts ====================
